      } else if (style === 'hatch') {
        paint = ((px + py) % 6) < 2;
      } else if (style === 'cross') {
        paint = ((px + py) % 6) < 2 || ((px - py + 12000) % 6) < 2;
      } else if (style === 'lines') {
        paint = (py % 5) < 2;
      }
      if (paint) {
        data[i] = fill[0]; data[i + 1] = fill[1]; data[i + 2] = fill[2]; data[i + 3] = 255;
      }
    }


    // scanline flood fill
    var visited = new Uint8Array(bw * bh);
    var stack = [y0, x0];
    var maxPixels = bw * bh;
    var count = 0;
    while (stack.length && count < maxPixels) {
      var x = stack.pop();
      var y = stack.pop();
      if (y === undefined) break;
      while (x > 0 && !visited[y * bw + (x - 1)] && isMatch(((y * bw + (x - 1)) * 4))) x--;
      var spanUp = false, spanDown = false;
      while (x < bw) {
        var vi = y * bw + x;
        var i = vi * 4;
        if (visited[vi] || !isMatch(i)) break;
        visited[vi] = 1;
        setPix(i, x, y);
        count++;
        if (y > 0) {
          var up = (y - 1) * bw + x;
          if (!visited[up] && isMatch(up * 4)) {
            if (!spanUp) { stack.push(y - 1); stack.push(x); spanUp = true; }
          } else spanUp = false;
        }
        if (y < bh - 1) {
          var dn = (y + 1) * bw + x;
          if (!visited[dn] && isMatch(dn * 4)) {
            if (!spanDown) { stack.push(y + 1); stack.push(x); spanDown = true; }
          } else spanDown = false;
        }
        x++;
      }
    }
    // putImageData مستقل از transform است
    ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  }

  wb.floodFill = floodFillFull;


  // ——— Selection tool: کادر دور سوژه، جابه‌جایی، حذف ———
  function boardBgRgbSel() {
    if (wb.boardStyle === 'black') return [15, 23, 42];
    if (wb.boardStyle === 'green') return [20, 83, 45];
    return [255, 255, 255];
  }
  function clearSelUI() {
    var ov = document.getElementById('wbShapeOverlay');
    if (!ov) return;
    ov.querySelectorAll('.wb-sel-box, .wb-sel-float').forEach(function(n) { n.remove(); });
  }
  function cancelSelection(keepHole) {
    // اگر انتخاب فعال است و ثبت نشده، محتوا را برمی‌گردانیم مگر keepHole
    if (wb.selection && wb.selection.dataUrl && !keepHole && wb.selection.cut) {
      try {
        var img = new Image();
        var sx = wb.selection.originX, sy = wb.selection.originY;
        var sw = wb.selection.w, sh = wb.selection.h;
        img.onload = function() {
          ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, sw, sh);
        };
        img.src = wb.selection.dataUrl;
      } catch (e) {}
    }
    wb.selection = null;
    wb.selecting = false;
    wb.selStart = null;
    clearSelUI();
  }
  function stampSelection() {
    if (!wb.selection || !wb.selection.dataUrl) return;
    snapshot();
    var img = new Image();
    var sx = wb.selection.x, sy = wb.selection.y;
    var sw = wb.selection.w, sh = wb.selection.h;
    img.onload = function() {
      try {
        // ctx با setTransform(dpr) است → مختصات CSS
        ctx.save();
        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
        ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, sw, sh);
        ctx.restore();
      } catch (e) {
        ctx.drawImage(img, sx, sy, sw, sh);
      }
      wb.selection = null;
      clearSelUI();
      showToast('ثبت شد');
    };
    img.src = wb.selection.dataUrl;
  }
  function deleteSelection() {
    if (!wb.selection) return;
    snapshot();
    // سوراخ از قبل روی بوم هست (cut) — فقط انتخاب را دور بریز
    wb.selection = null;
    clearSelUI();
    showToast('حذف شد');
  }
  function isBgPixel(r, g, b, a, bg) {
    if (a < 12) return true;
    var dr = Math.abs(r - bg[0]), dg = Math.abs(g - bg[1]), db = Math.abs(b - bg[2]);
    // تحمل بیشتر برای ضدپله و هایلایت نیمه‌شفاف
    return (dr + dg + db) < 48;
  }
  function extractSelection(ax, ay, bx, by) {
    var W = canvas.clientWidth, H = canvas.clientHeight;
    var xa = Math.max(0, Math.min(W, Math.floor(Math.min(ax, bx))));
    var ya = Math.max(0, Math.min(H, Math.floor(Math.min(ay, by))));
    var xb = Math.max(0, Math.min(W, Math.ceil(Math.max(ax, bx))));
    var yb = Math.max(0, Math.min(H, Math.ceil(Math.max(ay, by))));
    if (xb - xa < 4 || yb - ya < 4) {
      showToast('کادر خیلی کوچک است');
      return;
    }
    var bw = canvas.width, bh = canvas.height;
    var scaleX = bw / Math.max(1, W);
    var scaleY = bh / Math.max(1, H);
    var px = Math.floor(xa * scaleX), py = Math.floor(ya * scaleY);
    var pw = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((xb - xa) * scaleX));
    var ph = Math.max(1, Math.ceil((yb - ya) * scaleY));
    if (px + pw > bw) pw = bw - px;
    if (py + ph > bh) ph = bh - py;
    if (pw < 2 || ph < 2) {
      showToast('کادر خیلی کوچک است');
      return;
    }
    var img = ctx.getImageData(px, py, pw, ph);
    var data = img.data;
    var bg = boardBgRgbSel();
    var hasContent = false;
    var minX = pw, minY = ph, maxX = 0, maxY = 0;
    var mask = new Uint8Array(pw * ph);
    for (var i = 0, n = data.length; i < n; i += 4) {
      var r = data[i], g = data[i + 1], b = data[i + 2], a = data[i + 3];
      var pi = (i / 4) | 0;
      if (!isBgPixel(r, g, b, a, bg)) {
        hasContent = true;
        mask[pi] = 1;
        var ix = pi % pw, iy = (pi / pw) | 0;
        if (ix < minX) minX = ix;
        if (iy < minY) minY = iy;
        if (ix > maxX) maxX = ix;
        if (iy > maxY) maxY = iy;
      }
    }
    if (!hasContent) {
      showToast('محتوایی داخل کادر پیدا نشد');
      clearSelUI();
      return;
    }
    minX = Math.max(0, minX - 2);
    minY = Math.max(0, minY - 2);
    maxX = Math.min(pw - 1, maxX + 2);
    maxY = Math.min(ph - 1, maxY + 2);
    var tw = maxX - minX + 1, th = maxY - minY + 1;
    var off = document.createElement('canvas');
    off.width = tw; off.height = th;
    var octx = off.getContext('2d');
    var trimmed = octx.createImageData(tw, th);
    for (var yy = 0; yy < th; yy++) {
      for (var xx = 0; xx < tw; xx++) {
        var si = ((yy + minY) * pw + (xx + minX)) * 4;
        var di = (yy * tw + xx) * 4;
        var mpi = (yy + minY) * pw + (xx + minX);
        if (mask[mpi]) {
          trimmed.data[di] = data[si];
          trimmed.data[di + 1] = data[si + 1];
          trimmed.data[di + 2] = data[si + 2];
          trimmed.data[di + 3] = data[si + 3];
        }
      }
    }
    octx.putImageData(trimmed, 0, 0);
    var dataUrl = off.toDataURL('image/png');

    snapshot();
    var full = ctx.getImageData(px + minX, py + minY, tw, th);
    var fd = full.data;
    for (var j = 0; j < fd.length; j += 4) {
      if (trimmed.data[j + 3] > 10) {
        fd[j] = bg[0]; fd[j + 1] = bg[1]; fd[j + 2] = bg[2]; fd[j + 3] = 255;
      }
    }
    ctx.putImageData(full, px + minX, py + minY);

    var cssX = xa + minX / scaleX;
    var cssY = ya + minY / scaleY;
    var cssW = tw / scaleX;
    var cssH = th / scaleY;
    wb.selection = {
      dataUrl: dataUrl,
      x: cssX,
      y: cssY,
      w: cssW,
      h: cssH,
      originX: cssX,
      originY: cssY,
      cut: true
    };
    showFloatingSelection();
    showToast('انتخاب شد — بکشید جابه‌جا کنید');
  }
  function showFloatingSelection() {
    clearSelUI();
    if (!wb.selection) return;
    var ov = document.getElementById('wbShapeOverlay');
    if (!ov) return;
    // parent می‌تواند none بماند؛ خود float با auto رویداد می‌گیرد
    var wrap = document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className = 'wb-sel-float';
    wrap.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
    wrap.style.zIndex = '20';
    wrap.style.left = wb.selection.x + 'px';
    wrap.style.top = wb.selection.y + 'px';
    wrap.style.width = wb.selection.w + 'px';
    wrap.style.height = wb.selection.h + 'px';
    var cnv = document.createElement('canvas');

    var dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
    cnv.width = Math.max(1, Math.floor(wb.selection.w * dpr));
    cnv.height = Math.max(1, Math.floor(wb.selection.h * dpr));
    var c = cnv.getContext('2d');
    var im = new Image();
    im.onload = function() {
      c.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
      c.clearRect(0, 0, wb.selection.w, wb.selection.h);
      c.drawImage(im, 0, 0, wb.selection.w, wb.selection.h);
    };
    im.src = wb.selection.dataUrl;
    wrap.appendChild(cnv);
    var acts = document.createElement('div');
    acts.className = 'wb-sel-actions';
    var bDel = document.createElement('button');
    bDel.className = 'danger';
    bDel.textContent = '🗑 حذف';
    bDel.onclick = function(e) { e.stopPropagation(); deleteSelection(); };
    var bOk = document.createElement('button');
    bOk.className = 'ok';
    bOk.textContent = '✓ ثبت';
    bOk.onclick = function(e) { e.stopPropagation(); stampSelection(); };
    var bCancel = document.createElement('button');
    bCancel.textContent = '✕';
    bCancel.onclick = function(e) {
      e.stopPropagation();
      // بازگردانی به جای اول
      if (wb.selection) {
        wb.selection.x = wb.selection.originX;
        wb.selection.y = wb.selection.originY;
        stampSelection();
      }
    };
    acts.appendChild(bDel);
    acts.appendChild(bOk);
    acts.appendChild(bCancel);
    wrap.appendChild(acts);
    // drag
    var dragging = false, dsx = 0, dsy = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0;
    wrap.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(ev) {
      if (ev.target.closest && ev.target.closest('.wb-sel-actions')) return;
      dragging = true;
      wrap.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId);
      dsx = ev.clientX; dsy = ev.clientY;
      ox = wb.selection.x; oy = wb.selection.y;
      ev.preventDefault();
      ev.stopPropagation();
    });
    wrap.addEventListener('pointermove', function(ev) {
      if (!dragging || !wb.selection) return;
      var dx = (ev.clientX - dsx) / (wb.scale || 1);
      var dy = (ev.clientY - dsy) / (wb.scale || 1);
      // approximate: pan already in view; selection is in content coords under transform
      // overlay is not transformed with pan/scale the same way — match canvas content coords
      var rect = workspace.getBoundingClientRect();
      var sx = ev.clientX - rect.left;
      var sy = ev.clientY - rect.top;
      // better: use delta in screen / scale
      wb.selection.x = ox + (ev.clientX - dsx) / (wb.scale || 1);
      wb.selection.y = oy + (ev.clientY - dsy) / (wb.scale || 1);
      wrap.style.left = wb.selection.x + 'px';
      wrap.style.top = wb.selection.y + 'px';
    });
    wrap.addEventListener('pointerup', function() { dragging = false; });
    ov.appendChild(wrap);
  }
  function updateSelBox(a, b) {
    clearSelUI();
    var ov = document.getElementById('wbShapeOverlay');
    if (!ov) return;
    var x1 = Math.min(a.x, b.x), y1 = Math.min(a.y, b.y);
    var x2 = Math.max(a.x, b.x), y2 = Math.max(a.y, b.y);
    var box = document.createElement('div');
    box.className = 'wb-sel-box';
    box.style.left = x1 + 'px';
    box.style.top = y1 + 'px';
    box.style.width = Math.max(1, x2 - x1) + 'px';
    box.style.height = Math.max(1, y2 - y1) + 'px';
    ov.appendChild(box);
  }
  wb.cancelSelection = cancelSelection;
  wb.stampSelection = stampSelection;
  wb.deleteSelection = deleteSelection;

  var _dblTapT = 0, _dblTapX = 0, _dblTapY = 0;

  function startDraw(e) {
    // اگر فقط یک انگشت/کلیک است، قفل پینچ را باز کن (باگ قفل دائمی تخته)
    if (e.touches && e.touches.length >= 2) {
      isPinching = true;
      return;
    }
    if (e.touches && e.touches.length <= 1) {
      isPinching = false;
      isPanning = false;
    }
    // اگر mousePan گیر کرده بود و دکمه موس بالا است، باز کن
    if (mousePan && e.buttons === 0 && !(e.touches && e.touches.length)) {
      mousePan = false;
    }
    if (isPinching || isPanning) return;
    if (mousePan && wb.tool !== 'pan') { mousePan = false; }

    if (wb.tool === 'pan') {
      e.preventDefault();
      mousePan = true;
      var src = (e.touches && e.touches[0]) ? e.touches[0] : e;
      panStartX = src.clientX;
      panStartY = src.clientY;
      panOriginX = wb.panX || 0;
      panOriginY = wb.panY || 0;
      try { workspace.style.cursor = 'grabbing'; canvas.style.cursor = 'grabbing'; } catch (err) {}
      return;
    }
    e.preventDefault();
    const p = pos(e);

    // دابل‌تپ: تعویض ماژیک ↔ پاک‌کن (قبل از رسم) — قابل خاموش از تنظیمات
    if (isWbDblTapEnabled() && (wb.tool === 'marker' || wb.tool === 'eraser' || wb.tool === 'highlighter')) {
      var now = Date.now();
      var sx = p.sx || 0, sy = p.sy || 0;
      if (now - _dblTapT < 350 && Math.abs(sx - _dblTapX) < 30 && Math.abs(sy - _dblTapY) < 30) {
        // یک کلیک قبلی نقطه گذاشته — برگردان
        if (wb.history && wb.history.length) {
          try {
            var prev = wb.history.pop();
            var im = new Image();
            im.onload = function() {
              ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.clientWidth, canvas.clientHeight);
              ctx.drawImage(im, 0, 0, canvas.clientWidth, canvas.clientHeight);
            };
            im.src = prev;
          } catch (err) {}
        }
        if (wb.tool === 'marker' || wb.tool === 'highlighter') {
          setWbTool('eraser');
          showToast('پاک‌کن فعال شد');
        } else {
          setWbTool('marker');
          showToast('ماژیک فعال شد');
        }
        _dblTapT = 0;
        wb.drawing = false;
        return;
      }
      _dblTapT = now;
      _dblTapX = sx;
      _dblTapY = sy;
    }

    // Resizable shape placement: drag to size
    if (wb.tool === 'shapes' && wb.pendingShape) {
      snapshot();
      wb.shapeMode = wb.pendingShape;
      wb.shapeStart = p;
      wb.drawing = true;
      return;
    }

    if (wb.tool === 'shapes' || wb.tool === 'extra') return;

    if (wb.tool === 'select') {
      if (wb.selection) {
        // اگر روی float کلیک شده، رها کن
        var tgt = e.target;
        if (tgt && tgt.closest && tgt.closest('.wb-sel-float')) return;
        stampSelection();
        return;
      }
      wb.selecting = true;
      wb.selStart = p;
      wb.drawing = true;
      clearSelUI();
      return;
    }

    snapshot();
    wb.drawing = true;
    wb.lastX = p.x; wb.lastY = p.y;
    wb.midX = p.x; wb.midY = p.y;
    wb._drewStroke = false;
    wb._strokeDist = 0;
    if (wb.tool === 'filler') {
      var sl = document.getElementById('wbFillTol');
      if (sl) {
        var vv = parseInt(sl.value, 10);
        if (!isNaN(vv)) wb.fillTol = vv;
      }
      // اطمینان از سبک فیللر
      if (!wb.fillStyle) wb.fillStyle = 'solid';
      floodFillFull(Math.floor(p.x), Math.floor(p.y));
      wb.drawing = false;
      return;
    }
    // نقطه با یک کلیک — هایلایت در شروع نقطه نمی‌گذارد (فقط با stroke پیوسته)
    if (wb.tool === 'marker' || wb.tool === 'eraser') {
      ctx.save();
      if (wb.tool === 'eraser') {
        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
        var bgDot = '#ffffff';
        if (wb.boardStyle === 'black') bgDot = '#0f172a';
        else if (wb.boardStyle === 'green') bgDot = '#14532d';
        ctx.fillStyle = bgDot;
        var er = Math.max(10, wb.size * 3) / 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, er, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
        if (typeof wbRepaintPatternAlongStroke === 'function') {
          wbRepaintPatternAlongStroke(p.x, p.y, p.x, p.y, er);
        }
        wb._eraserDirty = true;
      } else {
        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
        ctx.fillStyle = wb.color || '#111827';
        var mr = Math.max(1, wb.size) / 2;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, Math.max(0.8, mr), 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
      wb._drewStroke = true;
    }
    // highlighter: snapshot برای رسم یکنواخت در move
    if (wb.tool === 'highlighter') {
      wb._hlPts = [{ x: p.x, y: p.y }];
      try {
        wb._hlSnap = document.createElement('canvas');
        wb._hlSnap.width = canvas.width;
        wb._hlSnap.height = canvas.height;
        wb._hlSnap.getContext('2d').drawImage(canvas, 0, 0);
      } catch (eH) { wb._hlSnap = null; }
    } else {
      wb._hlPts = null;
      wb._hlSnap = null;
    }
  }



  var _drawRaf = 0;
  var _pendingDraw = null;
  function moveDraw(e) {
    // اگر pan فعال است رسم نکن
    if (mousePan || isPanning || (wb.tool === 'pan')) return;
    if (!wb.drawing) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    const p = pos(e);
    // ادغام رویدادها برای روان‌تر شدن (کمتر لگ)
    _pendingDraw = p;
    if (_drawRaf) return;
    _drawRaf = requestAnimationFrame(function() {
      _drawRaf = 0;
      var p2 = _pendingDraw;
      if (!p2 || !wb.drawing) return;
      _doMoveDraw(p2);
    });
    return;
  }
  function _doMoveDraw(p) {

    if (wb.tool === 'select' && wb.selecting && wb.selStart) {
      updateSelBox(wb.selStart, p);
      wb.lastX = p.x; wb.lastY = p.y;
      return;
    }

    // live preview for shapes
    if (wb.shapeMode && wb.shapeStart) {
      if (wb.history.length) {
        const img = new Image();
        const start = wb.shapeStart;
        const type = wb.shapeMode;
        img.onload = function() {
          ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.clientWidth, canvas.clientHeight);
          ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.clientWidth, canvas.clientHeight);
          drawShapePreview(type, start, p);
        };
        img.src = wb.history[wb.history.length - 1];
      }
      return;
    }

    ctx.save();
    var x0 = wb.lastX, y0 = wb.lastY, x1 = p.x, y1 = p.y;
    var dist = Math.sqrt((x1 - x0) * (x1 - x0) + (y1 - y0) * (y1 - y0));
    if (wb.tool === 'eraser') {
      // به‌جای شفاف کردن (که در undo/JPEG سیاه می‌شود) با رنگ پس‌زمینه پاک کن
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      var bg = '#ffffff';
      if (wb.boardStyle === 'black') bg = '#0f172a';
      else if (wb.boardStyle === 'green') bg = '#14532d';
      var eW = Math.max(10, wb.size * 3);
      ctx.strokeStyle = bg;
      ctx.lineWidth = eW;
      ctx.lineCap = 'round';
      ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x0, y0);
      ctx.lineTo(x1, y1);
      ctx.stroke();
      // جدول و نقطه‌ها دوباره در مسیر پاک‌کن کشیده شوند
      if (typeof wbRepaintPatternAlongStroke === 'function') {
        wbRepaintPatternAlongStroke(x0, y0, x1, y1, eW / 2);
      }
      wb._eraserDirty = true;
    } else if (wb.tool === 'highlighter') {
      // یکنواخت: کل مسیر از شروع stroke یک‌بار با شفافیت ثابت کشیده می‌شود
      // (جلوگیری از تیره شدن محل هم‌پوشانی تکه‌های نیمه‌شفاف)
      if (!wb._hlPts) wb._hlPts = [{ x: x0, y: y0 }];
      wb._hlPts.push({ x: x1, y: y1 });
      var hlA = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(0.55, (wb.hlOpacity || 30) / 100));
      var hlW = Math.max(14, wb.size * 5.5);
      // بازگردانی تصویر شروع stroke
      if (wb._hlSnap) {
        try {
          ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
          ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
          ctx.drawImage(wb._hlSnap, 0, 0);
          var dpr = wb.renderScale || 1;
          ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
        } catch (eSnap) {}
      }
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      ctx.strokeStyle = hexAlpha(wb.color, hlA);
      ctx.lineWidth = hlW;
      ctx.lineCap = 'round';
      ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
      ctx.miterLimit = 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      var pts = wb._hlPts;
      if (pts.length === 1) {
        ctx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
        ctx.lineTo(pts[0].x + 0.01, pts[0].y);
      } else if (pts.length === 2) {
        ctx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
        ctx.lineTo(pts[1].x, pts[1].y);
      } else {
        ctx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
        // منحنی نرم برای یکنواختی بیشتر
        for (var hi = 1; hi < pts.length - 1; hi++) {
          var mx = (pts[hi].x + pts[hi + 1].x) / 2;
          var my = (pts[hi].y + pts[hi + 1].y) / 2;
          ctx.quadraticCurveTo(pts[hi].x, pts[hi].y, mx, my);
        }
        var last = pts[pts.length - 1];
        ctx.lineTo(last.x, last.y);
      }
      ctx.stroke();
      wb._drewStroke = true;
      wb._strokeDist = (wb._strokeDist || 0) + dist;
    } else {

      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      ctx.strokeStyle = wb.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = wb.size;
      ctx.lineCap = 'round';
      ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x0, y0);
      ctx.lineTo(x1, y1);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    wb.lastX = p.x; wb.lastY = p.y;
  }

  function drawShapePreview(type, a, b) {
    const x1 = Math.min(a.x, b.x), y1 = Math.min(a.y, b.y);
    const x2 = Math.max(a.x, b.x), y2 = Math.max(a.y, b.y);
    const w = x2 - x1, h = y2 - y1;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.strokeStyle = wb.color;
    ctx.fillStyle = hexAlpha(wb.color, 0.12);
    ctx.lineWidth = wb.size;
    ctx.beginPath();
    if (type === 'rect') {
      ctx.rect(x1, y1, w, h);
      ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    } else if (type === 'circle') {
      const r = Math.max(w, h) / 2;
      const cx = (a.x + b.x) / 2, cy = (a.y + b.y) / 2;
      ctx.arc(cx, cy, r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    } else if (type === 'ellipse') {
      const cx = (a.x + b.x) / 2, cy = (a.y + b.y) / 2;
      ctx.ellipse(cx, cy, Math.abs(w) / 2, Math.abs(h) / 2, 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    } else if (type === 'tri') {
      ctx.moveTo((a.x + b.x) / 2, y1);
      ctx.lineTo(x2, y2);
      ctx.lineTo(x1, y2);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
    } else if (type === 'line') {
      ctx.moveTo(a.x, a.y);
      ctx.lineTo(b.x, b.y);
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function endDraw(e) {
    // همیشه قفل‌های جابه‌جایی/پینچ را باز کن
    mousePan = false;
    if (!(e && e.touches && e.touches.length >= 2)) {
      isPinching = false;
      isPanning = false;
    }
    try {
      if (wb.tool === 'pan') {
        workspace.style.cursor = 'grab';
        canvas.style.cursor = 'grab';
      }
    } catch (errC) {}
    if (wb.tool === 'select' && wb.selecting && wb.selStart) {
      var pEnd;
      try {
        if (e && e.changedTouches && e.changedTouches[0]) {
          pEnd = pos({ clientX: e.changedTouches[0].clientX, clientY: e.changedTouches[0].clientY, touches: null });
        } else if (e) {
          pEnd = pos(e);
        } else {
          pEnd = { x: wb.lastX, y: wb.lastY };
        }
      } catch (err) {
        pEnd = { x: wb.lastX, y: wb.lastY };
      }
      wb.selecting = false;
      var s = wb.selStart;
      wb.selStart = null;
      clearSelUI();
      extractSelection(s.x, s.y, pEnd.x, pEnd.y);
      wb.drawing = false;
      return;
    }
    // هایلایت تک‌کلیک: یک نقطه گرد یکنواخت
    if (wb.tool === 'highlighter' && wb.drawing && !wb._drewStroke) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      var hlA2 = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(0.55, (wb.hlOpacity || 30) / 100));
      ctx.fillStyle = hexAlpha(wb.color, hlA2);
      var hr = Math.max(14, wb.size * 5.5) / 2;
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(wb.lastX, wb.lastY, hr, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
    // پاک کردن بافر هایلایت
    if (wb.tool === 'highlighter') {
      wb._hlPts = null;
      wb._hlSnap = null;
    }

    if (wb.shapeMode && wb.shapeStart) {
      const p = e ? pos(e.changedTouches ? e.changedTouches[0] : e) : { x: wb.lastX, y: wb.lastY };
      // finalize from last history frame + shape
      if (wb.history.length) {
        const img = new Image();
        const start = wb.shapeStart;
        const type = wb.shapeMode;
        img.onload = function() {
          ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.clientWidth, canvas.clientHeight);
          ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.clientWidth, canvas.clientHeight);
          drawShapePreview(type, start, p);
        };
        img.src = wb.history[wb.history.length - 1];
      }
      wb.shapeMode = null;
      wb.shapeStart = null;
    }
    // بعد از پاک‌کن: تصاویر واردشده را دوباره بکش تا پاک نشوند
    if (wb._eraserDirty) {
      wb._eraserDirty = false;
      if (typeof wbRedrawProtectedImages === 'function') wbRedrawProtectedImages();
    }
    wb.drawing = false;
  }

  canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', startDraw);

  canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', moveDraw);
  window.addEventListener('mouseup', endDraw);
  canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', startDraw, { passive: false });
  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', moveDraw, { passive: false });
  canvas.addEventListener('touchend', endDraw);
  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e) {
    if (e.pointerType === 'mouse') return; // mouse already handled
    try { canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
    startDraw(e);
  });
  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', function(e) {
    if (e.pointerType === 'mouse') return;
    moveDraw(e);
  });
  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', function(e) {
    // موس هم endDraw می‌خواهد (select/filler) — mouseup سراسری ممکن است دیر برسد
    endDraw(e);
  });

  // ثبت شکل نامنظم با کلیک جای دیگر + دابل‌کلیک ماژیک/پاک‌کن
  var lastTapTime = 0;
  var lastTapX = 0, lastTapY = 0;
  function handleBoardTap(e) {
    // ignore multi-touch
    if (e.touches && e.touches.length > 1) return;
    if (isPinching || mousePan) return;

    var now = Date.now();
    var p = pos(e);
    var isDbl = (now - lastTapTime < 320)
      && Math.abs((p.sx || 0) - lastTapX) < 24
      && Math.abs((p.sy || 0) - lastTapY) < 24;

    // اگر در حال ویرایش شکل نامنظم هستیم و روی دستگیره نیست
    if (wb.editingPoly && typeof wb.finishEditPoly === 'function') {
      var t = e.target;
      if (!t || !t.classList || !t.classList.contains('wb-handle')) {
        // delay slightly so handle drag can start first
        setTimeout(function() {
          if (wb.editingPoly && wb.finishEditPoly) wb.finishEditPoly();
        }, 10);
      }
      lastTapTime = now;
      lastTapX = p.sx || 0;
      lastTapY = p.sy || 0;
      return;
    }

    if (isDbl && isWbDblTapEnabled()) {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      if (wb.tool === 'marker' || wb.tool === 'highlighter') {
        setWbTool('eraser');
        showToast('پاک‌کن فعال شد');
      } else {
        setWbTool('marker');
        showToast('ماژیک فعال شد');
      }
      lastTapTime = 0;
      return;
    }
    lastTapTime = now;
    lastTapX = p.sx || 0;
    lastTapY = p.sy || 0;
  }

  function setWbTool(name) {
    wb.tool = name;
    wb.pendingShape = null;
    var boardTb = document.getElementById('wbToolbar');
    (boardTb ? boardTb.querySelectorAll('.wb-tool') : document.querySelectorAll('#wbToolbar .wb-tool')).forEach(function(b) {
      b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.tool === name);
    });
    if (wb.hidePops) wb.hidePops();
    try {
      if (workspace) {
        workspace.style.cursor = (name === 'pan') ? 'grab' : '';
        if (canvas) canvas.style.cursor = (name === 'pan') ? 'grab' : 'crosshair';
      }
    } catch (err) {}
  }

  wb.setTool = setWbTool;

  canvas.addEventListener('dblclick', function(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (wb.editingPoly && wb.finishEditPoly) {
      wb.finishEditPoly();
      return;
    }
    if (!isWbDblTapEnabled()) return;
    if (wb.tool === 'marker' || wb.tool === 'highlighter') {
      setWbTool('eraser');
      showToast('پاک‌کن فعال شد');
    } else {
      setWbTool('marker');
      showToast('ماژیک فعال شد');
    }
  });

  // touch double-tap
  canvas.addEventListener('touchend', function(e) {
    if (isPinching) return;
    if (e.touches && e.touches.length > 0) return;
    // synthesize dbl from last touch position
    if (!e.changedTouches || !e.changedTouches[0]) return;
    var fake = {
      touches: null,
      target: e.target,
      clientX: e.changedTouches[0].clientX,
      clientY: e.changedTouches[0].clientY,
      preventDefault: function() { e.preventDefault(); },
      stopPropagation: function() { e.stopPropagation(); }
    };
    // only for double-tap detection path when not drawing long strokes
    var now = Date.now();
    var rect = workspace.getBoundingClientRect();
    var sx = fake.clientX - rect.left;
    var sy = fake.clientY - rect.top;
    if (now - lastTapTime < 320 && Math.abs(sx - lastTapX) < 28 && Math.abs(sy - lastTapY) < 28) {
      if (wb.editingPoly && wb.finishEditPoly) {
        wb.finishEditPoly();
      } else if (isWbDblTapEnabled()) {
        if (wb.tool === 'marker' || wb.tool === 'highlighter') {
          setWbTool('eraser');
          showToast('پاک‌کن فعال شد');
        } else {
          setWbTool('marker');
          showToast('ماژیک فعال شد');
        }
      }
      lastTapTime = 0;
    } else {
      lastTapTime = now;
      lastTapX = sx;
      lastTapY = sy;
      // ثبت شکل اگر در حالت ویرایش
      if (wb.editingPoly && wb.finishEditPoly) {
        var tgt = e.target;
        if (!tgt || !tgt.classList || !tgt.classList.contains('wb-handle')) {
          setTimeout(function() {
            if (wb.editingPoly && wb.finishEditPoly) wb.finishEditPoly();
          }, 30);
        }
      }
    }
  }, { passive: true });



  
  // ——— Pinch zoom + two-finger pan + wheel zoom ———
  var pinchStartDist = 0;
  var pinchStartScale = 1;
  var pinchStartPanX = 0, pinchStartPanY = 0;
  var pinchMidX = 0, pinchMidY = 0;
  var isPinching = false;
  var isPanning = false;
  var mousePan = false;
  var spaceDown = false;
  var panStartX = 0, panStartY = 0, panOriginX = 0, panOriginY = 0;

  function touchDist(t0, t1) {
    var dx = t0.clientX - t1.clientX, dy = t0.clientY - t1.clientY;
    return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1;
  }
  function touchMidWorkspace(t0, t1) {
    var rect = workspace.getBoundingClientRect();
    return {
      x: ((t0.clientX + t1.clientX) / 2) - rect.left,
      y: ((t0.clientY + t1.clientY) / 2) - rect.top
    };
  }

  function onPinchStart(e) {
    if (!e.touches || e.touches.length !== 2) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    isPinching = true;
    isPanning = true;
    wb.drawing = false;
    wb.shapeMode = null;
    pinchStartDist = touchDist(e.touches[0], e.touches[1]);
    pinchStartScale = wb.scale || 1;
    pinchStartPanX = wb.panX || 0;
    pinchStartPanY = wb.panY || 0;
    var mid = touchMidWorkspace(e.touches[0], e.touches[1]);
    pinchMidX = mid.x;
    pinchMidY = mid.y;
  }

  function onPinchMove(e) {
    if (!isPinching || !e.touches || e.touches.length !== 2) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    var dist = touchDist(e.touches[0], e.touches[1]);
    var mid = touchMidWorkspace(e.touches[0], e.touches[1]);
    var newScale = clampScale(pinchStartScale * (dist / pinchStartDist));
    // keep the world point under original midpoint stable, then pan with mid movement
    var wx = (pinchMidX - pinchStartPanX) / pinchStartScale;
    var wy = (pinchMidY - pinchStartPanY) / pinchStartScale;
    wb.scale = newScale;
    wb.panX = mid.x - wx * newScale;
    wb.panY = mid.y - wy * newScale;
    applyViewTransform();
  }

  function onPinchEnd(e) {
    if (!e.touches || e.touches.length < 2) {
      isPinching = false;
      isPanning = false;
    }
  }

  // Listen on both workspace and canvas (capture) so 2-finger always works
  [workspace, canvas].forEach(function(el) {
    if (!el) return;
    el.addEventListener('touchstart', onPinchStart, { passive: false, capture: true });
    el.addEventListener('touchmove', onPinchMove, { passive: false, capture: true });
    el.addEventListener('touchend', onPinchEnd, { passive: false, capture: true });
    el.addEventListener('touchcancel', onPinchEnd, { passive: false, capture: true });
  });

  workspace.addEventListener('wheel', function(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    var rect = workspace.getBoundingClientRect();
    var sx = e.clientX - rect.left;
    var sy = e.clientY - rect.top;
    var factor = e.deltaY < 0 ? 1.12 : 1 / 1.12;
    zoomAt(sx, sy, wb.scale * factor);
  }, { passive: false });

  window.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
    if (e.code === 'Space') spaceDown = true;
  });
  window.addEventListener('keyup', function(e) {
    if (e.code === 'Space') { spaceDown = false; mousePan = false; }
  });
  workspace.addEventListener('mousedown', function(e) {
    if (e.button === 1 || spaceDown || (e.button === 0 && wb.tool === 'pan')) {
      e.preventDefault();
      mousePan = true;
      panStartX = e.clientX;
      panStartY = e.clientY;
      panOriginX = wb.panX;
      panOriginY = wb.panY;
      if (wb.tool === 'pan') {
        try { workspace.style.cursor = 'grabbing'; } catch (err) {}
      }
    }
  });
  window.addEventListener('mousemove', function(e) {
    if (!mousePan) return;
    wb.panX = panOriginX + (e.clientX - panStartX);
    wb.panY = panOriginY + (e.clientY - panStartY);
    applyViewTransform();
  });
  window.addEventListener('touchmove', function(e) {
    if (!mousePan || !e.touches || !e.touches[0]) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    var t = e.touches[0];
    wb.panX = panOriginX + (t.clientX - panStartX);
    wb.panY = panOriginY + (t.clientY - panStartY);
    applyViewTransform();
  }, { passive: false });
  function endMousePan() {
    mousePan = false;
    try {
      if (wb.tool === 'pan') {
        if (workspace) workspace.style.cursor = 'grab';
        if (canvas) canvas.style.cursor = 'grab';
      } else {
        if (canvas) canvas.style.cursor = 'crosshair';
      }
    } catch (err) {}
  }
  window.addEventListener('mouseup', endMousePan);
  window.addEventListener('pointerup', endMousePan);
  window.addEventListener('blur', endMousePan);

  applyViewTransform();


  var boardToolbar = document.getElementById('wbToolbar');
  if (boardToolbar) boardToolbar.querySelectorAll('.wb-tool').forEach(function(btn) {
    btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
      const t = btn.dataset.tool;
      if (t === 'filler' && wb.tool === 'filler') {
        togglePop('wbFillerPanel');
        return;
      }
      if (t === 'highlighter' && wb.tool === 'highlighter') {
        togglePop('wbHlPanel');
        return;
      }
      boardToolbar.querySelectorAll('.wb-tool').forEach(function(b) { b.classList.remove('active'); });
      btn.classList.add('active');
      if (wb.tool === 'select' && t !== 'select' && wb.selection) {
        stampSelection();
      }
      wb.tool = t;
      wb.pendingShape = null;
      hidePops();
      try {
        mousePan = false;
        isPanning = false;
        if (t !== 'pan') { isPinching = false; }
        if (workspace) workspace.style.cursor = (t === 'pan') ? 'grab' : '';
        if (canvas) canvas.style.cursor = (t === 'pan') ? 'grab' : 'crosshair';
      } catch (errCur) {}
      if (t === 'shapes') togglePop('wbShapesPanel');
      if (t === 'extra') togglePop('wbExtraPanel');
      if (t === 'filler') {
        if (!wb.fillStyle) wb.fillStyle = 'solid';
        togglePop('wbFillerPanel');
        showToast('سبک: ' + (wb.fillStyle || 'solid') + ' — روی ناحیه بزن');
      }
      if (t === 'select') {
        togglePop('wbSelectPanel');
        showToast('کادر دور نوشته/رسم بکشید، بعد جابه‌جا یا حذف کنید');
      }
    });
  });


  function hidePops() {
    [
      'wbFillerPanel', 'wbHlPanel', 'wbShapesPanel', 'wbExtraPanel', 'wbBoardStylePanel',
      'wbMenuDrop', 'wbSelectPanel',
      'wbPdfShapesPanel', 'wbPdfFillerPanel', 'wbPdfHlPanel', 'wbPdfExtraPanel', 'wbPdfSelectPanel'
    ].forEach(function(id) {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) el.style.display = 'none';
    });
  }
  function togglePop(id) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!el) return;
    const open = el.style.display === 'none' || !el.style.display;
    hidePops();
    el.style.display = open ? 'flex' : 'none';
  }
  wb.hidePops = hidePops;
  window.wbHideAllPops = hidePops;
  window.wbTogglePop = togglePop;

  // کلیک بیرون از نوار/پنل → بستن تنظیمات گجت‌ها
  if (!window._wbPopOutsideBound) {
    window._wbPopOutsideBound = true;
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e) {
      var t = e.target;
      if (!t) return;
      if (t.closest && (
        t.closest('.wb-toolbar') ||
        t.closest('.wb-pop') ||
        t.closest('.wb-menu-drop') ||
        t.closest('.wb-bottom-bar') ||
        t.closest('#wbMenuBtn') ||
        t.closest('#wbStyleBtn')
      )) return;
      hidePops();
      if (typeof wbPdfHidePops === 'function') wbPdfHidePops();
    }, true);
  }



  var selPanel = document.getElementById('wbSelectPanel');
  if (selPanel && !selPanel.dataset.bound) {
    selPanel.dataset.bound = '1';
    var bd = document.getElementById('wbSelDelete');
    var bc = document.getElementById('wbSelConfirm');
    var bx = document.getElementById('wbSelCancel');
    if (bd) bd.onclick = function() { if (wb.deleteSelection) wb.deleteSelection(); hidePops(); };
    if (bc) bc.onclick = function() { if (wb.stampSelection) wb.stampSelection(); hidePops(); };
    if (bx) bx.onclick = function() {
      if (wb.selection) {
        wb.selection.x = wb.selection.originX;
        wb.selection.y = wb.selection.originY;
        if (wb.stampSelection) wb.stampSelection();
      }
      hidePops();
    };
  }
  // Delete/Backspace key removes selection
  if (!window._wbSelKeyBound) {
    window._wbSelKeyBound = true;
    window.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
      if (!wb || wb.tool !== 'select' || !wb.selection) return;
      if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (wb.deleteSelection) wb.deleteSelection();
      } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (wb.stampSelection) wb.stampSelection();
      } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        if (wb.selection) {
          wb.selection.x = wb.selection.originX;
          wb.selection.y = wb.selection.originY;
          if (wb.stampSelection) wb.stampSelection();
        }
      }
    });
  }

  const fp = document.getElementById('wbFillerPanel');
  if (fp) fp.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-fill]');
    if (!b) return;
    wb.fillStyle = b.getAttribute('data-fill') || b.dataset.fill || 'solid';
    // برجسته کردن دکمه فعال
    fp.querySelectorAll('[data-fill]').forEach(function(x) {
      x.style.outline = (x === b) ? '2px solid var(--accent)' : '';
    });
    showToast('سبک فیللر: ' + (b.textContent || wb.fillStyle).trim());
    // پنل را باز نگه دار تا بتوان سبک را عوض کرد؛ با کلیک بیرون بسته می‌شود
  });


  var fillTolSlider = document.getElementById('wbFillTol');
  var fillTolLabel = document.getElementById('wbFillTolLabel');
  if (fillTolSlider) {
    function syncFillTol() {
      var v = parseInt(fillTolSlider.value, 10) || 50;
      wb.fillTol = v;
      if (fillTolLabel) {
        var fa = '۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹';
        fillTolLabel.textContent = String(v).replace(/\d/g, function(d){ return fa[+d]; }) + '٪';
      }
      try { localStorage.setItem('wbFillTol', String(v)); } catch (e) {}
    }
    try {
      var saved = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('wbFillTol') || '50', 10);
      if (saved >= 5 && saved <= 100) {
        fillTolSlider.value = String(saved);
        wb.fillTol = saved;
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    syncFillTol();
    fillTolSlider.addEventListener('input', syncFillTol);
  }


  document.getElementById('wbColor').oninput = function(e) { wb.color = e.target.value; };
  document.getElementById('wbSize').oninput = function(e) { wb.size = +e.target.value; };
  document.getElementById('wbSwatch1').onclick = function() {
    wb.color = document.getElementById('wbSwatch1').dataset.c;
    document.getElementById('wbColor').value = wb.color;
  };
  document.getElementById('wbSwatch2').onclick = function() {
    wb.color = document.getElementById('wbSwatch2').dataset.c;
    document.getElementById('wbColor').value = wb.color;
  };
  document.getElementById('wbSaveColor').onclick = function() {
    const slot = wb.swatchSlot === 1 ? 1 : 2;
    const el = document.getElementById('wbSwatch' + slot);
    el.dataset.c = wb.color;
    el.style.background = wb.color;
    wb.swatchSlot = slot === 1 ? 2 : 1;
    showToast('رنگ سواچ ' + slot + ' ذخیره شد');
  };

  document.getElementById('wbUndo').onclick = function() {
    if (!wb.history.length) return;
    wb.future.push(canvas.toDataURL('image/png'));
    restore(wb.history.pop());
  };
  document.getElementById('wbRedo').onclick = function() {
    if (!wb.future.length) return;
    wb.history.push(canvas.toDataURL('image/png'));
    restore(wb.future.pop());
  };

  document.getElementById('wbMenuBtn').onclick = function() { togglePop('wbMenuDrop'); };
  document.getElementById('wbStyleBtn').onclick = function() { togglePop('wbBoardStylePanel'); };
  document.getElementById('wbBoardStylePanel').onclick = function(e) {
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-board]');
    if (!b) return;
    snapshot();
    wb.boardStyle = b.dataset.board;
    wbPaintBackground();
    hidePops();
  };
  document.getElementById('wbClearPage').onclick = function() {
    snapshot();
    wbPaintBackground();
    hidePops();
  };
  document.getElementById('wbToggleGrid').onclick = function() {
    wb.grid = !wb.grid;
    showToast(wb.grid ? 'شبکه روشن' : 'شبکه خاموش');
    hidePops();
  };

  // Shapes: select type then drag on canvas to size
  document.getElementById('wbShapesPanel').addEventListener('click', function(e) {
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-shape]');
    if (b) {
      wb.pendingShape = b.dataset.shape;
      wb.tool = 'shapes';
      hidePops();
      showToast('روی تخته بکشید تا شکل با اندازه دلخواه رسم شود');
    }
  });

  document.getElementById('wbPolyM').onclick = function() {
    const n = parseInt(prompt('تعداد اضلاع چندضلعی منظم (۳ تا ۲۰):', '6'), 10);
    if (n >= 3 && n <= 20) {
      snapshot();
      placeRegularPolygon(n);
      hidePops();
    }
  };
  document.getElementById('wbPolyN').onclick = function() {
    const n = parseInt(prompt('تعداد رأس (۳ تا ۱۶):', '5'), 10);
    if (n >= 3 && n <= 16) { startEditablePolygon(n); hidePops(); }
  };
  document.getElementById('wbInsTableLight').onclick = function() { insertPeriodicThumb(false); hidePops(); };
  document.getElementById('wbInsTableDark').onclick = function() { insertPeriodicThumb(true); hidePops(); };
  document.getElementById('wbAxes').onclick = function() { drawCoordinateAxes(); hidePops(); };

  function placeRegularPolygon(sides) {
    const cx = canvas.clientWidth / 2, cy = canvas.clientHeight / 2, r = 70;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.strokeStyle = wb.color; ctx.lineWidth = wb.size;
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (var i = 0; i < sides; i++) {
      const a = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / sides - Math.PI / 2;
      const x = cx + r * Math.cos(a), y = cy + r * Math.sin(a);
      if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(x, y); else ctx.lineTo(x, y);
    }
    ctx.closePath(); ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }


  var hlOpSlider = document.getElementById('wbHlOpacity');
  var hlOpLabel = document.getElementById('wbHlOpacityLabel');
  if (hlOpSlider) {
    function syncHlOp() {
      var v = parseInt(hlOpSlider.value, 10) || 30;
      wb.hlOpacity = v;
      if (hlOpLabel) {
        var fa = '۰۱۲۳۴۵۶۷۸۹';
        hlOpLabel.textContent = String(v).replace(/\d/g, function(d){ return fa[+d]; }) + '٪';
      }
      try { localStorage.setItem('wbHlOpacity', String(v)); } catch (e) {}
    }
    try {
      var hs = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('wbHlOpacity') || '30', 10);
      if (hs >= 5 && hs <= 90) {
        hlOpSlider.value = String(hs);
        wb.hlOpacity = hs;
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    syncHlOp();
    hlOpSlider.addEventListener('input', syncHlOp);
  }

  function startEditablePolygon(sides) {
    const overlay = document.getElementById('wbShapeOverlay');
    overlay.innerHTML = '';
    // فقط دستگیره‌ها کلیک بخورند؛ کلیک جای دیگر صفحه = ثبت شکل
    overlay.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
    const cx = canvas.clientWidth / 2, cy = canvas.clientHeight / 2, r = 80;
    const pts = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < sides; i++) {
      const a = (Math.PI * 2 * i) / sides - Math.PI / 2;
      pts.push({ x: cx + r * Math.cos(a), y: cy + r * Math.sin(a) });
    }
    const svgNS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
    const svg = document.createElementNS(svgNS, 'svg');
    svg.setAttribute('width', '100%');
    svg.setAttribute('height', '100%');
    svg.style.position = 'absolute';
    svg.style.inset = '0';
    const poly = document.createElementNS(svgNS, 'polygon');
    poly.setAttribute('fill', hexAlpha(wb.color, 0.12));
    poly.setAttribute('stroke', wb.color);
    poly.setAttribute('stroke-width', String(wb.size));
    svg.appendChild(poly);
    overlay.appendChild(svg);
    function updatePoly() {
      poly.setAttribute('points', pts.map(function(p) { return p.x + ',' + p.y; }).join(' '));
      handles.forEach(function(h, i) {
        h.style.left = pts[i].x + 'px';
        h.style.top = pts[i].y + 'px';
      });
      const maxx = Math.max.apply(null, pts.map(function(p) { return p.x; }));
      const miny = Math.min.apply(null, pts.map(function(p) { return p.y; }));
      conf.style.left = (maxx + 8) + 'px';
      conf.style.top = (miny - 8) + 'px';
    }
    const handles = pts.map(function(p, i) {
      const h = document.createElement('div');
      h.className = 'wb-handle';
      h.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
      h.style.left = p.x + 'px';
      h.style.top = p.y + 'px';
      var drag = false;
      h.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(ev) { drag = true; h.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId); ev.preventDefault(); });
      h.addEventListener('pointermove', function(ev) {
        if (!drag) return;
        const r = overlay.getBoundingClientRect();
        pts[i].x = ev.clientX - r.left;
        pts[i].y = ev.clientY - r.top;
        updatePoly();
      });
      h.addEventListener('pointerup', function() { drag = false; });
      overlay.appendChild(h);
      return h;
    });
    const conf = document.createElement('button');
    conf.className = 'wb-confirm';
    conf.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
    conf.textContent = '✓';
    function finishEditPoly() {
      if (!wb.editingPoly) return;
      snapshot();
      ctx.save();
      ctx.strokeStyle = wb.color;
      ctx.fillStyle = hexAlpha(wb.color, 0.12);
      ctx.lineWidth = wb.size;
      ctx.beginPath();
      pts.forEach(function(p, i) { if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y); else ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y); });
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
      overlay.innerHTML = '';
      overlay.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
      wb.editingPoly = false;
      wb.finishEditPoly = null;
      showToast('شکل ثبت شد');
    }
    conf.onclick = function(e) {
      if (e) e.stopPropagation();
      finishEditPoly();
    };
    wb.editingPoly = true;
    wb.finishEditPoly = finishEditPoly;
    // کلیک روی خود overlay (نه روی دستگیره) = ثبت
    overlay.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(ev) {
      if (ev.target === overlay || ev.target === svg || ev.target === poly) {
        finishEditPoly();
      }
    });
    overlay.appendChild(conf);
    updatePoly();
  }

  function insertPeriodicThumb(dark) {
    // جدول تناوبی قابل جابه‌جایی و تغییر اندازه
    var overlay = document.getElementById('wbShapeOverlay');
    if (!overlay) return;
    overlay.innerHTML = '';
    overlay.style.pointerEvents = 'none';

    var W = canvas.clientWidth;
    var H = canvas.clientHeight;
    var box = {
      x: Math.max(10, W * 0.08),
      y: Math.max(10, H * 0.08),
      w: Math.min(W * 0.84, 520),
      h: Math.min(H * 0.55, 280),
      dark: !!dark
    };

    var wrap = document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className = 'wb-ptable-box';
    wrap.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;pointer-events:auto;border:2px solid var(--accent);border-radius:10px;box-shadow:0 8px 28px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);overflow:hidden;cursor:move;z-index:8;';
    var cnv = document.createElement('canvas');
    cnv.style.cssText = 'width:100%;height:100%;display:block;pointer-events:none;';
    wrap.appendChild(cnv);

    function drawTableTo(targetCtx, tw, th, darkMode) {
      var cols = 18, rows = 7;
      var pad = 8;
      var titleH = 22;
      var cellW = (tw - pad * 2) / cols;
      var cellH = (th - pad * 2 - titleH) / rows;
      targetCtx.clearRect(0, 0, tw, th);
      targetCtx.fillStyle = darkMode ? '#0f172a' : '#f8fafc';
      targetCtx.fillRect(0, 0, tw, th);
      targetCtx.fillStyle = darkMode ? '#e2e8f0' : '#334155';
      targetCtx.font = 'bold ' + Math.max(10, Math.min(14, tw / 40)) + 'px Tahoma';
      targetCtx.textAlign = 'center';
      targetCtx.fillText(darkMode ? 'جدول تناوبی (تیره)' : 'جدول تناوبی (روشن)', tw / 2, 16);
      targetCtx.font = Math.max(7, Math.min(11, cellW * 0.45)) + 'px Tahoma';
      var colors = {
        alkali: '#ff6b6b', alkaline: '#f7b731', transition: '#4b7bec', post: '#a55eea',
        metalloid: '#20bf6b', nonmetal: '#26de81', halogen: '#45aaf2', noble: '#8854d0'
      };
      els.forEach(function(e) {
        if (!e.group || e.group < 1) return;
        if (e.cls === 'lanthanide' || e.cls === 'actinide') return;
        var x = pad + (e.group - 1) * cellW;
        var y = pad + titleH + (e.period - 1) * cellH;
        targetCtx.globalAlpha = 0.9;
        targetCtx.fillStyle = colors[e.cls] || '#94a3b8';
        targetCtx.fillRect(x + 1, y + 1, cellW - 2, cellH - 2);
        targetCtx.globalAlpha = 1;
        targetCtx.fillStyle = darkMode ? '#fff' : '#0f172a';
        targetCtx.fillText(e.sym, x + cellW / 2, y + cellH / 2 + 3);
      });
    }

    function layout() {
      wrap.style.left = box.x + 'px';
      wrap.style.top = box.y + 'px';
      wrap.style.width = box.w + 'px';
      wrap.style.height = box.h + 'px';
      var dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
      cnv.width = Math.max(1, Math.floor(box.w * dpr));
      cnv.height = Math.max(1, Math.floor(box.h * dpr));
      var c = cnv.getContext('2d');
      c.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
      drawTableTo(c, box.w, box.h, box.dark);
      placeHandles();
    }

    function placeHandles() {
      // remove old handles
      overlay.querySelectorAll('.wb-pt-handle, .wb-pt-confirm').forEach(function(n) { n.remove(); });
      var corners = [
        { name: 'nw', x: box.x, y: box.y, cur: 'nwse-resize' },
        { name: 'ne', x: box.x + box.w, y: box.y, cur: 'nesw-resize' },
        { name: 'sw', x: box.x, y: box.y + box.h, cur: 'nesw-resize' },
        { name: 'se', x: box.x + box.w, y: box.y + box.h, cur: 'nwse-resize' }
      ];
      corners.forEach(function(cn) {
        var h = document.createElement('div');
        h.className = 'wb-handle wb-pt-handle';
        h.dataset.corner = cn.name;
        h.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:' + cn.x + 'px;top:' + cn.y + 'px;pointer-events:auto;cursor:' + cn.cur + ';z-index:9;';
        var dragging = false, sx = 0, sy = 0, ob = null;
        h.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(ev) {
          dragging = true;
          h.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId);
          sx = ev.clientX; sy = ev.clientY;
          ob = { x: box.x, y: box.y, w: box.w, h: box.h };
          ev.preventDefault(); ev.stopPropagation();
        });
        h.addEventListener('pointermove', function(ev) {
          if (!dragging) return;
          var rect = overlay.getBoundingClientRect();
          var dx = ev.clientX - sx, dy = ev.clientY - sy;
          var minW = 160, minH = 100;
          if (cn.name === 'se') {
            box.w = Math.max(minW, ob.w + dx);
            box.h = Math.max(minH, ob.h + dy);
          } else if (cn.name === 'sw') {
            box.w = Math.max(minW, ob.w - dx);
            box.h = Math.max(minH, ob.h + dy);
            box.x = ob.x + (ob.w - box.w);
          } else if (cn.name === 'ne') {
            box.w = Math.max(minW, ob.w + dx);
            box.h = Math.max(minH, ob.h - dy);
            box.y = ob.y + (ob.h - box.h);
          } else {
            box.w = Math.max(minW, ob.w - dx);
            box.h = Math.max(minH, ob.h - dy);
            box.x = ob.x + (ob.w - box.w);
            box.y = ob.y + (ob.h - box.h);
          }
          layout();
        });
        h.addEventListener('pointerup', function() { dragging = false; });
        overlay.appendChild(h);
      });
      var conf = document.createElement('button');
      conf.className = 'wb-confirm wb-pt-confirm';
      conf.textContent = '✓ ثبت';
      conf.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:' + (box.x + box.w + 8) + 'px;top:' + box.y + 'px;pointer-events:auto;z-index:10;';
      conf.onclick = function(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        commit();
      };
      overlay.appendChild(conf);
    }

    function commit() {
      snapshot();
      // draw into main canvas at box position
      var dpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
      var tmp = document.createElement('canvas');
      tmp.width = Math.floor(box.w * dpr);
      tmp.height = Math.floor(box.h * dpr);
      var tc = tmp.getContext('2d');
      tc.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
      drawTableTo(tc, box.w, box.h, box.dark);
      ctx.drawImage(tmp, 0, 0, tmp.width, tmp.height, box.x, box.y, box.w, box.h);
      overlay.innerHTML = '';
      overlay.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
      wb.editingPtable = false;
      wb.finishPtable = null;
      showToast('جدول تناوبی ثبت شد');
    }

    // drag move whole box
    var moving = false, msx = 0, msy = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0;
    wrap.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(ev) {
      if (ev.target !== wrap && ev.target !== cnv) return;
      moving = true;
      wrap.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId);
      msx = ev.clientX; msy = ev.clientY;
      ox = box.x; oy = box.y;
      ev.preventDefault();
    });
    wrap.addEventListener('pointermove', function(ev) {
      if (!moving) return;
      box.x = ox + (ev.clientX - msx);
      box.y = oy + (ev.clientY - msy);
      layout();
    });
    wrap.addEventListener('pointerup', function() { moving = false; });

    overlay.appendChild(wrap);
    wb.editingPtable = true;
    wb.finishPtable = commit;
    layout();
    showToast('جابه‌جا و تغییر اندازه کنید، بعد ✓ یا کلیک بیرون');
  }


  function niceStep(raw) {
    // pick 1/2/5 * 10^n closest to raw
    if (raw <= 0 || !isFinite(raw)) return 1;
    var exp = Math.floor(Math.log(raw) / Math.LN10);
    var frac = raw / Math.pow(10, exp);
    var nice;
    if (frac < 1.5) nice = 1;
    else if (frac < 3.5) nice = 2;
    else if (frac < 7.5) nice = 5;
    else nice = 10;
    return nice * Math.pow(10, exp);
  }

  function formatAxisLabel(v) {
    if (Math.abs(v) < 1e-10) return '0';
    var a = Math.abs(v);
    if (a >= 1000) return String(Math.round(v));
    if (a >= 10) return (Math.round(v * 10) / 10).toString();
    if (a >= 1) return (Math.round(v * 100) / 100).toString();
    if (a >= 0.1) return (Math.round(v * 100) / 100).toString();
    if (a >= 0.01) return (Math.round(v * 100) / 100).toString();
    return (Math.round(v * 1000) / 1000).toString();
  }

  function ensureAxesCanvas() {
    var ac = document.getElementById('wbAxesCanvas');
    if (!ac) return null;
    var r = workspace.getBoundingClientRect();
    var dpr = wb.renderScale || Math.min(3, Math.max(2, window.devicePixelRatio || 1));
    if (ac.width !== Math.floor(r.width * dpr) || ac.height !== Math.floor(r.height * dpr)) {
      ac.width = Math.max(1, Math.floor(r.width * dpr));
      ac.height = Math.max(1, Math.floor(r.height * dpr));
      ac.style.width = r.width + 'px';
      ac.style.height = r.height + 'px';
    }
    return ac;
  }

  function redrawLiveAxes() {
    var ac = ensureAxesCanvas();
    if (!ac) return;
    var actx = ac.getContext('2d');
    var dpr = wb.renderScale || 2;
    actx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    var W = workspace.clientWidth;
    var H = workspace.clientHeight;
    actx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
    if (!wb.showAxes) return;

    // world units: 1 unit = 40 CSS px at scale 1
    var unitPx = 40;
    var scale = wb.scale || 1;
    var panX = wb.panX || 0;
    var panY = wb.panY || 0;

    // origin on screen
    var ox = panX + (W / 2) * 0; // we'll place origin at center of content space
    // world (0,0) maps to screen: pan + center * scale... 
    // Drawing coords on main canvas are CSS pixels without pan in bitmap.
    // View is CSS transform on main canvas: translate(pan) scale(scale)
    // Axes overlay should match the same transform visually.
    // Content point (cx, cy) on main bitmap appears at: pan + (cx,cy)*scale on workspace.
    // We put mathematical origin at center of canvas content:
    var originContentX = canvas.clientWidth / 2;
    var originContentY = canvas.clientHeight / 2;
    var originScreenX = panX + originContentX * scale;
    var originScreenY = panY + originContentY * scale;

    // visible world range in "axis units" (1 unit = unitPx content pixels)
    // content pixel relative to origin -> world = contentDelta / unitPx
    // screen = pan + content * scale  => content = (screen - pan) / scale
    function screenToWorldX(sx) {
      var contentX = (sx - panX) / scale;
      return (contentX - originContentX) / unitPx;
    }
    function screenToWorldY(sy) {
      var contentY = (sy - panY) / scale;
      return -((contentY - originContentY) / unitPx); // y up
    }
    function worldToScreenX(wx) {
      return panX + (originContentX + wx * unitPx) * scale;
    }
    function worldToScreenY(wy) {
      return panY + (originContentY - wy * unitPx) * scale;
    }

    var xMin = screenToWorldX(0);
    var xMax = screenToWorldX(W);
    var yMin = screenToWorldY(H); // bottom is smaller y in math if y up... screenToWorldY(H) is more negative
    var yMax = screenToWorldY(0);

    // major step: aim ~60-90px between labels on screen
    var targetPx = 70;
    var worldPerScreenPxX = Math.abs(xMax - xMin) / W;
    var rawStep = worldPerScreenPxX * targetPx;
    var step = niceStep(rawStep);
    // clamp extremes: allow 0.01 when zoomed, 5+ when zoomed out
    if (step < 0.01) step = 0.01;
    if (step > 50) step = niceStep(step);

    // minor subdivisions
    var minorEvery = 5;
    if (step <= 0.01) minorEvery = 2;
    else if (step <= 0.1) minorEvery = 5;
    else if (step >= 5) minorEvery = 5;
    var minorStep = step / minorEvery;

    var color = wb.color || '#334155';
    actx.save();
    actx.strokeStyle = color;
    actx.fillStyle = color;
    actx.lineWidth = 1.5;

    // grid minor
    actx.globalAlpha = 0.12;
    actx.lineWidth = 1;
    var mix0 = Math.floor(xMin / minorStep);
    var mix1 = Math.ceil(xMax / minorStep);
    var miy0 = Math.floor(yMin / minorStep);
    var miy1 = Math.ceil(yMax / minorStep);
    for (var mix = mix0; mix <= mix1; mix++) {
      var sx = worldToScreenX(mix * minorStep);
      actx.beginPath(); actx.moveTo(sx, 0); actx.lineTo(sx, H); actx.stroke();
    }
    for (var miy = miy0; miy <= miy1; miy++) {
      var sy = worldToScreenY(miy * minorStep);
      actx.beginPath(); actx.moveTo(0, sy); actx.lineTo(W, sy); actx.stroke();
    }

    // major grid (integer indices → stable at 0.01)
    actx.globalAlpha = 0.22;
    var ix0 = Math.floor(xMin / step);
    var ix1 = Math.ceil(xMax / step);
    var iy0 = Math.floor(yMin / step);
    var iy1 = Math.ceil(yMax / step);
    for (var ix = ix0; ix <= ix1; ix++) {
      var sx2 = worldToScreenX(ix * step);
      actx.beginPath(); actx.moveTo(sx2, 0); actx.lineTo(sx2, H); actx.stroke();
    }
    for (var iy = iy0; iy <= iy1; iy++) {
      var sy2 = worldToScreenY(iy * step);
      actx.beginPath(); actx.moveTo(0, sy2); actx.lineTo(W, sy2); actx.stroke();
    }

    // main axes
    actx.globalAlpha = 1;
    actx.lineWidth = 2;
    var axisY = worldToScreenY(0);
    var axisX = worldToScreenX(0);
    actx.beginPath();
    actx.moveTo(0, axisY); actx.lineTo(W, axisY);
    actx.moveTo(axisX, 0); actx.lineTo(axisX, H);
    actx.stroke();

    // arrows
    actx.beginPath();
    actx.moveTo(W - 6, axisY); actx.lineTo(W - 16, axisY - 5); actx.lineTo(W - 16, axisY + 5); actx.closePath(); actx.fill();
    actx.beginPath();
    actx.moveTo(axisX, 6); actx.lineTo(axisX - 5, 16); actx.lineTo(axisX + 5, 16); actx.closePath(); actx.fill();

    // labels
    actx.font = '12px Tahoma';
    actx.textAlign = 'center';
    actx.textBaseline = 'top';
    for (var ix = ix0; ix <= ix1; ix++) {
      var lx = ix * step;
      if (ix === 0) continue;
      var lsx = worldToScreenX(lx);
      actx.beginPath(); actx.moveTo(lsx, axisY - 5); actx.lineTo(lsx, axisY + 5); actx.stroke();
      actx.fillText(formatAxisLabel(lx), lsx, axisY + 7);
    }
    actx.textAlign = 'right';
    actx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    for (var iy = iy0; iy <= iy1; iy++) {
      var ly = iy * step;
      if (iy === 0) continue;
      var lsy = worldToScreenY(ly);
      actx.beginPath(); actx.moveTo(axisX - 5, lsy); actx.lineTo(axisX + 5, lsy); actx.stroke();
      actx.fillText(formatAxisLabel(ly), axisX - 8, lsy);
    }

    actx.textAlign = 'left';
    actx.font = '13px Tahoma';
    actx.fillText('x', W - 22, axisY - 14);
    actx.fillText('y', axisX + 10, 18);
    actx.font = '11px Tahoma';
    actx.fillStyle = 'rgba(100,116,139,0.95)';
    actx.fillText('گام: ' + formatAxisLabel(step) + '  |  زوم ' + Math.round(scale * 100) + '%', 10, H - 14);
    actx.restore();
  }
  wb.redrawAxes = redrawLiveAxes;

  function drawCoordinateAxes() {
    wb.showAxes = true;
    redrawLiveAxes();
    showToast('محور مختصات — با زوم دقت عوض می‌شود');
  }


  function doWbSave() {
    if (!wb || !wb.canvas) return;
    var name = wb.currentName || '';
    name = prompt('نام فایل وایت‌برد:', name || ('تخته-' + new Date().toLocaleDateString('fa-IR')));
    if (!name) return;
    name = String(name).trim();
    if (!name) return;
    var store = wbLoadStore();
    if (!store.boards[name] && Object.keys(store.boards).length >= WB_MAX) {
      showToast('ظرفیت پر است');
      return;
    }
    try {
      var dataUrl = wb.canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.88);
      wb._savedData = dataUrl;
      var prevFolder = (store.boards[name] && store.boards[name].folderId !== undefined)
        ? store.boards[name].folderId
        : wbCurrentFolderId;
      store.boards[name] = {
        data: dataUrl,
        savedAt: Date.now(),
        boardStyle: wb.boardStyle || 'white',
        folderId: prevFolder
      };
      wbSaveStore(store);
      wb.currentName = name;
      var t = document.getElementById('wbEditorTitle');
      if (t) t.textContent = name;
      showToast('ذخیره شد: ' + name);
    } catch (e) {
      showToast('خطا در ذخیره — حافظه پر است؟');
    }
  }
  var saveBtn = document.getElementById('wbSaveBtn');
  if (saveBtn) {
    saveBtn.onclick = function(e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      doWbSave();
    };
  }
  wb.doSave = doWbSave;

  // drag toolbar — مختصات نسبت به والد موقعیت‌دار
  const toolbar = document.getElementById('wbToolbar');
  const handle = document.getElementById('wbDragHandle');
  var dragTb = false, ox = 0, oy = 0;
  function getTbScale() {
    try {
      return (parseInt(localStorage.getItem('wbToolbarScale') || '100', 10) || 100) / 100;
    } catch (err) { return 1; }
  }
  function applyTbPos(left, top) {
    if (!toolbar) return;
    toolbar.style.left = left + 'px';
    toolbar.style.top = top + 'px';
    toolbar.style.right = 'auto';
    toolbar.style.bottom = 'auto';
    toolbar.style.transformOrigin = 'top left';
    toolbar.style.transform = 'scale(' + getTbScale() + ')';
  }
  function onTbPointerDown(e) {
    if (!toolbar || !handle) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    dragTb = true;
    try { handle.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
    var parent = toolbar.offsetParent || toolbar.parentElement || document.body;
    var pr = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var r = toolbar.getBoundingClientRect();
    ox = e.clientX - r.left;
    oy = e.clientY - r.top;
    applyTbPos(r.left - pr.left, r.top - pr.top);
  }
  function onTbPointerMove(e) {
    if (!dragTb || !toolbar) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    var parent = toolbar.offsetParent || toolbar.parentElement || document.body;
    var pr = parent.getBoundingClientRect();
    var left = e.clientX - pr.left - ox;
    var top = e.clientY - pr.top - oy;
    applyTbPos(left, top);
  }
  function onTbPointerUp(e) {
    if (!dragTb) return;
    dragTb = false;
    try { if (e && handle) handle.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
  }
  if (handle && toolbar && !handle.dataset.dragBound) {
    handle.dataset.dragBound = '1';
    handle.addEventListener('pointerdown', onTbPointerDown);
    window.addEventListener('pointermove', onTbPointerMove);
    window.addEventListener('pointerup', onTbPointerUp);
    window.addEventListener('pointercancel', onTbPointerUp);
  }

  // کلیک بیرون از پنل تنظیمات گجت → بستن
  if (!window._wbOutsideCloseBound) {
    window._wbOutsideCloseBound = true;
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e) {
      var t = e.target;
      if (!t) return;
      // داخل نوار ابزار یا پنل‌های باز یا منو → نادیده
      if (t.closest && (
        t.closest('#wbToolbar') ||
        t.closest('.wb-pop') ||
        t.closest('#wbMenuDrop') ||
        t.closest('#wbLobbyMenu') ||
        t.closest('.wb-menu-drop')
      )) return;
      if (typeof hidePops === 'function') hidePops();
      else if (wb && wb.hidePops) wb.hidePops();
      // منوی لابی
      var lm = document.getElementById('wbLobbyMenu');
      if (lm) lm.style.display = 'none';
    }, true);
  }

  snapshot();
  wbPaintBackground();
}

function wbNewBoard() {
  const md = document.getElementById('wbMenuDrop');
  if (md) md.style.display = 'none';
  showWbEditor('');
  setTimeout(function() {
    if (wb && wb.paintBg) {
      wb.snapshot();
      wb.paintBg();
    }
    const t = document.getElementById('wbEditorTitle');
    if (t) t.textContent = 'تخته جدید';
    if (wb) wb.currentName = '';
  }, 80);
  showToast('تخته جدید');
}

function wbShowBoards() {
  wbBackToLobby();
}

function wbCloseMenu() {
  var md = document.getElementById('wbMenuDrop');
  if (md) md.style.display = 'none';
}

function wbExportPng() {
  if (!wb || !wb.canvas) return;
  try {
    var a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = wb.canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
    a.download = (wb.currentName || 'whiteboard') + '.png';
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    showToast('تصویر PNG ذخیره شد');
  } catch (e) {
    showToast('خطا در ذخیره تصویر');
  }
  wbCloseMenu();
}

function wbExportJpeg() {
  if (!wb || !wb.canvas) return;
  try {
    var a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = wb.canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.92);
    a.download = (wb.currentName || 'whiteboard') + '.jpg';
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    showToast('تصویر JPEG ذخیره شد');
  } catch (e) {
    showToast('خطا در ذخیره تصویر');
  }
  wbCloseMenu();
}

function wbExportPdfClean() {
  if (!wb || !wb.canvas) return;
  try {
    var src = wb.canvas;
    var w = src.width, h = src.height;
    var out = document.createElement('canvas');
    out.width = w; out.height = h;
    var ctx = out.getContext('2d');
    // پس‌زمینه سفید تمیز برای چاپ
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);
    ctx.drawImage(src, 0, 0);
    var data = out.toDataURL('image/png');
    var title = (wb.currentName || 'whiteboard').replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*]/g, '_');
    var htmlDoc = '<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="fa" dir="rtl"><head><meta charset="UTF-8"><title>' + title + '</title>' +
      '<style>@page{margin:12mm}body{margin:0;background:#fff;display:flex;justify-content:center}' +
      'img{max-width:100%;height:auto;page-break-inside:avoid}</style></head><body>' +
      '<img src="' + data + '" alt="whiteboard"/></body></html>';
    var win = window.open('', '_blank');
    if (!win) {
      // fallback download PNG clean
      var a = document.createElement('a');
      a.href = data; a.download = title + '_print.png';
      document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
      showToast('پنجره مسدود بود — PNG تمیز ذخیره شد');
      wbCloseMenu();
      return;
    }
    win.document.write(htmlDoc);
    win.document.close();
    setTimeout(function() {
      try { win.focus(); win.print(); } catch (e) {}
    }, 400);
    showToast('پیش‌نمایش چاپ / PDF باز شد');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(e);
    showToast('خطا در خروجی PDF');
  }
  wbCloseMenu();
}


/* ——— اندازه تخته: A4 / هوشمند / دستی ——— */
function wbGetSizeModeLabel() {
  var mode = 'smart';
  try { mode = localStorage.getItem('wbBoardSizeMode') || 'smart'; } catch (e) {}
  if (mode === 'a4') return 'A4 عمودی';
  if (mode === 'a4-landscape') return 'A4 افقی';
  if (mode === 'manual') {
    var w = 0, h = 0;
    try {
      w = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('wbBoardCustomW') || '0', 10) || 0;
      h = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('wbBoardCustomH') || '0', 10) || 0;
    } catch (e2) {}
    return 'دستی ' + w + '×' + h;
  }
  return 'تخته هوشمند';
}

function wbSetBoardSize() {
  wbCloseMenu();
  var cur = wbGetSizeModeLabel();
  var choice = prompt(
    'اندازه تخته را انتخاب کنید:\n' +
    '۱ = A4 عمودی (مناسب چاپ)\n' +
    '۲ = A4 افقی\n' +
    '۳ = تخته هوشمند (سایز فعلی / تمام‌صفحه)\n' +
    '۴ = دستی (عرض و ارتفاع به پیکسل)\n\n' +
    'حالت فعلی: ' + cur + '\nعدد ۱ تا ۴ را وارد کنید:',
    '3'
  );
  if (choice === null) return;
  choice = String(choice).trim();
  var mode = 'smart';
  if (choice === '1') mode = 'a4';
  else if (choice === '2') mode = 'a4-landscape';
  else if (choice === '3') mode = 'smart';
  else if (choice === '4') {
    mode = 'manual';
    var defW = 1600, defH = 900;
    try {
      defW = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('wbBoardCustomW') || '1600', 10) || 1600;
      defH = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('wbBoardCustomH') || '900', 10) || 900;
    } catch (e) {}
    var ww = prompt('عرض تخته به پیکسل (۲۰۰ تا ۴۲۰۰):', String(defW));
    if (ww === null) return;
    var hh = prompt('ارتفاع تخته به پیکسل (۲۰۰ تا ۴۲۰۰):', String(defH));
    if (hh === null) return;
    var nw = parseInt(ww, 10);
    var nh = parseInt(hh, 10);
    if (isNaN(nw) || isNaN(nh) || nw < 200 || nh < 200 || nw > 4200 || nh > 4200) {
      showToast('ابعاد نامعتبر — بین ۲۰۰ تا ۴۲۰۰ وارد کنید');
      return;
    }
    try {
      localStorage.setItem('wbBoardCustomW', String(nw));
      localStorage.setItem('wbBoardCustomH', String(nh));
    } catch (e3) {}
  } else {
    showToast('عدد ۱ تا ۴ وارد کنید');
    return;
  }
  try { localStorage.setItem('wbBoardSizeMode', mode); } catch (e4) {}
  if (wb && wb.resize) {
    // محتوا حفظ می‌شود چون resize خودش prev را نگه می‌دارد
    wb._lastCssW = 0; // اجبار تغییر اندازه
    wb._lastCssH = 0;
    wb.resize();
  }
  showToast('اندازه تخته: ' + wbGetSizeModeLabel());
}

/* ——— لایه شناور تصویر: جابه‌جایی + تغییر اندازه ——— */
function wbClearImgFloat() {
  var ov = document.getElementById('wbShapeOverlay');
  if (!ov) return;
  ov.querySelectorAll('.wb-img-float').forEach(function(n) { n.remove(); });
  if (wb) wb._imgFloat = null;
}

function wbStampImgFloat() {
  if (!wb || !wb._imgFloat || !wb.canvas) return;
  var f = wb._imgFloat;
  var img = new Image();
  img.onload = function() {
    if (typeof wb.snapshot === 'function') wb.snapshot();
    try {
      wb.ctx.drawImage(img, f.x, f.y, f.w, f.h);
      // ثبت به‌عنوان تصویر محافظت‌شده در برابر پاک‌کن
      if (!wb.protectedImages) wb.protectedImages = [];
      wb.protectedImages.push({
        dataUrl: f.dataUrl,
        x: f.x,
        y: f.y,
        w: f.w,
        h: f.h
      });
      showToast('تصویر ثبت شد (پاک‌کن پاکش نمی‌کند)');
    } catch (e) {
      showToast('خطا در ثبت تصویر');
    }
    wbClearImgFloat();
  };
  img.onerror = function() { showToast('خطا در ثبت تصویر'); };
  img.src = f.dataUrl;
}


function wbPlaceFloatingImage(dataUrl, natW, natH) {
  if (!wb || !wb.canvas) return;
  wbClearImgFloat();
  var ov = document.getElementById('wbShapeOverlay');
  if (!ov) return;
  var cw = wb.canvas.clientWidth || 800;
  var ch = wb.canvas.clientHeight || 600;
  // شروع با حداکثر ۸۵٪ تخته، حفظ نسبت
  var fit = Math.min((cw * 0.85) / natW, (ch * 0.85) / natH, 1);
  var w = Math.max(40, Math.round(natW * fit));
  var h = Math.max(40, Math.round(natH * fit));
  var x = Math.round((cw - w) / 2);
  var y = Math.round((ch - h) / 2);
  var aspect = natW / natH;

  var state = { dataUrl: dataUrl, x: x, y: y, w: w, h: h, aspect: aspect, natW: natW, natH: natH };
  wb._imgFloat = state;

  var wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.className = 'wb-img-float';
  wrap.style.left = x + 'px';
  wrap.style.top = y + 'px';
  wrap.style.width = w + 'px';
  wrap.style.height = h + 'px';

  var imEl = document.createElement('img');
  imEl.src = dataUrl;
  imEl.alt = '';
  wrap.appendChild(imEl);

  var badge = document.createElement('div');
  badge.className = 'wb-size-badge';
  function updateBadge() {
    badge.textContent = Math.round(state.w) + ' × ' + Math.round(state.h) + ' px';
  }
  updateBadge();
  wrap.appendChild(badge);

  var acts = document.createElement('div');
  acts.className = 'wb-sel-actions';
  var bOk = document.createElement('button');
  bOk.className = 'ok';
  bOk.textContent = '✓ ثبت';
  bOk.onclick = function(e) { e.stopPropagation(); wbStampImgFloat(); };
  var bCancel = document.createElement('button');
  bCancel.className = 'danger';
  bCancel.textContent = '✕ لغو';
  bCancel.onclick = function(e) { e.stopPropagation(); wbClearImgFloat(); showToast('لغو شد'); };
  // دکمه‌های سریع اندازه
  var bBig = document.createElement('button');
  bBig.textContent = '＋';
  bBig.title = 'بزرگ‌تر';
  bBig.onclick = function(e) {
    e.stopPropagation();
    var nw = Math.min(cw * 1.5, state.w * 1.15);
    var nh = nw / state.aspect;
    state.x -= (nw - state.w) / 2;
    state.y -= (nh - state.h) / 2;
    state.w = nw; state.h = nh;
    applyPos();
  };
  var bSmall = document.createElement('button');
  bSmall.textContent = '－';
  bSmall.title = 'کوچک‌تر';
  bSmall.onclick = function(e) {
    e.stopPropagation();
    var nw = Math.max(40, state.w * 0.87);
    var nh = nw / state.aspect;
    state.x -= (nw - state.w) / 2;
    state.y -= (nh - state.h) / 2;
    state.w = nw; state.h = nh;
    applyPos();
  };
  acts.appendChild(bSmall);
  acts.appendChild(bBig);
  acts.appendChild(bOk);
  acts.appendChild(bCancel);
  wrap.appendChild(acts);

  function applyPos() {
    state.w = Math.max(40, state.w);
    state.h = Math.max(40, state.h);
    wrap.style.left = state.x + 'px';
    wrap.style.top = state.y + 'px';
    wrap.style.width = state.w + 'px';
    wrap.style.height = state.h + 'px';
    updateBadge();
  }

  // ۸ دستگیره تغییر اندازه
  var handles = ['nw', 'n', 'ne', 'e', 'se', 's', 'sw', 'w'];
  handles.forEach(function(pos) {
    var rh = document.createElement('div');
    rh.className = 'wb-rh ' + pos;
    rh.dataset.handle = pos;
    wrap.appendChild(rh);
  });

  // جابه‌جایی
  var dragging = false, resizing = false, handle = null;
  var startSX = 0, startSY = 0, ox = 0, oy = 0, ow = 0, oh = 0;
  var keepAspect = true; // پیش‌فرض حفظ نسبت — با Shift آزاد

  wrap.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(ev) {
    if (ev.target.closest && ev.target.closest('.wb-sel-actions')) return;
    var hEl = ev.target.closest && ev.target.closest('.wb-rh');
    startSX = ev.clientX;
    startSY = ev.clientY;
    ox = state.x; oy = state.y; ow = state.w; oh = state.h;
    keepAspect = !ev.shiftKey;
    if (hEl) {
      resizing = true;
      handle = hEl.dataset.handle;
    } else {
      dragging = true;
    }
    try { wrap.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId); } catch (e) {}
    ev.preventDefault();
    ev.stopPropagation();
  });

  wrap.addEventListener('pointermove', function(ev) {
    if (!dragging && !resizing) return;
    var sc = (wb && wb.scale) ? wb.scale : 1;
    var dx = (ev.clientX - startSX) / sc;
    var dy = (ev.clientY - startSY) / sc;
    if (dragging) {
      state.x = ox + dx;
      state.y = oy + dy;
      applyPos();
      return;
    }
    // resize
    var nx = ox, ny = oy, nw = ow, nh = oh;
    var h = handle;
    if (h.indexOf('e') >= 0) nw = ow + dx;
    if (h.indexOf('w') >= 0) { nw = ow - dx; nx = ox + dx; }
    if (h.indexOf('s') >= 0) nh = oh + dy;
    if (h.indexOf('n') >= 0) { nh = oh - dy; ny = oy + dy; }

    // فقط گوشه‌ها یا با حفظ نسبت
    var isCorner = (h === 'nw' || h === 'ne' || h === 'sw' || h === 'se');
    var isEdge = !isCorner;
    if (keepAspect || isCorner) {
      if (h === 'n' || h === 's') {
        nw = nh * state.aspect;
        nx = ox + (ow - nw) / 2;
      } else if (h === 'e' || h === 'w') {
        nh = nw / state.aspect;
        ny = oy + (oh - nh) / 2;
      } else {
        // گوشه: بر اساس محور غالب
        if (Math.abs(dx) * state.aspect > Math.abs(dy)) {
          nh = nw / state.aspect;
          if (h.indexOf('n') >= 0) ny = oy + (oh - nh);
        } else {
          nw = nh * state.aspect;
          if (h.indexOf('w') >= 0) nx = ox + (ow - nw);
        }
      }
    }

    if (nw < 40) { nw = 40; if (h.indexOf('w') >= 0) nx = ox + ow - 40; }
    if (nh < 40) { nh = 40; if (h.indexOf('n') >= 0) ny = oy + oh - 40; }
    state.x = nx; state.y = ny; state.w = nw; state.h = nh;
    applyPos();
  });

  function endPtr() { dragging = false; resizing = false; handle = null; }
  wrap.addEventListener('pointerup', endPtr);
  wrap.addEventListener('pointercancel', endPtr);

  // دابل‌کلیک = ثبت
  wrap.addEventListener('dblclick', function(e) {
    e.stopPropagation();
    wbStampImgFloat();
  });

  ov.appendChild(wrap);
  showToast('جابه‌جا کنید · گوشه‌ها را بکشید · ＋/－ · سپس «ثبت»');
}

/* ——— وارد کردن تصویر ——— */
function wbImportImage() {
  wbCloseMenu();
  if (!wb || !wb.canvas) {
    showToast('ابتدا تخته را باز کنید');
    return;
  }
  var inp = document.getElementById('wbImportImageInput');
  if (!inp) return;
  inp.value = '';
  inp.onchange = function() {
    var file = inp.files && inp.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    if (!file.type || file.type.indexOf('image/') !== 0) {
      showToast('فایل باید تصویر باشد');
      return;
    }
    var reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = function(ev) {
      var img = new Image();
      img.onload = function() {
        wbPlaceFloatingImage(ev.target.result, img.naturalWidth || img.width, img.naturalHeight || img.height);
      };
      img.onerror = function() { showToast('بارگذاری تصویر ناموفق بود'); };
      img.src = ev.target.result;
    };
    reader.onerror = function() { showToast('خواندن فایل ناموفق بود'); };
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  };
  inp.click();
}


/* ——— وارد کردن PDF (pdf.js از CDN — چند منبع پشتیبان) ——— */
var _wbPdfJsLoading = null;
var _wbPdfSession = {}; // id -> doc (اگر localStorage پر باشد، برای همین جلسه نگه می‌داریم)
function wbLoadPdfJs() {
  if (window.pdfjsLib) {
    try {
      window.pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc =
        window.pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc ||
        'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js';
    } catch (e) {}
    return Promise.resolve(window.pdfjsLib);
  }
  if (_wbPdfJsLoading) return _wbPdfJsLoading;
  var urls = [
    'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.min.js',
    'https://unpkg.com/pdfjs-dist@3.11.174/build/pdf.min.js',
    'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/pdfjs-dist@3.11.174/build/pdf.min.js'
  ];
  var workers = [
    'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js',
    'https://unpkg.com/pdfjs-dist@3.11.174/build/pdf.worker.min.js',
    'https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/pdfjs-dist@3.11.174/build/pdf.worker.min.js'
  ];
  _wbPdfJsLoading = new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {
    var i = 0;
    function tryNext() {
      if (i >= urls.length) {
        _wbPdfJsLoading = null;
        reject(new Error('network'));
        return;
      }
      var idx = i++;
      var s = document.createElement('script');
      s.src = urls[idx];
      s.onload = function() {
        if (window.pdfjsLib) {
          try {
