            window.pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = workers[idx];
          } catch (e) {}
          resolve(window.pdfjsLib);
        } else {
          tryNext();
        }
      };
      s.onerror = function() { tryNext(); };
      document.head.appendChild(s);
    }
    tryNext();
  });
  return _wbPdfJsLoading;
}
function wbGetPdfDoc(id) {
  if (_wbPdfSession[id] && _wbPdfSession[id].pages && _wbPdfSession[id].pages.length) {
    return _wbPdfSession[id];
  }
  var store = wbLoadStore();
  return (store.pdfs && store.pdfs[id]) || null;
}
function wbTrySavePdfs(store) {
  try {
    wbSaveStore(store);
    return true;
  } catch (e) {
    return false;
  }
}



function wbImportPdf() {
  wbCloseMenu();
  if (!wb || !wb.canvas) {
    showToast('ابتدا تخته را باز کنید');
    return;
  }
  var inp = document.getElementById('wbImportPdfInput');
  if (!inp) return;
  inp.value = '';
  inp.onchange = function() {
    var file = inp.files && inp.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    showToast('در حال بارگذاری PDF…');
    wbLoadPdfJs().then(function(pdfjsLib) {
      var reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = function(ev) {
        var data = new Uint8Array(ev.target.result);
        pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: data }).promise.then(function(pdf) {
          var total = pdf.numPages || 1;
          var pageStr = '1';
          if (total > 1) {
            pageStr = prompt('این PDF دارای ' + total + ' صفحه است.\nشماره صفحه را وارد کنید (۱ تا ' + total + '):', '1') || '1';
          }
          var pageNum = parseInt(pageStr, 10) || 1;
          if (pageNum < 1) pageNum = 1;
          if (pageNum > total) pageNum = total;
          return pdf.getPage(pageNum).then(function(page) {
            var cw = wb.canvas.clientWidth || 800;
            var ch = wb.canvas.clientHeight || 600;
            var base = page.getViewport({ scale: 1 });
            var scale = Math.min(cw / base.width, ch / base.height) * 1.5;
            // محدود کردن رزولوشن برای جلوگیری از کرش
            if (base.width * scale > 3000) scale = 3000 / base.width;
            if (base.height * scale > 3000) scale = 3000 / base.height;
            var viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: scale });
            var off = document.createElement('canvas');
            off.width = Math.floor(viewport.width);
            off.height = Math.floor(viewport.height);
            var octx = off.getContext('2d');
            return page.render({ canvasContext: octx, viewport: viewport }).promise.then(function() {
              var dataUrl = off.toDataURL('image/png');
              wbPlaceFloatingImage(dataUrl, off.width, off.height);
              showToast('صفحه ' + pageNum + ' آماده — اندازه را تنظیم کنید سپس ثبت');
            });

          });
        }).catch(function(err) {
          console.error(err);
          showToast('خطا در خواندن PDF');
        });
      };
      reader.onerror = function() { showToast('خواندن فایل ناموفق بود'); };
      reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
    }).catch(function() {
      showToast('برای وارد کردن PDF به اینترنت نیاز است (بار اول)');
    });
  };
  inp.click();
}

function wbSetToolbarSize() {

  var md = document.getElementById('wbMenuDrop');
  if (md) md.style.display = 'none';
  var cur = 100;
  try {
    cur = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('wbToolbarScale') || '100', 10) || 100;
  } catch (e) {}
  var v = prompt('اندازه نوار ابزار را وارد کنید (۵۰ تا ۲۰۰ درصد):', String(cur));
  if (v === null) return;
  var n = parseInt(v, 10);
  if (isNaN(n) || n < 50 || n > 200) {
    showToast('عدد بین ۵۰ تا ۲۰۰ وارد کنید');
    return;
  }
  try { localStorage.setItem('wbToolbarScale', String(n)); } catch (e) {}
  applyWbToolbarSize(n);
  showToast('اندازه نوار: ' + n + '%');
}

function applyWbToolbarSize(n) {
  n = n || 100;
  var tb = document.getElementById('wbToolbar');
  if (!tb) return;
  var s = n / 100;
  tb.style.transform = 'scale(' + s + ')';
  // keep position stable — scale from bottom center if not dragged
  if (!tb.style.left || tb.style.left === '') {
    tb.style.transformOrigin = 'center bottom';
  } else {
    tb.style.transformOrigin = 'top left';
  }
  // also scale button sizes via font/css variable
  tb.style.setProperty('--wb-tb-scale', String(s));
  var btns = tb.querySelectorAll('.wb-tool, .wb-icon-btn, .wb-swatch');
  var base = Math.round(38 * s);
  btns.forEach(function(b) {
    if (b.classList.contains('wb-swatch')) {
      b.style.width = Math.round(28 * s) + 'px';
      b.style.height = Math.round(28 * s) + 'px';
    } else {
      b.style.width = base + 'px';
      b.style.height = base + 'px';
      b.style.fontSize = Math.round(16 * s) + 'px';
    }
  });
  var range = document.getElementById('wbSize');
  if (range) range.style.width = Math.round(80 * s) + 'px';
  var color = document.getElementById('wbColor');
  if (color) {
    color.style.width = Math.round(36 * s) + 'px';
    color.style.height = Math.round(36 * s) + 'px';
  }
  tb.style.padding = Math.round(8 * s) + 'px ' + Math.round(10 * s) + 'px';
  tb.style.gap = Math.round(6 * s) + 'px';
  tb.style.borderRadius = Math.round(16 * s) + 'px';
}


/* ——— PDF در لابی + اسکرول پیوسته + گجت‌های رسم ——— */
var wbPdfState = {
  id: null,
  name: '',
  total: 0,
  pages: [],
  pageCanvases: [],
  tool: 'marker',
  shape: 'rect',
  fillStyle: 'solid',
  fillTol: 50,
  hlOpacity: 0.30,
  color: '#111827',
  size: 4,
  drawing: false,
  last: null,
  shapeStart: null,
  shapeSnapshot: null,
  activePage: -1,
  history: {},
  redo: {},
  dirty: false
};



function wbShowPdfView() {
  var lobby = document.getElementById('wbLobby');
  var editor = document.getElementById('wbEditor');
  var pdfv = document.getElementById('wbPdfView');
  if (lobby) lobby.style.display = 'none';
  if (editor) editor.style.display = 'none';
  if (pdfv) {
    pdfv.style.display = 'flex';
    pdfv.style.flexDirection = 'column';
    pdfv.style.flex = '1';
    pdfv.style.minHeight = '0';
  }
  wbInitPdfGadgets();
}

function wbHidePdfView() {
  if (wbPdfState.dirty) {
    if (confirm('یادداشت‌های ذخیره‌نشده دارید. ذخیره شود؟')) {
      wbSavePdfDoc();
    }
  }
  var pdfv = document.getElementById('wbPdfView');
  if (pdfv) pdfv.style.display = 'none';
  wbPdfState.id = null;
  wbPdfState.pages = [];
  wbPdfState.pageCanvases = [];
  var pages = document.getElementById('wbPdfPages');
  if (pages) pages.innerHTML = '';
  showWbLobby();
}

function wbPdfHidePops() {
  ['wbPdfShapesPanel', 'wbPdfFillerPanel', 'wbPdfHlPanel', 'wbPdfExtraPanel', 'wbPdfSelectPanel'].forEach(function(id) {
    var el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (el) el.style.display = 'none';
  });
  if (typeof window.wbHideAllPops === 'function') {
    // فقط پنل‌های تخته را هم اگر باز بودند ببند (از hidePops کامل استفاده نکن تا حلقه نشود)
  }
}

function wbPdfTogglePop(id) {
  var el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!el) return;
  var open = el.style.display === 'none' || !el.style.display || el.style.display === '';
  wbPdfHidePops();
  if (typeof window.wbHideAllPops === 'function') window.wbHideAllPops();
  el.style.display = open ? 'flex' : 'none';
}

function wbInitPdfGadgets() {
  var tb = document.getElementById('wbPdfToolbar');
  if (!tb) return;
  if (tb.dataset.bound) return;
  tb.dataset.bound = '1';

  // دقیقاً مثل منطق تخته
  tb.querySelectorAll('[data-pdft]').forEach(function(btn) {
    btn.addEventListener('click', function() {
      var t = btn.getAttribute('data-pdft');
      // کلیک دوباره روی filler/highlighter → باز/بسته پنل
      if (t === 'filler' && wbPdfState.tool === 'filler') {
        wbPdfTogglePop('wbPdfFillerPanel');
        return;
      }
      if (t === 'highlighter' && wbPdfState.tool === 'highlighter') {
        wbPdfTogglePop('wbPdfHlPanel');
        return;
      }
      tb.querySelectorAll('[data-pdft]').forEach(function(b) { b.classList.remove('active'); });
      btn.classList.add('active');
      wbPdfState.tool = t;
      wbPdfHidePops();
      if (typeof window.wbHideAllPops === 'function') window.wbHideAllPops();
      if (t === 'shapes') wbPdfTogglePop('wbPdfShapesPanel');
      if (t === 'extra') wbPdfTogglePop('wbPdfExtraPanel');
      if (t === 'filler') {
        if (!wbPdfState.fillStyle) wbPdfState.fillStyle = 'solid';
        wbPdfTogglePop('wbPdfFillerPanel');
        showToast('سبک: ' + wbPdfState.fillStyle + ' — روی ناحیه بزن');
      }
      if (t === 'highlighter') wbPdfTogglePop('wbPdfHlPanel');
      if (t === 'select') showToast('کادر دور سوژه — روی یادداشت بکشید');
    });
  });

  tb.querySelectorAll('[data-pdfc]').forEach(function(sw) {
    sw.addEventListener('click', function() {
      var c = sw.getAttribute('data-pdfc');
      wbPdfState.color = c;
      var col = document.getElementById('wbPdfColor');
      if (col) col.value = c;
    });
  });
  var col = document.getElementById('wbPdfColor');
  var sz = document.getElementById('wbPdfSize');
  if (col) col.addEventListener('input', function() { wbPdfState.color = col.value; });
  if (sz) sz.addEventListener('input', function() { wbPdfState.size = parseInt(sz.value, 10) || 4; });

  // اشکال — مثل تخته
  var shapesPanel = document.getElementById('wbPdfShapesPanel');
  if (shapesPanel && !shapesPanel.dataset.bound) {
    shapesPanel.dataset.bound = '1';
    shapesPanel.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      var b = e.target.closest('[data-pdfshape]');
      if (!b) return;
      wbPdfState.shape = b.getAttribute('data-pdfshape');
      wbPdfState.tool = 'shapes';
      tb.querySelectorAll('[data-pdft]').forEach(function(x) {
        x.classList.toggle('active', x.getAttribute('data-pdft') === 'shapes');
      });
      if (wbPdfState.shape === 'text') showToast('روی صفحه کلیک کنید و متن بنویسید');
      else showToast('شکل انتخاب شد — بکشید');
      // پنل باز می‌ماند تا شکل بعدی هم انتخاب شود؛ با کلیک بیرون بسته می‌شود
    });
  }

  var fillerPanel = document.getElementById('wbPdfFillerPanel');
  if (fillerPanel && !fillerPanel.dataset.bound) {
    fillerPanel.dataset.bound = '1';
    fillerPanel.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      var b = e.target.closest('[data-pdffill]');
      if (!b) return;
      wbPdfState.fillStyle = b.getAttribute('data-pdffill') || 'solid';
      var panel = document.getElementById('wbPdfFillerPanel');
      if (panel) panel.querySelectorAll('[data-pdffill]').forEach(function(x) {
        x.style.outline = (x === b) ? '2px solid var(--accent)' : '';
      });
      showToast('سبک فیللر: ' + (b.textContent || wbPdfState.fillStyle).trim());
    });
  }

  var ft = document.getElementById('wbPdfFillTol');
  if (ft) ft.addEventListener('input', function() {
    wbPdfState.fillTol = parseInt(ft.value, 10) || 50;
    var lab = document.getElementById('wbPdfFillTolLabel');
    if (lab) lab.textContent = wbPdfState.fillTol + '٪';
  });
  var hl = document.getElementById('wbPdfHlOpacity');
  if (hl) hl.addEventListener('input', function() {
    wbPdfState.hlOpacity = (parseInt(hl.value, 10) || 30) / 100;
    var lab = document.getElementById('wbPdfHlOpacityLabel');
    if (lab) lab.textContent = Math.round(wbPdfState.hlOpacity * 100) + '٪';
  });

  var und = document.getElementById('wbPdfUndo');
  if (und) und.onclick = function() { wbPdfUndoPage(); };
  var red = document.getElementById('wbPdfRedo');
  if (red) red.onclick = function() { wbPdfRedoPage(); };
  var sav = document.getElementById('wbPdfSaveBtn');
  if (sav) sav.onclick = function() { wbSavePdfDoc(); };
  var sav2 = document.getElementById('wbPdfSaveNotes');
  if (sav2) sav2.onclick = function() { wbSavePdfDoc(); wbPdfHidePops(); };
  var clr = document.getElementById('wbPdfClearPage');
  if (clr) clr.onclick = function() { wbPdfClearActivePage(); wbPdfHidePops(); };
  var addB = document.getElementById('wbPdfAddBlank');
  if (addB) addB.onclick = function() {
    var idx = wbPdfState.activePage;
    if (idx < 0) idx = 0;
    wbPdfInsertBlankAfter(idx);
    wbPdfHidePops();
  };

  // درگ نوار — کپی از منطق تخته
  var handle = document.getElementById('wbPdfDragHandle');
  if (handle && !handle.dataset.bound) {
    handle.dataset.bound = '1';
    var dragging = false, ox = 0, oy = 0;
    handle.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(e) {
      dragging = true;
      var r = tb.getBoundingClientRect();
      ox = e.clientX - r.left;
      oy = e.clientY - r.top;
      tb.style.left = r.left + 'px';
      tb.style.bottom = 'auto';
      tb.style.top = r.top + 'px';
      tb.style.transform = 'none';
      try { handle.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
      e.stopPropagation();
    });
    handle.addEventListener('pointermove', function(e) {
      if (!dragging) return;
      tb.style.left = Math.max(0, e.clientX - ox) + 'px';
      tb.style.top = Math.max(0, e.clientY - oy) + 'px';
    });
    handle.addEventListener('pointerup', function() { dragging = false; });
  }
}




/** وارد کردن PDF → ذخیره در لابی → باز کردن با اسکرول و گجت */
/** ذخیره باینری PDF — IndexedDB (اصلی) + OPFS (پشتیبان) */
var _ariyaPdfIdb = null;
function ariyaPdfIdbOpen() {
  if (_ariyaPdfIdb) return Promise.resolve(_ariyaPdfIdb);
  return new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {
    try {
      var req = indexedDB.open('AriyaKhPdfStore', 1);
      req.onupgradeneeded = function() {
        var db = req.result;
        if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('pdfs')) db.createObjectStore('pdfs');
        if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('annots')) db.createObjectStore('annots');
      };
      req.onsuccess = function() {
        _ariyaPdfIdb = req.result;
        resolve(_ariyaPdfIdb);
      };
      req.onerror = function() { reject(req.error); };
    } catch (e) { reject(e); }
  });
}
function ariyaPdfIdbPut(storeName, key, val) {
  return ariyaPdfIdbOpen().then(function(db) {
    return new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {
      try {
        var tx = db.transaction(storeName, 'readwrite');
        tx.objectStore(storeName).put(val, key);
        tx.oncomplete = function() { resolve(true); };
        tx.onerror = function() { reject(tx.error); };
      } catch (e) { reject(e); }
    });
  });
}
function ariyaPdfIdbGet(storeName, key) {
  return ariyaPdfIdbOpen().then(function(db) {
    return new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {
      try {
        var tx = db.transaction(storeName, 'readonly');
        var r = tx.objectStore(storeName).get(key);
        r.onsuccess = function() { resolve(r.result || null); };
        r.onerror = function() { reject(r.error); };
      } catch (e) { reject(e); }
    });
  });
}
async function ariyaSavePdfBinary(pdfId, uint8) {
  var okIdb = false;
  try {
    // ذخیره در IndexedDB (قابل‌اعتمادتر از OPFS روی اکثر مرورگرها)
    var buf = uint8 instanceof ArrayBuffer ? uint8 : (uint8.buffer ? uint8.buffer.slice(uint8.byteOffset, uint8.byteOffset + uint8.byteLength) : uint8);
    await ariyaPdfIdbPut('pdfs', pdfId, buf);
    okIdb = true;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('ariyaSavePdfBinary IDB', e);
  }
  // پشتیبان OPFS (اختیاری)
  try {
    var dir = typeof ariyaActiveHandle === 'function' ? ariyaActiveHandle() : null;
    if (!dir) {
      if (typeof ariyaInitOpfsAuto === 'function') await ariyaInitOpfsAuto();
      dir = typeof ariyaActiveHandle === 'function' ? ariyaActiveHandle() : null;
    }
    if (dir) {
      var pdfsDir = await dir.getDirectoryHandle('pdfs', { create: true });
      var thisDir = await pdfsDir.getDirectoryHandle(pdfId, { create: true });
      var fh = await thisDir.getFileHandle('original.pdf', { create: true });
      var w = await fh.createWritable();
      await w.write(uint8);
      await w.close();
    }
  } catch (e2) {
    console.warn('ariyaSavePdfBinary OPFS', e2);
  }
  return okIdb;
}
async function ariyaLoadPdfBinary(pdfId) {
  // ۱) IndexedDB
  try {
    var data = await ariyaPdfIdbGet('pdfs', pdfId);
    if (data) {
      if (data instanceof ArrayBuffer) return new Uint8Array(data);
      if (data instanceof Uint8Array) return data;
      if (data && data.buffer) return new Uint8Array(data.buffer);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('ariyaLoadPdfBinary IDB', e);
  }
  // ۲) OPFS
  try {
    var dir = typeof ariyaActiveHandle === 'function' ? ariyaActiveHandle() : null;
    if (!dir) {
      if (typeof ariyaInitOpfsAuto === 'function') await ariyaInitOpfsAuto();
      dir = typeof ariyaActiveHandle === 'function' ? ariyaActiveHandle() : null;
    }
    if (dir) {
      var pdfsDir = await dir.getDirectoryHandle('pdfs');
      var thisDir = await pdfsDir.getDirectoryHandle(pdfId);
      var fh = await thisDir.getFileHandle('original.pdf');
      var buf = await (await fh.getFile()).arrayBuffer();
      var u8 = new Uint8Array(buf);
      // مهاجرت به IndexedDB برای دفعات بعد
      try { await ariyaPdfIdbPut('pdfs', pdfId, buf); } catch (mig) {}
      return u8;
    }
  } catch (e2) {}
  return null;
}


async function ariyaSavePdfAnnots(pdfId, annotMap) {
  try {
    await ariyaPdfIdbPut('annots', pdfId, annotMap || {});
    return true;
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('ariyaSavePdfAnnots', e);
    return false;
  }
}
async function ariyaLoadPdfAnnots(pdfId) {
  try {
    var data = await ariyaPdfIdbGet('annots', pdfId);
    if (data && typeof data === 'object') return data;
  } catch (e) {}
  return null;
}

function wbOpenPdfScrollViewer() {
  var lobbyMenu = document.getElementById('wbLobbyMenu');
  if (lobbyMenu) lobbyMenu.style.display = 'none';
  var inp = document.getElementById('wbLobbyPdfScrollInput');
  if (!inp) {
    showToast('خطای داخلی: ورودی فایل پیدا نشد');
    return;
  }
  inp.value = '';
  inp.onchange = function() {
    var file = inp.files && inp.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    if (file.type && file.type !== 'application/pdf' && !/\.pdf$/i.test(file.name || '')) {
      showToast('لطفاً فایل PDF انتخاب کنید');
      return;
    }
    var baseName = (file.name || 'PDF').replace(/\.pdf$/i, '').trim() || 'PDF';
    showToast('در حال باز کردن PDF…');
    wbLoadPdfJs().then(function(pdfjsLib) {
      var reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = function(ev) {
        var raw = new Uint8Array(ev.target.result);
        // دو کپی جدا: یکی برای pdf.js، یکی برای ذخیره دائمی (جلوگیری از detach)
        var dataForPdf = new Uint8Array(raw.byteLength);
        dataForPdf.set(raw);
        var dataKeep = new Uint8Array(raw.byteLength);
        dataKeep.set(raw);
        function loadDoc(useWorker) {
          var slice = new Uint8Array(dataForPdf.byteLength);
          slice.set(dataForPdf);
          var opts = { data: slice };
          if (!useWorker) opts.disableWorker = true;
          return pdfjsLib.getDocument(opts).promise;
        }
        loadDoc(true).catch(function() {
          return loadDoc(false);
        }).then(function(pdf) {
          var total = pdf.numPages || 1;
          var WB_PDF_MAX_PAGES = 400;
          var pageCount = Math.min(total, WB_PDF_MAX_PAGES);
          if (total > WB_PDF_MAX_PAGES) {
            showToast('حداکثر ۴۰۰ صفحه — ' + pageCount + ' صفحه اول');
          }

          var store = wbLoadStore();
          if (!store.pdfs) store.pdfs = {};
          var id = 'pdf_' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);
          var uniqueName = baseName;
          var k = 2;
          while (Object.keys(store.pdfs).some(function(pid) { return store.pdfs[pid].name === uniqueName; }) ||
                 Object.keys(_wbPdfSession).some(function(pid) { return _wbPdfSession[pid].name === uniqueName; })) {
            uniqueName = baseName + ' (' + k++ + ')';
          }

          var doc = {
            id: id,
            name: uniqueName,
            pageCount: pageCount,
            savedAt: Date.now(),
            folderId: wbCurrentFolderId == null ? null : wbCurrentFolderId,
            storage: 'pdf-binary',
            mode: 'lazy'
          };
          // نگه داشتن PDF در حافظه جلسه با کپی سالم
          _wbPdfSession[id] = Object.assign({}, doc, {
            pdfDoc: pdf,
            pdfData: dataKeep,
            annotMap: {}
          });

          var light = {
            id: id,
            name: uniqueName,
            pageCount: pageCount,
            savedAt: doc.savedAt,
            folderId: doc.folderId,
            storage: 'pdf-binary',
            mode: 'lazy',
            thumb: null
          };
          store.pdfs[id] = light;
          try { wbSaveStore(store); } catch (eLs) {}

          // ذخیره دائمی در IndexedDB (منتظر می‌مانیم تا بعد از خروج هم باز شود)
          ariyaSavePdfBinary(id, dataKeep).then(function(ok) {
            try {
              var st2 = wbLoadStore();
              if (st2.pdfs && st2.pdfs[id]) {
                st2.pdfs[id].storage = 'pdf-binary';
                st2.pdfs[id].persisted = !!ok;
                wbSaveStore(st2);
              }
            } catch (eMeta) {}
            if (ok) showToast(pageCount + ' صفحه ذخیره شد — اسکرول کنید');
            else showToast(pageCount + ' صفحه باز شد (فقط همین جلسه)');
          }).catch(function() {
            showToast(pageCount + ' صفحه باز شد (ذخیره دائمی ناموفق)');
          });

          // پیش‌نمایش صفحه ۱ برای لابی (سبک)
          pdf.getPage(1).then(function(page) {
            try {
              var vp = page.getViewport({ scale: 0.25 });
              var c = document.createElement('canvas');
              c.width = Math.floor(vp.width);
              c.height = Math.floor(vp.height);
              return page.render({ canvasContext: c.getContext('2d'), viewport: vp }).promise.then(function() {
                light.thumb = c.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.5);
                try {
                  var st3 = wbLoadStore();
                  if (st3.pdfs && st3.pdfs[id]) {
                    st3.pdfs[id].thumb = light.thumb;
                    wbSaveStore(st3);
                  }
                } catch (e) {}
                try { renderWbLobbyGrid(); } catch (e) {}
              });
            } catch (e) {}
          }).catch(function() {});

          wbOpenPdfDocLazy(id);
          try { renderWbLobbyGrid(); } catch (e4) {}
        }).catch(function(err) {
          console.error(err);
          showToast('خطا در خواندن PDF — فایل را بررسی کنید');
        });
      };
      reader.onerror = function() { showToast('خواندن فایل ناموفق بود'); };
      reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
    }).catch(function() {
      showToast('برای PDF یک‌بار اینترنت لازم است (بارگذاری کتابخانه)');
    });
  };
  setTimeout(function() { inp.click(); }, 50);
}

/** باز کردن PDF با رندر تنبل — همه صفحات بدون کرش */
function wbOpenPdfDocLazy(id) {
  var sess = _wbPdfSession[id];
  var store = wbLoadStore();
  var meta = (store.pdfs && store.pdfs[id]) || sess || { id: id, name: 'PDF' };

  function startWithPdf(pdf, pageCount, annotMap) {
    wbPdfState.id = id;
    wbPdfState.name = meta.name || 'PDF';
    wbPdfState.total = pageCount;
    wbPdfState.pdfDoc = pdf;
    wbPdfState.mode = 'lazy';
    wbPdfState.pageCanvases = [];
    wbPdfState.history = {};
    wbPdfState.dirty = false;
    wbPdfState.annotMap = annotMap || {};
    wbPdfState.pages = [];
    var title = document.getElementById('wbPdfTitle');
    if (title) title.textContent = '📄 ' + wbPdfState.name;
    var info = document.getElementById('wbPdfPageInfo');
    if (info) info.textContent = pageCount + ' صفحه';
    wbShowPdfView();
    wbRenderPdfLazyPages();
    showToast(pageCount + ' صفحه — اسکرول کنید (رندر هوشمند)');
  }

  function buildFromData(data, annotMap) {
    return wbLoadPdfJs().then(function(pdfjsLib) {
      // کپی بافر تا pdf.js اصل داده را خراب/detach نکند
      var src = data instanceof Uint8Array ? data : new Uint8Array(data);
      var copy = new Uint8Array(src.byteLength);
      copy.set(src);
      var keep = new Uint8Array(src.byteLength);
      keep.set(src);
      return pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: copy, disableWorker: true }).promise.then(function(pdf) {
        var pc = Math.min(pdf.numPages || 1, 400);
        _wbPdfSession[id] = Object.assign({}, meta, {
          pdfDoc: pdf,
          pdfData: keep,
          pageCount: pc,
          annotMap: annotMap || {},
          mode: 'lazy'
        });
        startWithPdf(pdf, pc, annotMap || {});
        try {
          if (keep && typeof ariyaSavePdfBinary === 'function') {
            ariyaSavePdfBinary(id, keep).catch(function() {});
          }
        } catch (eRep) {}
      });
    });
  }

  // ۱) pdfDoc زنده در حافظه
  if (sess && sess.pdfDoc) {
    try {
      startWithPdf(sess.pdfDoc, sess.pageCount || sess.pdfDoc.numPages, sess.annotMap || {});
      return;
    } catch (eLive) {
      console.warn('pdfDoc live failed', eLive);
    }
  }

  // ۲) باینری هنوز در حافظه جلسه
  if (sess && sess.pdfData) {
    showToast('در حال بارگذاری PDF…');
    var am2 = sess.annotMap || {};
    buildFromData(sess.pdfData, am2).catch(function(err) {
      console.error(err);
      showToast('PDF پیدا نشد — دوباره وارد کنید');
    });
    return;
  }

  // ۳) IndexedDB / OPFS + annot
  showToast('در حال بارگذاری PDF…');
  Promise.all([
    ariyaLoadPdfBinary(id),
    (typeof ariyaLoadPdfAnnots === 'function' ? ariyaLoadPdfAnnots(id).catch(function(){ return null; }) : Promise.resolve(null))
  ]).then(function(results) {
    var data = results[0];
    var loadedAnnots = results[1];
    var annotMap = (sess && sess.annotMap) || loadedAnnots || {};
    if (data) {
      return buildFromData(data, annotMap);
    }
    return ariyaLoadPdfPages(id).then(function(pages) {
      if (pages && pages.length) {
        wbOpenPdfDocLegacy(id, meta, pages);
        return null;
      }
      throw new Error('no data');
    });
  }).catch(function(err) {
    console.error(err);
    showToast('PDF پیدا نشد — دوباره وارد کنید');
  });
}

function wbOpenPdfDocLegacy(id, meta, pages) {
  _wbPdfSession[id] = Object.assign({}, meta, { pages: pages });
  wbPdfState.id = id;
  wbPdfState.name = meta.name || 'PDF';
  wbPdfState.pages = pages.map(function(p) { return { base: p.base, annot: p.annot || null }; });
  wbPdfState.total = pages.length;
  wbPdfState.mode = 'images';
  wbPdfState.pdfDoc = null;
  wbPdfState.pageCanvases = [];
  wbPdfState.history = {};
  wbPdfState.dirty = false;
  var title = document.getElementById('wbPdfTitle');
  if (title) title.textContent = '📄 ' + wbPdfState.name;
  var info = document.getElementById('wbPdfPageInfo');
  if (info) info.textContent = wbPdfState.total + ' صفحه';
  wbShowPdfView();
  wbRenderPdfDocPages();
}

function wbOpenPdfDoc(id) {
  var sess = _wbPdfSession[id];
  if (sess && sess.pdfDoc) {
    wbOpenPdfDocLazy(id);
    return;
  }
  if (sess && sess.pages && sess.pages.length) {
    wbOpenPdfDocLegacy(id, sess, sess.pages);
    return;
  }
  var store = wbLoadStore();
  var meta = store.pdfs && store.pdfs[id];
  if (meta && (meta.mode === 'lazy' || meta.storage === 'pdf-binary')) {
    wbOpenPdfDocLazy(id);
    return;
  }
  wbOpenPdfDocLazy(id);
}

function wbRenderPdfLazyPages() {
  var container = document.getElementById('wbPdfPages');
  var pdf = wbPdfState.pdfDoc;
  if (!container || !pdf) return;
  container.innerHTML = '';
  container.style.transform = '';
  container.dataset.zoom = '1';
  wbPdfState.pageCanvases = [];
  var total = wbPdfState.total;
  var info = document.getElementById('wbPdfPageInfo');
  if (info) info.textContent = total + ' صفحه';

  var observer = null;
  if (typeof IntersectionObserver !== 'undefined') {
    observer = new IntersectionObserver(function(entries) {
      entries.forEach(function(entry) {
        if (!entry.isIntersecting) return;
        var wrap = entry.target;
        if (wrap.dataset.rendered === '1') return;
        var pn = parseInt(wrap.dataset.pageNum, 10);
        if (!pn) return;
        wrap.dataset.rendered = '1';
        wbLazyRenderOnePage(wrap, pn);
      });
    }, { root: document.getElementById('wbPdfScroll'), rootMargin: '400px 0px', threshold: 0.01 });
  }

  for (var i = 1; i <= total; i++) {
    (function(pn) {
      var wrap = document.createElement('div');
      wrap.className = 'wb-pdf-page';
      wrap.dataset.pageNum = String(pn);
      wrap.dataset.pageIdx = String(pn - 1);
      wrap.style.cssText = 'background:#fff;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);border-radius:4px;overflow:hidden;position:relative;line-height:0;width:100%;max-width:920px;min-height:280px;';
      var label = document.createElement('div');
      label.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;top:6px;left:8px;background:rgba(15,23,42,0.75);color:#fff;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:6px;z-index:3;direction:ltr;pointer-events:none';
      label.textContent = pn + ' / ' + total;
      var addBtn = document.createElement('button');
      addBtn.type = 'button';
      addBtn.textContent = '＋ صفحه سفید بعد';
      addBtn.title = 'صفحه سفید بعد از این صفحه';
      addBtn.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;top:6px;right:8px;z-index:4;border:none;border-radius:8px;padding:4px 10px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;cursor:pointer;background:#2563eb;color:#fff';
      addBtn.onclick = function(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        showToast('در حالت PDF اصلی، صفحه سفید روی لایه یادداشت اعمال می‌شود');
      };
      var placeholder = document.createElement('div');
      placeholder.className = 'wb-pdf-ph';
      placeholder.style.cssText = 'display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-height:280px;color:#94a3b8;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;background:#f8fafc';
      placeholder.textContent = 'در حال آماده‌سازی صفحه ' + pn + '…';
      wrap.appendChild(placeholder);
      wrap.appendChild(label);
      wrap.appendChild(addBtn);
      container.appendChild(wrap);
      if (observer) observer.observe(wrap);
      else {
        // بدون IntersectionObserver — رندر ترتیبی سبک
        setTimeout(function() { wbLazyRenderOnePage(wrap, pn); }, pn * 30);
      }
    })(i);
  }
}

function wbLazyRenderOnePage(wrap, pageNum) {
  var pdf = wbPdfState.pdfDoc;
  if (!pdf || !wrap) return;
  pdf.getPage(pageNum).then(function(page) {
    var base = page.getViewport({ scale: 1 });
    var maxW = Math.min(920, (document.getElementById('wbPdfScroll') || {}).clientWidth || 900);
    maxW = Math.max(280, maxW - 24);
    var scale = maxW / base.width;
    // محدود کردن رزولوشن برای موبایل/حافظه
    if (base.width * scale > 1400) scale = 1400 / base.width;
    var viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: scale });
    var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    var canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
    canvas.width = Math.floor(viewport.width * dpr);
    canvas.height = Math.floor(viewport.height * dpr);
    canvas.style.width = Math.floor(viewport.width) + 'px';
    canvas.style.height = Math.floor(viewport.height) + 'px';
    canvas.style.display = 'block';
    canvas.style.maxWidth = '100%';
    canvas.style.height = 'auto';
    var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, viewport.width, viewport.height);

    var annot = document.createElement('canvas');
    annot.className = 'wb-pdf-annot';
    annot.dataset.page = String(pageNum - 1);
    annot.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:100%;height:100%;z-index:2;touch-action:none;cursor:crosshair;';

    return page.render({ canvasContext: ctx, viewport: viewport }).promise.then(function() {
      var ph = wrap.querySelector('.wb-pdf-ph');
      if (ph) ph.remove();
      // ترتیب: canvas صفحه، annot، label و دکمه از قبل هست
      wrap.insertBefore(canvas, wrap.firstChild);
      wrap.insertBefore(annot, canvas.nextSibling);
      wrap.style.minHeight = '';
      var idx = pageNum - 1;
      wbPdfState.pageCanvases[idx] = annot;
      // اندازه annot
      var w = canvas.clientWidth || viewport.width;
      var h = canvas.clientHeight || viewport.height;
      annot.width = Math.floor(w * dpr);
      annot.height = Math.floor(h * dpr);
      annot.style.width = w + 'px';
      annot.style.height = h + 'px';
      var actx = annot.getContext('2d');
      actx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
      // بازیابی یادداشت ذخیره‌شده
      var am = wbPdfState.annotMap || {};
      if (am[idx]) {
        var im = new Image();
        im.onload = function() { actx.drawImage(im, 0, 0, w, h); };
        im.src = am[idx];
      }
      if (typeof wbBindPdfAnnotCanvas === 'function') {
        wbBindPdfAnnotCanvas(annot, idx);
      }
    });
  }).catch(function(err) {
    console.error('page render', pageNum, err);
    var ph = wrap.querySelector('.wb-pdf-ph');
    if (ph) ph.textContent = 'خطا در صفحه ' + pageNum;
  });
}




function wbRenderPdfDocPages() {
  var container = document.getElementById('wbPdfPages');
  if (!container) return;
  container.innerHTML = '';
  wbPdfState.pageCanvases = [];
  wbPdfState.total = wbPdfState.pages.length;
  var total = wbPdfState.total;
  var info = document.getElementById('wbPdfPageInfo');
  if (info) info.textContent = total + ' صفحه';

  wbPdfState.pages.forEach(function(pg, idx) {
    var wrap = document.createElement('div');
    wrap.className = 'wb-pdf-page';
    wrap.dataset.pageIdx = String(idx);
    wrap.style.cssText = 'background:#fff;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);border-radius:4px;overflow:hidden;position:relative;line-height:0;width:100%;max-width:920px;';
    var label = document.createElement('div');
    label.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;top:6px;left:8px;background:rgba(15,23,42,0.75);color:#fff;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:6px;z-index:3;direction:ltr;pointer-events:none';
    label.textContent = (idx + 1) + ' / ' + total;
    var addBtn = document.createElement('button');
    addBtn.type = 'button';
    addBtn.textContent = '＋ صفحه سفید بعد';
    addBtn.title = 'یک صفحه سفید بعد از این صفحه اضافه کن';
    addBtn.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;top:6px;right:8px;z-index:4;border:none;border-radius:8px;padding:4px 10px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;cursor:pointer;background:#2563eb;color:#fff;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.25)';
    addBtn.onclick = function(e) {
      e.stopPropagation();
      wbPdfInsertBlankAfter(idx);
    };
    var baseImg = document.createElement('img');
    baseImg.src = pg.base;
    baseImg.alt = 'page ' + (idx + 1);
    baseImg.style.cssText = 'display:block;width:100%;height:auto;max-width:100%;user-select:none;-webkit-user-drag:none;';
    var annot = document.createElement('canvas');
    annot.className = 'wb-pdf-annot';
    annot.dataset.page = String(idx);
    annot.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:100%;height:100%;z-index:2;touch-action:none;cursor:crosshair;';
    wrap.appendChild(baseImg);
    wrap.appendChild(annot);
    wrap.appendChild(label);
    wrap.appendChild(addBtn);
    container.appendChild(wrap);
    wbPdfState.pageCanvases[idx] = annot;

    function syncSize() {
      var w = baseImg.clientWidth || baseImg.naturalWidth;
      var h = baseImg.clientHeight || baseImg.naturalHeight;
      if (!w || !h) return;
      var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      annot.width = Math.floor(w * dpr);
      annot.height = Math.floor(h * dpr);
      annot.style.width = w + 'px';
      annot.style.height = h + 'px';
      var ctx = annot.getContext('2d');
      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
      if (pg.annot) {
        var im = new Image();
        im.onload = function() { ctx.drawImage(im, 0, 0, w, h); };
        im.src = pg.annot;
      }
    }
    if (baseImg.complete && baseImg.naturalWidth) setTimeout(syncSize, 0);
    else baseImg.onload = syncSize;

    wbBindPdfAnnotCanvas(annot, idx);
  });
}


function wbBindPdfAnnotCanvas(canvas, pageIndex) {
  if (canvas.dataset.boundPdf) return;
  canvas.dataset.boundPdf = '1';

  function cssPos(e) {
    var r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    var src = (e.touches && e.touches[0]) ? e.touches[0] : (e.changedTouches && e.changedTouches[0]) ? e.changedTouches[0] : e;
    var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    return {
      x: (src.clientX - r.left) / r.width * (canvas.width / dpr),
      y: (src.clientY - r.top) / r.height * (canvas.height / dpr)
    };
  }
  function getCtx() {
    var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    return ctx;
  }
  function pushHist() {
    if (!wbPdfState.history[pageIndex]) wbPdfState.history[pageIndex] = [];
    if (!wbPdfState.redo[pageIndex]) wbPdfState.redo[pageIndex] = [];
    try {
      wbPdfState.history[pageIndex].push(canvas.toDataURL('image/png'));
      if (wbPdfState.history[pageIndex].length > 30) wbPdfState.history[pageIndex].shift();
      wbPdfState.redo[pageIndex] = [];
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  function saveAnnot() {
    try {
      if (!wbPdfState.pages[pageIndex]) wbPdfState.pages[pageIndex] = { base: null, annot: null };
      wbPdfState.pages[pageIndex].annot = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
      if (!wbPdfState.annotMap) wbPdfState.annotMap = {};
      wbPdfState.annotMap[pageIndex] = wbPdfState.pages[pageIndex].annot;
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  function snapCanvas() {
    try {
      var img = new Image();
      img.src = canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
      return img;
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  }
  function drawShape(ctx, shape, x0, y0, x1, y1) {
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
    ctx.strokeStyle = wbPdfState.color;
    ctx.fillStyle = wbPdfState.color;
    ctx.lineWidth = wbPdfState.size;
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
    var minX = Math.min(x0, x1), maxX = Math.max(x0, x1);
    var minY = Math.min(y0, y1), maxY = Math.max(y0, y1);
    var w = maxX - minX, h = maxY - minY;
    if (shape === 'line') {
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0, y0); ctx.lineTo(x1, y1); ctx.stroke();
    } else if (shape === 'arrow') {
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(x0, y0); ctx.lineTo(x1, y1); ctx.stroke();
      var ang = Math.atan2(y1 - y0, x1 - x0);
      var len = Math.max(10, wbPdfState.size * 3);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(x1, y1);
      ctx.lineTo(x1 - len * Math.cos(ang - 0.4), y1 - len * Math.sin(ang - 0.4));
      ctx.lineTo(x1 - len * Math.cos(ang + 0.4), y1 - len * Math.sin(ang + 0.4));
      ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    } else if (shape === 'rect') {
      ctx.strokeRect(minX, minY, w, h);
    } else if (shape === 'circle') {
      var r = Math.sqrt(w * w + h * h) / 2;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc((minX + maxX) / 2, (minY + maxY) / 2, r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.stroke();
    } else if (shape === 'ellipse') {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.ellipse((minX + maxX) / 2, (minY + maxY) / 2, Math.max(1, w / 2), Math.max(1, h / 2), 0, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctx.stroke();
    } else if (shape === 'tri') {
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo((minX + maxX) / 2, minY);
      ctx.lineTo(maxX, maxY);
      ctx.lineTo(minX, maxY);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.stroke();
    }
  }
  function floodFill(cx, cy) {
    var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    var w = canvas.width, h = canvas.height;
    var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    var img = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h);
    var data = img.data;
    var sx = Math.floor(cx * dpr), sy = Math.floor(cy * dpr);
    if (sx < 0 || sy < 0 || sx >= w || sy >= h) return;
    var i0 = (sy * w + sx) * 4;
    var tr = data[i0], tg = data[i0 + 1], tb = data[i0 + 2], ta = data[i0 + 3];
    var hex = (wbPdfState.color || '#111827').replace('#', '');
    if (hex.length === 3) hex = hex[0] + hex[0] + hex[1] + hex[1] + hex[2] + hex[2];
    var fr = parseInt(hex.slice(0, 2), 16), fg = parseInt(hex.slice(2, 4), 16), fb = parseInt(hex.slice(4, 6), 16);
    var tol = Math.round((wbPdfState.fillTol || 50) / 100 * 90);
    var style = wbPdfState.fillStyle || 'solid';
    // شفاف = قابل پر شدن مثل سفید
    if (ta < 20) { tr = 255; tg = 255; tb = 255; ta = 0; }
    function match(i) {
      var a = data[i + 3];
      if (ta < 20 && a < 20) return true;
      if (a < 20) return ta < 20;
      return Math.abs(data[i] - tr) <= tol && Math.abs(data[i + 1] - tg) <= tol &&
             Math.abs(data[i + 2] - tb) <= tol;
    }
    function paintAt(i, x, y) {
      var ok = true;
      if (style === 'dots') ok = (x % 6 < 2 && y % 6 < 2);
      else if (style === 'hatch') ok = ((x + y) % 6) < 2;
      else if (style === 'cross') ok = ((x + y) % 6) < 2 || ((x - y + 12000) % 6) < 2;
      else if (style === 'lines') ok = (y % 5) < 2;
      // solid یا الگوی فعال
      if (!ok && style !== 'solid') return;
      data[i] = fr; data[i + 1] = fg; data[i + 2] = fb; data[i + 3] = 255;
    }
    if (tr === fr && tg === fg && tb === fb && ta > 200) return;
    var stack = [[sx, sy]];
    var seen = new Uint8Array(w * h);
    var max = w * h;
    var count = 0;
    while (stack.length && count < max) {
      var p = stack.pop();
      var x = p[0], y = p[1];
      if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= w || y >= h) continue;
      var idx = y * w + x;
      if (seen[idx]) continue;
      seen[idx] = 1;
      var i = idx * 4;
      if (!match(i)) continue;
      paintAt(i, x, y);
      count++;
      stack.push([x + 1, y], [x - 1, y], [x, y + 1], [x, y - 1]);
    }
    ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
  }


  function start(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    wbPdfState.activePage = pageIndex;
    var p0 = cssPos(e);
    var tool = wbPdfState.tool;
    var shape = wbPdfState.shape;

    if (tool === 'filler') {
      pushHist();
      floodFill(p0.x, p0.y);
      saveAnnot();
      wbPdfState.dirty = true;
      return;
    }
    if (tool === 'shapes' && shape === 'text') {
      var txt = prompt('متن را وارد کنید:');
      if (txt) {
        pushHist();
        var ctx = getCtx();
        ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
        ctx.fillStyle = wbPdfState.color;
        ctx.font = Math.max(12, wbPdfState.size * 4) + 'px sans-serif';
        ctx.fillText(txt, p0.x, p0.y);
        saveAnnot();
        wbPdfState.dirty = true;
      }
      return;
    }
    if (tool === 'select') {
      // انتخاب واقعی روی لایه یادداشت PDF
      wbPdfState.drawing = true;
      wbPdfState.selStart = p0;
      wbPdfState.selecting = true;
      wbPdfState.last = p0;
      // کادر موقت
      var box = document.getElementById('wbPdfSelBox_' + pageIndex);
      if (!box) {
        box = document.createElement('div');
        box.id = 'wbPdfSelBox_' + pageIndex;
        box.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;border:2px dashed #2563eb;background:rgba(37,99,235,0.08);pointer-events:none;z-index:5;display:none';
        var parent = canvas.parentElement;
        if (parent) {
          if (getComputedStyle(parent).position === 'static') parent.style.position = 'relative';
          parent.appendChild(box);
        }
      }
      wbPdfState._selBox = box;
      if (box) {
        box.style.display = 'block';
        box.style.left = p0.x + 'px';
        box.style.top = p0.y + 'px';
        box.style.width = '0px';
        box.style.height = '0px';
      }
      try { canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
      return;
    }

    wbPdfState.drawing = true;
    wbPdfState.last = p0;
    pushHist();

    if (tool === 'shapes') {
      wbPdfState.shapeStart = p0;
      wbPdfState.shapeSnapshot = snapCanvas();
    } else {
      var ctx0 = getCtx();
      if (tool === 'eraser') {
        ctx0.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
        ctx0.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,1)';
        ctx0.beginPath();
        ctx0.arc(p0.x, p0.y, Math.max(12, wbPdfState.size * 3) / 2, 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx0.fill();
      } else if (tool === 'highlighter') {
        wbPdfState._hlMoved = false;
        wbPdfState._hlPts = [{ x: p0.x, y: p0.y }];
        try {
          wbPdfState._hlSnap = document.createElement('canvas');
          wbPdfState._hlSnap.width = canvas.width;
          wbPdfState._hlSnap.height = canvas.height;
          // قبل از هر علامت — تصویر فعلی (بعد از pushHist)
          var ctmp = getCtx();
          var dpr0 = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
          ctmp.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
          wbPdfState._hlSnap.getContext('2d').drawImage(canvas, 0, 0);
          ctmp.setTransform(dpr0,0,0,dpr0,0,0);
        } catch (ePs) { wbPdfState._hlSnap = null; }
      } else {
        ctx0.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
        ctx0.fillStyle = wbPdfState.color;
        ctx0.beginPath();
        ctx0.arc(p0.x, p0.y, Math.max(0.8, wbPdfState.size / 2), 0, Math.PI * 2);
        ctx0.fill();
      }

    }
    wbPdfState.dirty = true;
    try { canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (err) {}
  }

  function move(e) {
    if (!wbPdfState.drawing || wbPdfState.activePage !== pageIndex) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    var p = cssPos(e);
    var tool = wbPdfState.tool;

    if (tool === 'select' && wbPdfState.selecting && wbPdfState.selStart) {
      var s = wbPdfState.selStart;
      var box = wbPdfState._selBox;
      if (box) {
        var x1 = Math.min(s.x, p.x), y1 = Math.min(s.y, p.y);
        var x2 = Math.max(s.x, p.x), y2 = Math.max(s.y, p.y);
        box.style.left = x1 + 'px';
        box.style.top = y1 + 'px';
        box.style.width = Math.max(1, x2 - x1) + 'px';
        box.style.height = Math.max(1, y2 - y1) + 'px';
        box.style.display = 'block';
      }
      wbPdfState.last = p;
      return;
    }

    if (tool === 'shapes' && wbPdfState.shapeStart) {
      var ctx = getCtx();
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      if (wbPdfState.shapeSnapshot && wbPdfState.shapeSnapshot.complete) {
        var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
        ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
        ctx.drawImage(wbPdfState.shapeSnapshot, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
        ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
      }
      drawShape(ctx, wbPdfState.shape, wbPdfState.shapeStart.x, wbPdfState.shapeStart.y, p.x, p.y);
      wbPdfState.dirty = true;
      return;
    }

    var ctx = getCtx();
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
    if (tool === 'eraser') {
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'destination-out';
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,1)';
      ctx.lineWidth = Math.max(12, wbPdfState.size * 3);
    } else if (tool === 'highlighter') {
      // یکنواخت: بازسازی کل مسیر stroke
      if (!wbPdfState._hlPts) wbPdfState._hlPts = [{ x: wbPdfState.last.x, y: wbPdfState.last.y }];
      wbPdfState._hlPts.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y });
      var hlA = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(0.55, wbPdfState.hlOpacity || 0.3));
      var hlW = Math.max(14, wbPdfState.size * 5.5);
      var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      if (wbPdfState._hlSnap) {
        try {
          ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
          ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
          ctx.drawImage(wbPdfState._hlSnap, 0, 0);
          ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
        } catch (eR) {}
      }
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      ctx.strokeStyle = (typeof hexAlpha === 'function')
        ? hexAlpha(wbPdfState.color, hlA)
        : wbPdfState.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = hlW;
      ctx.lineCap = 'round';
      ctx.lineJoin = 'round';
      var pts = wbPdfState._hlPts;
      ctx.beginPath();
      if (pts.length < 2) {
        ctx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
        ctx.lineTo(pts[0].x + 0.01, pts[0].y);
      } else {
        ctx.moveTo(pts[0].x, pts[0].y);
        for (var hi = 1; hi < pts.length - 1; hi++) {
          var mx = (pts[hi].x + pts[hi + 1].x) / 2;
          var my = (pts[hi].y + pts[hi + 1].y) / 2;
          ctx.quadraticCurveTo(pts[hi].x, pts[hi].y, mx, my);
        }
        ctx.lineTo(pts[pts.length - 1].x, pts[pts.length - 1].y);
      }
      ctx.stroke();
      wbPdfState._hlMoved = true;
      wbPdfState.last = p;
      wbPdfState.dirty = true;
      return;
    } else {
      ctx.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      ctx.strokeStyle = wbPdfState.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = wbPdfState.size;
    }
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(wbPdfState.last.x, wbPdfState.last.y);
    ctx.lineTo(p.x, p.y);
    ctx.stroke();
    wbPdfState.last = p;
    wbPdfState.dirty = true;
  }

  function end(e) {
    if (!wbPdfState.drawing) return;

    // پایان انتخاب PDF
    if (wbPdfState.tool === 'select' && wbPdfState.selecting && wbPdfState.selStart) {
      var s = wbPdfState.selStart;
      var p = wbPdfState.last || s;
      wbPdfState.selecting = false;
      wbPdfState.drawing = false;
      var box = wbPdfState._selBox;
      if (box) box.style.display = 'none';
      var x1 = Math.min(s.x, p.x), y1 = Math.min(s.y, p.y);
      var x2 = Math.max(s.x, p.x), y2 = Math.max(s.y, p.y);
      var rw = x2 - x1, rh = y2 - y1;
      if (rw < 6 || rh < 6) {
        showToast('کادر خیلی کوچک است');
        wbPdfState.selStart = null;
        return;
      }
      // برش از لایه annot
      pushHist();
      var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      var ctx = getCtx();
      var px = Math.floor(x1 * dpr), py = Math.floor(y1 * dpr);
      var pw = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(rw * dpr)), ph = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(rh * dpr));
      var imgData;
      try {
        ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
        imgData = ctx.getImageData(px, py, pw, ph);
      } catch (err) {
        ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
        showToast('انتخاب ناموفق');
        return;
      }
      // آیا محتوایی هست؟
      var has = false;
      for (var i = 3; i < imgData.data.length; i += 4) {
        if (imgData.data[i] > 15) { has = true; break; }
      }
      if (!has) {
        ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
        showToast('یادداشتی داخل کادر نیست');
        wbPdfState.selStart = null;
        return;
      }
      // پاک کردن ناحیه از بوم
      ctx.clearRect(px, py, pw, ph);
      ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
      // ساخت float
      var off = document.createElement('canvas');
      off.width = pw; off.height = ph;
      off.getContext('2d').putImageData(imgData, 0, 0);
      var parent = canvas.parentElement;
      var floatEl = document.getElementById('wbPdfSelFloat_' + pageIndex);
      if (floatEl) floatEl.remove();
      floatEl = document.createElement('div');
      floatEl.id = 'wbPdfSelFloat_' + pageIndex;
      floatEl.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:' + x1 + 'px;top:' + y1 + 'px;width:' + rw + 'px;height:' + rh +
        'px;border:2px solid #2563eb;box-shadow:0 0 0 1px #fff,0 8px 24px rgba(37,99,235,0.35);z-index:8;cursor:move;touch-action:none;background:transparent';
      var fc = document.createElement('canvas');
      fc.width = pw; fc.height = ph;
      fc.style.width = '100%'; fc.style.height = '100%'; fc.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
      fc.getContext('2d').drawImage(off, 0, 0);
      floatEl.appendChild(fc);
      var acts = document.createElement('div');
      acts.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;top:-36px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);display:flex;gap:6px;white-space:nowrap';
      var btnOk = document.createElement('button');
      btnOk.textContent = '✓ ثبت';
      btnOk.style.cssText = 'border:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--card);color:#059669;border-radius:8px;padding:4px 10px;font-size:12px;font-weight:800;cursor:pointer';
      var btnDel = document.createElement('button');
      btnDel.textContent = '🗑 حذف';
      btnDel.style.cssText = 'border:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--card);color:#ef4444;border-radius:8px;padding:4px 10px;font-size:12px;font-weight:800;cursor:pointer';
      acts.appendChild(btnOk); acts.appendChild(btnDel);
      floatEl.appendChild(acts);
      if (parent) {
        if (getComputedStyle(parent).position === 'static') parent.style.position = 'relative';
        parent.appendChild(floatEl);
      }
      var dragging = false, ox = 0, oy = 0, baseL = x1, baseT = y1;
      floatEl.addEventListener('pointerdown', function(ev) {
        if (ev.target.tagName === 'BUTTON') return;
        dragging = true; ox = ev.clientX; oy = ev.clientY;
        baseL = parseFloat(floatEl.style.left) || 0;
        baseT = parseFloat(floatEl.style.top) || 0;
        try { floatEl.setPointerCapture(ev.pointerId); } catch (e2) {}
        ev.preventDefault(); ev.stopPropagation();
      });
      floatEl.addEventListener('pointermove', function(ev) {
        if (!dragging) return;
        floatEl.style.left = (baseL + (ev.clientX - ox)) + 'px';
        floatEl.style.top = (baseT + (ev.clientY - oy)) + 'px';
      });
      floatEl.addEventListener('pointerup', function() { dragging = false; });
      function commitFloat(del) {
        var lx = parseFloat(floatEl.style.left) || 0;
        var ly = parseFloat(floatEl.style.top) || 0;
        if (!del) {
          pushHist();
          var ctx2 = getCtx();
          ctx2.drawImage(off, lx, ly, rw, rh);
        }
        floatEl.remove();
        saveAnnot();
        wbPdfState.dirty = true;
        showToast(del ? 'حذف شد' : 'ثبت شد');
      }
      btnOk.onclick = function(ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); commitFloat(false); };
      btnDel.onclick = function(ev) { ev.stopPropagation(); commitFloat(true); };
      wbPdfState.selStart = null;
      wbPdfState.dirty = true;
      saveAnnot();
      showToast('انتخاب شد — بکشید یا ثبت/حذف کنید');
      return;
    }

    // هایلایت تک‌کلیک
    if (wbPdfState.tool === 'highlighter' && !wbPdfState._hlMoved && wbPdfState.last) {
      var ctxE = getCtx();
      ctxE.globalCompositeOperation = 'source-over';
      ctxE.fillStyle = (typeof hexAlpha === 'function')
        ? hexAlpha(wbPdfState.color, wbPdfState.hlOpacity || 0.3)
        : wbPdfState.color;
      var hr = Math.max(12, wbPdfState.size * 5) / 2;
      ctxE.beginPath();
      ctxE.arc(wbPdfState.last.x, wbPdfState.last.y, hr, 0, Math.PI * 2);
      ctxE.fill();
    }
    wbPdfState.drawing = false;
    wbPdfState.shapeStart = null;
    wbPdfState.shapeSnapshot = null;
    wbPdfState._hlMoved = false;
    wbPdfState._hlPts = null;
    wbPdfState._hlSnap = null;
    saveAnnot();
  }

  canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', start);
  canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', move);
  canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', end);
  canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', end);
}

function wbPdfRedoPage() {
  var idx = wbPdfState.activePage;
  if (idx < 0) idx = 0;
  var redo = wbPdfState.redo[idx];
  var canvas = wbPdfState.pageCanvases[idx];
  if (!redo || !redo.length || !canvas) {
    showToast('چیزی برای جلو نیست');
    return;
  }
  if (!wbPdfState.history[idx]) wbPdfState.history[idx] = [];
  try {
    wbPdfState.history[idx].push(canvas.toDataURL('image/png'));
  } catch (e) {}
  var url = redo.pop();
  var img = new Image();
  img.onload = function() {
    var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    wbPdfState.dirty = true;
  };
  img.src = url;
}


function wbPdfUndoPage() {
  var idx = wbPdfState.activePage;
  if (idx < 0) idx = 0;
  var hist = wbPdfState.history[idx];
  var canvas = wbPdfState.pageCanvases[idx];
  if (!hist || !hist.length || !canvas) {
    showToast('چیزی برای بازگشت نیست');
    return;
  }
  if (!wbPdfState.redo[idx]) wbPdfState.redo[idx] = [];
  try { wbPdfState.redo[idx].push(canvas.toDataURL('image/png')); } catch (e) {}
  var prev = hist.pop();
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
  var w = canvas.width / dpr, h = canvas.height / dpr;
  ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
  var im = new Image();
  im.onload = function() {
    ctx.clearRect(0, 0, w, h);
    ctx.drawImage(im, 0, 0, w, h);
    if (!wbPdfState.pages[idx]) wbPdfState.pages[idx] = { base: null, annot: null };
    wbPdfState.pages[idx].annot = prev;
    wbPdfState.dirty = true;
  };
  im.src = prev;
}


function wbPdfClearActivePage() {
  var idx = wbPdfState.activePage;
  if (idx < 0) idx = 0;
  var canvas = wbPdfState.pageCanvases[idx];
  if (!canvas) {
    showToast('صفحه‌ای برای پاک کردن نیست — اول روی صفحه بکشید');
    return;
  }
  if (!wbPdfState.history[idx]) wbPdfState.history[idx] = [];
  try { wbPdfState.history[idx].push(canvas.toDataURL('image/png')); } catch (e) {}
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
  ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
  if (!wbPdfState.pages[idx]) wbPdfState.pages[idx] = { base: null, annot: null };
  wbPdfState.pages[idx].annot = null;
  if (wbPdfState.annotMap) wbPdfState.annotMap[idx] = null;
  wbPdfState.dirty = true;
  showToast('یادداشت این صفحه پاک شد');
}

function wbPdfInsertBlankAfter(pageIndex) {
  // در حالت lazy: یک صفحه سفید بعد از صفحه فعلی در لیست DOM
  var container = document.getElementById('wbPdfPages');
  if (!container) {
    showToast('نمایش PDF باز نیست');
    return;
  }
  var after = typeof pageIndex === 'number' ? pageIndex : (wbPdfState.activePage >= 0 ? wbPdfState.activePage : 0);
  var wraps = container.querySelectorAll('.wb-pdf-page');
  var ref = wraps[after] || wraps[wraps.length - 1] || null;

  var w = 800, h = 1100;
  if (ref) {
    var baseC = ref.querySelector('canvas:not(.wb-pdf-annot)');
    if (baseC) {
      w = baseC.clientWidth || w;
      h = baseC.clientHeight || h;
    } else {
      w = ref.clientWidth || w;
      h = Math.max(400, Math.round(w * 1.35));
    }
  }

  var wrap = document.createElement('div');
  wrap.className = 'wb-pdf-page';
  wrap.dataset.blank = '1';
  wrap.style.cssText = 'background:#fff;box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.35);border-radius:4px;overflow:hidden;position:relative;line-height:0;width:100%;max-width:920px;';

  var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
  var base = document.createElement('canvas');
  base.width = Math.floor(w * dpr);
  base.height = Math.floor(h * dpr);
  base.style.width = w + 'px';
  base.style.height = h + 'px';
  base.style.display = 'block';
  base.style.maxWidth = '100%';
  var bctx = base.getContext('2d');
  bctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
  bctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
  bctx.fillRect(0, 0, w, h);

  var annot = document.createElement('canvas');
  annot.className = 'wb-pdf-annot';
  annot.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;left:0;top:0;width:100%;height:100%;z-index:2;touch-action:none;cursor:crosshair;';
  annot.width = Math.floor(w * dpr);
  annot.height = Math.floor(h * dpr);

  var label = document.createElement('div');
  label.style.cssText = 'position:absolute;top:6px;left:8px;background:rgba(15,23,42,0.75);color:#fff;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:6px;z-index:3;pointer-events:none';
  label.textContent = 'صفحه سفید';

  wrap.appendChild(base);
  wrap.appendChild(annot);
  wrap.appendChild(label);

  if (ref && ref.nextSibling) container.insertBefore(wrap, ref.nextSibling);
  else container.appendChild(wrap);

  var newIdx = after + 1;
  // شیفت pageCanvases
  if (!wbPdfState.pageCanvases) wbPdfState.pageCanvases = [];
  wbPdfState.pageCanvases.splice(newIdx, 0, annot);
  if (!wbPdfState.pages) wbPdfState.pages = [];
  wbPdfState.pages.splice(newIdx, 0, { base: null, annot: null, blank: true });
  wbPdfState.total = (wbPdfState.total || 0) + 1;
  annot.dataset.page = String(newIdx);
  if (typeof wbBindPdfAnnotCanvas === 'function') wbBindPdfAnnotCanvas(annot, newIdx);
  // به‌روزرسانی ایندکس‌های بعدی
  for (var i = newIdx + 1; i < wbPdfState.pageCanvases.length; i++) {
    if (wbPdfState.pageCanvases[i]) wbPdfState.pageCanvases[i].dataset.page = String(i);
  }
  var info = document.getElementById('wbPdfPageInfo');
  if (info) info.textContent = wbPdfState.total + ' صفحه';
  wbPdfState.dirty = true;
  showToast('صفحه سفید اضافه شد');
  try { wrap.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' }); } catch (e) {}
}

function wbSavePdfDoc() {
  if (!wbPdfState.id) return;
  var store = wbLoadStore();
  if (!store.pdfs || !store.pdfs[wbPdfState.id]) {
    showToast('PDF در لابی پیدا نشد');
    return;
  }
  if (!wbPdfState.annotMap) wbPdfState.annotMap = {};
  // همگام‌سازی annot از canvasها
  wbPdfState.pageCanvases.forEach(function(canvas, idx) {
    if (!canvas) return;
    try {
      var dpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
      var tmp = document.createElement('canvas');
      tmp.width = Math.floor(canvas.width / dpr);
      tmp.height = Math.floor(canvas.height / dpr);
      if (tmp.width < 2) return;
      var tctx = tmp.getContext('2d');
      tctx.drawImage(canvas, 0, 0, tmp.width, tmp.height);
      var data = tmp.toDataURL('image/png');
      if (!wbPdfState.pages[idx]) wbPdfState.pages[idx] = { base: null, annot: null };
      wbPdfState.pages[idx].annot = data;
      wbPdfState.annotMap[idx] = data;
    } catch (e) {}
  });
  // برای lazy: فقط متا + annot در session؛ base صفحات را در localStorage نریز
  var meta = store.pdfs[wbPdfState.id];
  meta.savedAt = Date.now();
  meta.pageCount = wbPdfState.total || meta.pageCount;
  if (wbPdfState.mode !== 'lazy') {
    meta.pages = (wbPdfState.pages || []).map(function(p) {
      return p ? { base: p.base, annot: p.annot } : null;
    });
  } else {
    // annotها را در session نگه دار
    if (typeof _wbPdfSession !== 'undefined' && _wbPdfSession[wbPdfState.id]) {
      _wbPdfSession[wbPdfState.id].annotMap = wbPdfState.annotMap;
      _wbPdfSession[wbPdfState.id].pages = wbPdfState.pages;
    }
  }
  try {
    wbSaveStore(store);
    wbPdfState.dirty = false;
    showToast('یادداشت‌ها ذخیره شد');
  } catch (e) {
    // حتی اگر localStorage پر باشد، در session مانده
    wbPdfState.dirty = false;
    showToast('در این جلسه ذخیره شد (حافظه مرورگر پر است)');
  }
  try {
    if (typeof ariyaSavePdfAnnots === 'function') {
      ariyaSavePdfAnnots(wbPdfState.id, wbPdfState.annotMap || {}).catch(function() {});
    }
  } catch (eAnn) {}
}


function wbPdfZoom(delta) {
  // زوم نمایشی با CSS روی ظرف صفحات
  var container = document.getElementById('wbPdfPages');
  if (!container) return;
  var cur = parseFloat(container.dataset.zoom || '1');
  var next = Math.max(0.6, Math.min(2.2, cur + delta));
  container.dataset.zoom = String(next);
  container.style.transformOrigin = 'top center';
  container.style.transform = 'scale(' + next + ')';
  var info = document.getElementById('wbPdfPageInfo');
  if (info) info.textContent = wbPdfState.total + ' صفحه · زوم ' + Math.round(next * 100) + '%';
}

function wbImportPdfToLobby() {

  var lobbyMenu = document.getElementById('wbLobbyMenu');
  if (lobbyMenu) lobbyMenu.style.display = 'none';
  var inp = document.getElementById('wbLobbyPdfInput');
  if (!inp) return;
  inp.value = '';
  inp.onchange = function() {

    var file = inp.files && inp.files[0];
    if (!file) return;
    var baseName = (file.name || 'PDF').replace(/\.pdf$/i, '').trim() || 'PDF';
    showToast('در حال خواندن PDF…');
    wbLoadPdfJs().then(function(pdfjsLib) {
      var reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = function(ev) {
        var data = new Uint8Array(ev.target.result);
        pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: data }).promise.then(function(pdf) {
          var total = pdf.numPages || 1;
          var WB_PDF_MAX_PAGES = 400;
          var fromPage = 1;
          var toPage = Math.min(total, WB_PDF_MAX_PAGES);

          // برای PDFهای خیلی بزرگ از کاربر بازه بپرس (پیش‌فرض: تا سقف ۴۰۰)
          if (total > 80) {
            var defEnd = Math.min(total, WB_PDF_MAX_PAGES);
            var rangeAns = prompt(
              'این PDF دارای ' + total + ' صفحه است.\n' +
              'حداکثر ' + WB_PDF_MAX_PAGES + ' صفحه پشتیبانی می‌شود.\n' +
              'بازه را بنویسید (مثلاً 1-100 یا 50-400).\n' +
              'خالی = صفحات ۱ تا ' + defEnd + ':',
              '1-' + defEnd
            );
            if (rangeAns === null) {
              showToast('لغو شد');
              return null;
            }
            rangeAns = String(rangeAns).trim();
            if (rangeAns) {
              var m = rangeAns.match(/(\d+)\s*[-–—تاto]+\s*(\d+)/i) || rangeAns.match(/^(\d+)$/);
              if (m) {
                fromPage = parseInt(m[1], 10) || 1;
                toPage = m[2] ? (parseInt(m[2], 10) || defEnd) : fromPage;
              }
            }
          }
          fromPage = Math.max(1, Math.min(total, fromPage));
          toPage = Math.max(fromPage, Math.min(total, toPage));
          // سقف سخت ۴۰۰ صفحه
          if (toPage - fromPage + 1 > WB_PDF_MAX_PAGES) {
            toPage = fromPage + WB_PDF_MAX_PAGES - 1;
            if (toPage > total) toPage = total;
            showToast('حداکثر ۴۰۰ صفحه — از ' + fromPage + ' تا ' + toPage);
          }
          var pageCount = toPage - fromPage + 1;

          // کیفیت تطبیقی: صفحات بیشتر → رزولوشن و کیفیت JPEG کمتر (برای حافظه)
          var maxW = 1400, maxH = 2000, jpgQ = 0.8;
          if (pageCount > 30) { maxW = 1200; maxH = 1700; jpgQ = 0.72; }
          if (pageCount > 60) { maxW = 1000; maxH = 1400; jpgQ = 0.65; }
          if (pageCount > 100) { maxW = 900; maxH = 1270; jpgQ = 0.58; }
          if (pageCount > 150) { maxW = 800; maxH = 1130; jpgQ = 0.52; }
          if (pageCount > 250) { maxW = 700; maxH = 990; jpgQ = 0.45; }
          if (pageCount > 350) { maxW = 600; maxH = 850; jpgQ = 0.40; }


          var store = wbLoadStore();
          var fid = wbUid();
          var folderLabel = '📄 ' + baseName;
          if (fromPage !== 1 || toPage !== total) {
            folderLabel += ' (' + fromPage + '–' + toPage + ')';
          }
          store.folders[fid] = {
            id: fid,
            name: folderLabel,
            createdAt: Date.now()
          };
          wbCurrentFolderId = fid;

          var chain = Promise.resolve();
          var done = 0;
          var stoppedEarly = false;

          function trySaveStore() {
            try {
              wbSaveStore(store);
              return true;
            } catch (e) {
              // حافظه پر — صفحات ذخیره‌شده تا اینجا را نگه دار
              try { wbSaveStore(store); } catch (e2) {}
              stoppedEarly = true;
              return false;
            }
          }

          for (var pageNum = fromPage; pageNum <= toPage; pageNum++) {
            (function(pn) {
              chain = chain.then(function() {
                if (stoppedEarly) return null;
                return pdf.getPage(pn).then(function(page) {
                  var base = page.getViewport({ scale: 1 });
                  var scale = Math.min(1.4, maxW / base.width);
                  if (base.width * scale > maxW) scale = maxW / base.width;
                  if (base.height * scale > maxH) scale = maxH / base.height;
                  var viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: scale });
                  var off = document.createElement('canvas');
                  off.width = Math.max(1, Math.floor(viewport.width));
                  off.height = Math.max(1, Math.floor(viewport.height));
                  var octx = off.getContext('2d');
                  octx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';
                  octx.fillRect(0, 0, off.width, off.height);
                  return page.render({ canvasContext: octx, viewport: viewport }).promise.then(function() {
                    if (stoppedEarly) return;
                    var dataUrl = off.toDataURL('image/jpeg', jpgQ);
                    // آزادسازی حافظه canvas
                    off.width = 0;
                    off.height = 0;
                    var boardName = baseName + ' — صفحه ' + pn;
                    var unique = boardName;
                    var k = 2;
                    while (store.boards[unique]) {
                      unique = boardName + ' (' + k + ')';
                      k++;
                    }
                    store.boards[unique] = {
                      data: dataUrl,
                      savedAt: Date.now(),
                      boardStyle: 'white',
                      folderId: fid
                    };
                    done++;
                    // ذخیره میان‌دوره‌ای هر ۱۰ صفحه (کمک به از دست نرفتن کار)
                    if (done % 10 === 0) {
                      if (!trySaveStore()) {
                        showToast('حافظه مرورگر پر شد — تا صفحه ' + pn + ' ذخیره شد');
                        return;
                      }
                    }
                    if (done % 5 === 0 || done === pageCount) {
                      showToast('صفحه ' + done + ' از ' + pageCount + '…');
                    }
                  });
                });
              });
            })(pageNum);
          }
          return chain.then(function() {
            trySaveStore();
            renderWbLobbyGrid();
            if (stoppedEarly) {
              showToast(done + ' صفحه ذخیره شد (حافظه پر شد — بقیه وارد نشد)');
            } else {
              showToast(done + ' صفحه از ' + total + ' به لابی اضافه شد');
            }
          });
        }).catch(function(err) {
          console.error(err);
          showToast('خطا در خواندن PDF');
        });
      };
      reader.onerror = function() { showToast('خواندن فایل ناموفق بود'); };
      reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
    }).catch(function() {
      showToast('برای وارد کردن PDF به اینترنت نیاز است (بار اول)');
    });
  };
  inp.click();
}


function initWhiteboardView() {
  showWbLobby();
  try {
    var sc = parseInt(localStorage.getItem('wbToolbarScale') || '100', 10) || 100;
    setTimeout(function(){ applyWbToolbarSize(sc); }, 100);
  } catch (e) {}

  // lobby menu
  const lobbyMenuBtn = document.getElementById('wbLobbyMenuBtn');
  const lobbyMenu = document.getElementById('wbLobbyMenu');
  const lobbyNew = document.getElementById('wbLobbyNew');
  const lobbyNewFolder = document.getElementById('wbLobbyNewFolder');
  const lobbyImportPdf = document.getElementById('wbLobbyImportPdf');
  const lobbyImportPdfBoards = document.getElementById('wbLobbyImportPdfBoards');
  const backLobby = document.getElementById('wbBackLobby');
  if (lobbyMenuBtn && lobbyMenu) {
    lobbyMenuBtn.onclick = function(e) {
      e.stopPropagation();
      lobbyMenu.style.display = lobbyMenu.style.display === 'none' ? 'flex' : 'none';
    };
  }
  if (lobbyNew) {
    lobbyNew.onclick = function() {
      if (lobbyMenu) lobbyMenu.style.display = 'none';
      wbNewBoard();
    };
  }
  if (lobbyNewFolder) {
    lobbyNewFolder.onclick = function() {
      if (lobbyMenu) lobbyMenu.style.display = 'none';
      // فولدر فقط در ریشه
      if (wbCurrentFolderId) {
        wbCurrentFolderId = null;
        renderWbLobbyGrid();
      }
      wbCreateFolder();
    };
  }
  if (lobbyImportPdf) {
    lobbyImportPdf.onclick = function() {
      if (lobbyMenu) lobbyMenu.style.display = 'none';
      wbOpenPdfScrollViewer();
    };
  }
  if (lobbyImportPdfBoards) {
    lobbyImportPdfBoards.onclick = function() {
      if (lobbyMenu) lobbyMenu.style.display = 'none';
      wbImportPdfToLobby();
    };
  }
  if (backLobby) backLobby.onclick = function() { wbBackToLobby(); };

  // کنترل‌های نمایشگر PDF
  var pdfBack = document.getElementById('wbPdfBackLobby');
  var pdfZi = document.getElementById('wbPdfZoomIn');
  var pdfZo = document.getElementById('wbPdfZoomOut');
  if (pdfBack) pdfBack.onclick = function() { wbHidePdfView(); };
  if (pdfZi) pdfZi.onclick = function() { wbPdfZoom(0.15); };
  if (pdfZo) pdfZo.onclick = function() { wbPdfZoom(-0.15); };
}





/* ——— Set theory calculator ——— */

/* ——— زنجیر و پادزنجیر مجموعه‌ها (P(S), ⊆) ——— */
var _chainMode = 'chain';

function setChainMode(mode) {
  _chainMode = mode || 'chain';
  var labels = { chain: 'زنجیر (Chain)', anti: 'پادزنجیر (Antichain)', both: 'هر دو' };
  var colors = { chain: '#ec4899', anti: '#8b5cf6', both: '#06b6d4' };
  ['chain','anti','both'].forEach(function(m) {
    var el = document.getElementById(m === 'chain' ? 'chainModeChain' : (m === 'anti' ? 'chainModeAnti' : 'chainModeBoth'));
    if (!el) return;
    el.classList.toggle('active', m === _chainMode);
    el.classList.toggle('is-selected', m === _chainMode);
    el.setAttribute('aria-pressed', m === _chainMode ? 'true' : 'false');
  });
  var lab = document.getElementById('chainModeLabel');
  if (lab) {
    lab.textContent = '✓ انتخاب فعلی: ' + (labels[_chainMode] || _chainMode);
    lab.style.color = colors[_chainMode] || 'var(--accent)';
  }
  try { sfxClick(); } catch (e) {}
}

function chainSubsetKey(arr) {
  return arr.slice().sort().join('\u0001');
}

function chainFormatSubset(arr) {
  if (!arr || !arr.length) return '∅';
  return '{ ' + arr.slice().sort(function(a, b) {
    var na = Number(a), nb = Number(b);
    if (!isNaN(na) && !isNaN(nb) && String(na) === String(a) && String(nb) === String(b)) return na - nb;
    return String(a).localeCompare(String(b), 'fa');
  }).join(', ') + ' }';
}

function chainIsSubset(a, b) {
  // a ⊆ b ?
  if (a.length > b.length) return false;
  var setB = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < b.length; i++) setB[b[i]] = 1;
  for (var j = 0; j < a.length; j++) if (!setB[a[j]]) return false;
  return true;
}

function chainPowerSet(elems) {
  var n = elems.length;
  var out = [];
  var total = 1 << n;
  for (var mask = 0; mask < total; mask++) {
    var sub = [];
    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) if (mask & (1 << i)) sub.push(elems[i]);
    out.push(sub);
  }
  // sort by size then lexicographic
  out.sort(function(a, b) {
    if (a.length !== b.length) return a.length - b.length;
    return chainSubsetKey(a).localeCompare(chainSubsetKey(b));
  });
  return out;
}

function chainFindAllChains(power) {
  // all non-empty chains as lists of subset-indices, always can start from ∅
  // We generate every chain by DFS from every starting subset, but user wants including those starting with ∅.
  // List ALL chains (sequences strictly increasing by inclusion).
  var m = power.length;
  // precompute covers: j is strict superset of i
  var supers = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < m; i++) {
    supers[i] = [];
    for (var j = 0; j < m; j++) {
      if (i === j) continue;
      if (power[j].length > power[i].length && chainIsSubset(power[i], power[j])) {
        // minimal cover not required — any strict superset for full enumeration of chains
        supers[i].push(j);
      }
    }
  }
  var chains = [];
  function dfs(path) {
    chains.push(path.slice());
    var last = path[path.length - 1];
    var nexts = supers[last];
    for (var k = 0; k < nexts.length; k++) {
      path.push(nexts[k]);
      dfs(path);
      path.pop();
    }
  }
  for (var s = 0; s < m; s++) {
    dfs([s]);
  }
  return chains;
}

function chainFindAllAntichains(power) {
  // all antichains (including singletons and empty family? — include empty family and all subsets of incomparable sets)
  var m = power.length;
  // incomparable matrix
  var inc = [];
  for (var i = 0; i < m; i++) {
    inc[i] = [];
    for (var j = 0; j < m; j++) {
      if (i === j) { inc[i][j] = true; continue; }
      var a = power[i], b = power[j];
      inc[i][j] = !chainIsSubset(a, b) && !chainIsSubset(b, a);
    }
  }
  var antichains = [];
  // bit DP / DFS choosing increasing indices
  function dfs(start, chosen) {
    antichains.push(chosen.slice());
    for (var i = start; i < m; i++) {
      var ok = true;
      for (var c = 0; c < chosen.length; c++) {
        if (!inc[chosen[c]][i]) { ok = false; break; }
      }
      if (!ok) continue;
      chosen.push(i);
      dfs(i + 1, chosen);
      chosen.pop();
    }
  }
  dfs(0, []);
  return antichains;
}

function chainIsMaximalChain(chainIdx, power) {
  // maximal in P(S): starts at ∅ and ends at S, and consecutive differ by one element (saturated)
  if (!chainIdx.length) return false;
  var first = power[chainIdx[0]];
  var last = power[chainIdx[chainIdx.length - 1]];
  if (first.length !== 0) return false;
  // S is the unique max-size set
  var maxSize = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < power.length; i++) if (power[i].length > maxSize) maxSize = power[i].length;
  if (last.length !== maxSize) return false;
  for (var k = 1; k < chainIdx.length; k++) {
    if (power[chainIdx[k]].length !== power[chainIdx[k - 1]].length + 1) return false;
    if (!chainIsSubset(power[chainIdx[k - 1]], power[chainIdx[k]])) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

function chainIsMaximalAntichain(antiIdx, power) {
  // Sperner: cannot add any other subset while remaining antichain
  var m = power.length;
  var used = {};
  for (var i = 0; i < antiIdx.length; i++) used[antiIdx[i]] = 1;
  for (var j = 0; j < m; j++) {
    if (used[j]) continue;
    var ok = true;
    for (var a = 0; a < antiIdx.length; a++) {
      var A = power[antiIdx[a]], B = power[j];
      if (chainIsSubset(A, B) || chainIsSubset(B, A)) { ok = false; break; }
    }
    if (ok) return false; // can add j
  }
  return true;
}

function runChainAntichain() {
  var raw = (document.getElementById('chainSetInput') || {}).value || '';
  var elems = Array.from(parseSetInput(raw));
  var res = document.getElementById('chainResult');
  if (!res) return;
  if (!elems.length) {
    res.style.display = 'block';
    res.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef4444">مجموعهٔ پایه را وارد کنید (حداقل یک عضو).</span>';
    try { sfxError(); } catch (e) {}
    return;
  }
  if (elems.length > 6) {
    res.style.display = 'block';
    res.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef4444">حداکثر ۶ عضو پشتیبانی می‌شود (۲⁶ = ۶۴ زیرمجموعه). تعداد اعضا را کم کنید.</span>';
    try { sfxError(); } catch (e) {}
    return;
  }
  var onlyMax = !!(document.getElementById('chainOnlyMax') || {}).checked;
  var power = chainPowerSet(elems);
  var mode = _chainMode || 'chain';

  var html = [];
  html.push('<b>مجموعهٔ پایه:</b> <span dir="ltr">' + chainFormatSubset(elems) + '</span>');
  html.push('<b>|S| =</b> ' + elems.length + ' &nbsp;·&nbsp; <b>|P(S)| =</b> ' + power.length);
  html.push('<div class="set-ans" style="margin-top:10px"><b>P(S) — همهٔ زیرمجموعه‌ها (شامل ∅):</b><br><span dir="ltr" style="font-size:13px;line-height:1.9">' +
    power.map(function(s){ return chainFormatSubset(s); }).join(' , ') + '</span></div>');

  if (mode === 'chain' || mode === 'both') {
    var chains = chainFindAllChains(power);
    if (onlyMax) {
      chains = chains.filter(function(c){ return chainIsMaximalChain(c, power); });
    }
    // prefer listing those that start with ∅ first
    chains.sort(function(a, b) {
      var a0 = power[a[0]].length, b0 = power[b[0]].length;
      if (a0 !== b0) return a0 - b0;
      if (a.length !== b.length) return b.length - a.length;
      return 0;
    });
    html.push('<h3 style="margin:16px 0 8px;color:#ec4899">⛓ زنجیرها' + (onlyMax ? ' (حداکثر / اشباع‌شده از ∅ تا S)' : ' (همه)') + ' — تعداد: ' + chains.length + '</h3>');
    if (!chains.length) {
      html.push('<span style="color:var(--muted)">موردی یافت نشد.</span>');
    } else {
      var limit = elems.length >= 5 && !onlyMax ? 200 : 500;
      var show = chains.slice(0, limit);
      show.forEach(function(c, idx) {
        var parts = c.map(function(i){ return chainFormatSubset(power[i]); });
        var isMax = chainIsMaximalChain(c, power);
        html.push('<div style="padding:8px 10px;margin:4px 0;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--card);direction:ltr;text-align:left;font-family:monospace;font-size:13px">' +
          '<span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11px">#' + (idx + 1) + (isMax ? ' ★ max' : '') + '</span><br>' +
          parts.join(' ⊂ ') + '</div>');
      });
      if (chains.length > limit) {
        html.push('<span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">… و ' + (chains.length - limit) + ' زنجیر دیگر (برای دیدن همه، «فقط حداکثرها» را فعال کنید یا |S| را کم کنید).</span>');
      }
    }
  }

  if (mode === 'anti' || mode === 'both') {
