    var antis = chainFindAllAntichains(power);
    if (onlyMax) {
      antis = antis.filter(function(a){ return chainIsMaximalAntichain(a, power); });
    }
    // sort by size desc then
    antis.sort(function(a, b) {
      if (a.length !== b.length) return b.length - a.length;
      return 0;
    });
    html.push('<h3 style="margin:16px 0 8px;color:#8b5cf6">⊥ پادزنجیرها' + (onlyMax ? ' (حداکثر)' : ' (همه، شامل خانوادهٔ تهی و تک‌عضوی‌ها)') + ' — تعداد: ' + antis.length + '</h3>');
    if (!antis.length) {
      html.push('<span style="color:var(--muted)">موردی یافت نشد.</span>');
    } else {
      var limitA = elems.length >= 5 && !onlyMax ? 200 : 500;
      var showA = antis.slice(0, limitA);
      showA.forEach(function(a, idx) {
        var parts = a.map(function(i){ return chainFormatSubset(power[i]); });
        var isMax = chainIsMaximalAntichain(a, power);
        var body = parts.length ? parts.join(' , ') : '∅ (خانوادهٔ تهی)';
        html.push('<div style="padding:8px 10px;margin:4px 0;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--card);direction:ltr;text-align:left;font-family:monospace;font-size:13px">' +
          '<span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:11px">#' + (idx + 1) + (isMax ? ' ★ max' : '') + ' |A|=' + a.length + '</span><br>{ ' + body + ' }</div>');
      });
      if (antis.length > limitA) {
        html.push('<span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">… و ' + (antis.length - limitA) + ' پادزنجیر دیگر.</span>');
      }
    }
  }

  html.push('<p style="margin-top:14px;font-size:12px;color:var(--muted)">نماد ∅ همان مجموعهٔ تهی است و در ابتدای زنجیرهای کامل از کوچک‌ترین عضو P(S) ظاهر می‌شود.</p>');

  res.style.display = 'block';
  res.innerHTML = html.join('');
  try { sfxSuccess(); } catch (e) {}
}


/* ——— فیزیک: ماشین حساب سرعت متوسط (Ultra 9.1) ——— */
var AVG_PRESETS = [
  { mode: 'speed', label: '۱۲۰ km در ۲ h', dist: 120, distU: 'km', time: 2, timeU: 'h' },
  { mode: 'speed', label: '۱۰۰ m در ۱۰ s', dist: 100, distU: 'm', time: 10, timeU: 's' },
  { mode: 'speed', label: '۴۰۰ m در ۵۰ s', dist: 400, distU: 'm', time: 50, timeU: 's' },
  { mode: 'dist', label: '۲۰ m/s · ۳۰ s', speed: 20, speedU: 'm_s', time: 30, timeU: 's' },
  { mode: 'dist', label: '۷۲ km/h · ۱٫۵ h', speed: 72, speedU: 'km_h', time: 1.5, timeU: 'h' },
  { mode: 'time', label: '۹۰ km با ۴۵ km/h', dist: 90, distU: 'km', speed: 45, speedU: 'km_h' },
  { mode: 'time', label: '۲۰۰ m با ۸ m/s', dist: 200, distU: 'm', speed: 8, speedU: 'm_s' }
];

function avgGetMode() {
  var el = document.getElementById('avgMode');
  return (el && el.value) || 'speed';
}

function avgSetMode(mode) {
  var hid = document.getElementById('avgMode');
  if (hid) hid.value = mode;
  var box = document.getElementById('avgModeFilters');
  if (box) {
    box.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn').forEach(function(b) {
      b.classList.toggle('active', b.getAttribute('data-avgmode') === mode);
    });
  }
  avgRenderInputs();
  avgRenderPresets();
  avgClearResult();
  try { sfxClick(); } catch (e) {}
}

function avgSelectHtml(id, options, selected) {
  var h = '<select id="' + id + '" style="min-width:110px;padding:10px 12px;border-radius:12px;border:2px solid var(--border);background:var(--card);color:var(--text);font-weight:800;font-size:13px">';
  options.forEach(function(o) {
    h += '<option value="' + o.v + '"' + (o.v === selected ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o.l + '</option>';
  });
  h += '</select>';
  return h;
}

function avgNumInput(id, val, placeholder) {
  return '<input type="number" id="' + id + '" value="' + (val != null ? val : '') + '" step="any" min="0" dir="ltr" placeholder="' + (placeholder || '') + '" style="flex:1;min-width:120px;padding:12px 14px;border:2px solid var(--border);border-radius:12px;background:var(--card);color:var(--text);font-size:16px;font-weight:800;font-family:\'Courier New\',monospace">';
}

function avgRenderInputs() {
  var box = document.getElementById('avgInputs');
  if (!box) return;
  var mode = avgGetMode();
  var distU = [
    { v: 'm', l: 'm (متر)' },
    { v: 'km', l: 'km (کیلومتر)' },
    { v: 'cm', l: 'cm' }
  ];
  var timeU = [
    { v: 's', l: 's (ثانیه)' },
    { v: 'min', l: 'min (دقیقه)' },
    { v: 'h', l: 'h (ساعت)' }
  ];
  var speedU = [
    { v: 'm_s', l: 'm/s' },
    { v: 'km_h', l: 'km/h' }
  ];
  var html = '';
  if (mode === 'speed') {
    html += '<div class="set-row" style="margin-bottom:10px"><label style="min-width:90px;font-weight:800">مسافت</label>' +
      avgNumInput('avgDist', 100, 'مثلاً 100') + avgSelectHtml('avgDistU', distU, 'm') + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="set-row"><label style="min-width:90px;font-weight:800">زمان</label>' +
      avgNumInput('avgTime', 10, 'مثلاً 10') + avgSelectHtml('avgTimeU', timeU, 's') + '</div>';
  } else if (mode === 'dist') {
    html += '<div class="set-row" style="margin-bottom:10px"><label style="min-width:90px;font-weight:800">سرعت</label>' +
      avgNumInput('avgSpeed', 20, 'مثلاً 20') + avgSelectHtml('avgSpeedU', speedU, 'm_s') + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="set-row"><label style="min-width:90px;font-weight:800">زمان</label>' +
      avgNumInput('avgTime', 30, 'مثلاً 30') + avgSelectHtml('avgTimeU', timeU, 's') + '</div>';
  } else {
    html += '<div class="set-row" style="margin-bottom:10px"><label style="min-width:90px;font-weight:800">مسافت</label>' +
      avgNumInput('avgDist', 90, 'مثلاً 90') + avgSelectHtml('avgDistU', distU, 'km') + '</div>';
    html += '<div class="set-row"><label style="min-width:90px;font-weight:800">سرعت</label>' +
      avgNumInput('avgSpeed', 45, 'مثلاً 45') + avgSelectHtml('avgSpeedU', speedU, 'km_h') + '</div>';
  }
  box.innerHTML = html;
}

function avgToMeters(val, u) {
  if (u === 'km') return val * 1000;
  if (u === 'cm') return val * 0.01;
  return val; // m
}
function avgFromMeters(m, u) {
  if (u === 'km') return m / 1000;
  if (u === 'cm') return m / 0.01;
  return m;
}
function avgToSeconds(val, u) {
  if (u === 'min') return val * 60;
  if (u === 'h') return val * 3600;
  return val; // s
}
function avgFromSeconds(s, u) {
  if (u === 'min') return s / 60;
  if (u === 'h') return s / 3600;
  return s;
}
function avgToMs(val, u) {
  if (u === 'km_h') return val / 3.6;
  return val; // m/s
}
function avgFmt(n) {
  if (!isFinite(n)) return '—';
  var a = Math.abs(n);
  if (a !== 0 && (a >= 1e6 || a < 1e-4)) return n.toExponential(4);
  var s = (Math.round(n * 1e8) / 1e8).toString();
  return s;
}

function avgClearResult() {
  var res = document.getElementById('avgResult');
  if (res) { res.style.display = 'none'; res.innerHTML = ''; }
}

function avgClear() {
  avgRenderInputs();
  avgClearResult();
  try { sfxClick(); } catch (e) {}
}

function runAvgSpeed() {
  var mode = avgGetMode();
  var res = document.getElementById('avgResult');
  if (!res) return;

  function num(id) {
    var v = parseFloat((document.getElementById(id) || {}).value);
    return v;
  }
  function val(id) {
    return (document.getElementById(id) || {}).value;
  }

  if (mode === 'speed') {
    var d = num('avgDist'), t = num('avgTime');
    var du = val('avgDistU'), tu = val('avgTimeU');
    if (!(d > 0) || !(t > 0)) {
      res.style.display = 'block';
      res.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef4444">مسافت و زمان باید عدد مثبت باشند.</span>';
      try { sfxError(); } catch (e) {}
      return;
    }
    var xm = avgToMeters(d, du);
    var ts = avgToSeconds(t, tu);
    var vms = xm / ts;
    var vkh = vms * 3.6;
    res.style.display = 'block';
    res.innerHTML =
      '<b style="color:var(--accent)">نتیجه: سرعت متوسط</b><br>' +
      '<div class="set-ans" dir="ltr" style="font-family:monospace;font-size:18px;font-weight:900;margin:10px 0">' +
      avgFmt(vms) + ' m/s<br>' + avgFmt(vkh) + ' km/h</div>' +
      '<b>گام‌ها:</b><br>' +
      '۱) مسافت به متر: ' + avgFmt(d) + ' ' + du + ' → <b dir="ltr">' + avgFmt(xm) + ' m</b><br>' +
      '۲) زمان به ثانیه: ' + avgFmt(t) + ' ' + tu + ' → <b dir="ltr">' + avgFmt(ts) + ' s</b><br>' +
      '۳) v = x / t = ' + avgFmt(xm) + ' / ' + avgFmt(ts) + ' = <b dir="ltr">' + avgFmt(vms) + ' m/s</b><br>' +
      '۴) km/h = (m/s) × ۳٫۶ = ' + avgFmt(vms) + ' × ۳٫۶ = <b dir="ltr">' + avgFmt(vkh) + ' km/h</b>';
    try { sfxSuccess(); } catch (e) {}
    return;
  }

  if (mode === 'dist') {
    var sp = num('avgSpeed'), t2 = num('avgTime');
    var su = val('avgSpeedU'), tu2 = val('avgTimeU');
    if (!(sp > 0) || !(t2 > 0)) {
      res.style.display = 'block';
      res.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef4444">سرعت و زمان باید عدد مثبت باشند.</span>';
      try { sfxError(); } catch (e) {}
      return;
    }
    var vms2 = avgToMs(sp, su);
    var ts2 = avgToSeconds(t2, tu2);
    var xm2 = vms2 * ts2;
    res.style.display = 'block';
    res.innerHTML =
      '<b style="color:var(--accent)">نتیجه: مسافت</b><br>' +
      '<div class="set-ans" dir="ltr" style="font-family:monospace;font-size:18px;font-weight:900;margin:10px 0">' +
      avgFmt(xm2) + ' m<br>' + avgFmt(xm2 / 1000) + ' km</div>' +
      '<b>گام‌ها:</b><br>' +
      '۱) سرعت به m/s: ' + avgFmt(sp) + ' ' + (su === 'km_h' ? 'km/h' : 'm/s') +
      (su === 'km_h' ? ' → ' + avgFmt(sp) + ' / ۳٫۶ = <b dir="ltr">' + avgFmt(vms2) + ' m/s</b>' : ' = <b dir="ltr">' + avgFmt(vms2) + ' m/s</b>') + '<br>' +
      '۲) زمان به ثانیه: ' + avgFmt(t2) + ' ' + tu2 + ' → <b dir="ltr">' + avgFmt(ts2) + ' s</b><br>' +
      '۳) x = v × t = ' + avgFmt(vms2) + ' × ' + avgFmt(ts2) + ' = <b dir="ltr">' + avgFmt(xm2) + ' m</b> = <b dir="ltr">' + avgFmt(xm2/1000) + ' km</b>';
    try { sfxSuccess(); } catch (e) {}
    return;
  }

  // time
  var d3 = num('avgDist'), sp3 = num('avgSpeed');
  var du3 = val('avgDistU'), su3 = val('avgSpeedU');
  if (!(d3 > 0) || !(sp3 > 0)) {
    res.style.display = 'block';
    res.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef4444">مسافت و سرعت باید عدد مثبت باشند.</span>';
    try { sfxError(); } catch (e) {}
    return;
  }
  var xm3 = avgToMeters(d3, du3);
  var vms3 = avgToMs(sp3, su3);
  var ts3 = xm3 / vms3;
  res.style.display = 'block';
  res.innerHTML =
    '<b style="color:var(--accent)">نتیجه: زمان</b><br>' +
    '<div class="set-ans" dir="ltr" style="font-family:monospace;font-size:18px;font-weight:900;margin:10px 0">' +
    avgFmt(ts3) + ' s<br>' +
    avgFmt(ts3 / 60) + ' min<br>' +
    avgFmt(ts3 / 3600) + ' h</div>' +
    '<b>گام‌ها:</b><br>' +
    '۱) مسافت به متر: ' + avgFmt(d3) + ' ' + du3 + ' → <b dir="ltr">' + avgFmt(xm3) + ' m</b><br>' +
    '۲) سرعت به m/s: ' + avgFmt(sp3) + ' ' + (su3 === 'km_h' ? 'km/h' : 'm/s') +
    (su3 === 'km_h' ? ' → <b dir="ltr">' + avgFmt(vms3) + ' m/s</b>' : ' = <b dir="ltr">' + avgFmt(vms3) + ' m/s</b>') + '<br>' +
    '۳) t = x / v = ' + avgFmt(xm3) + ' / ' + avgFmt(vms3) + ' = <b dir="ltr">' + avgFmt(ts3) + ' s</b>';
  try { sfxSuccess(); } catch (e) {}
}

function avgRenderPresets() {
  var box = document.getElementById('avgPresets');
  if (!box) return;
  var mode = avgGetMode();
  box.innerHTML = '';
  AVG_PRESETS.filter(function(p){ return p.mode === mode; }).forEach(function(p) {
    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.type = 'button';
    btn.className = 'filter-btn';
    btn.textContent = p.label;
    btn.onclick = function() {
      box.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn').forEach(function(x){ x.classList.remove('active'); });
      btn.classList.add('active');
      avgApplyPreset(p);
    };
    box.appendChild(btn);
  });
}

function avgApplyPreset(p) {
  avgSetMode(p.mode);
  // فقط پر کردن ورودی‌ها — محاسبه فقط با دکمه «محاسبه»
  setTimeout(function() {
    if (p.dist != null) {
      var d = document.getElementById('avgDist');
      var du = document.getElementById('avgDistU');
      if (d) d.value = p.dist;
      if (du && p.distU) du.value = p.distU;
    }
    if (p.time != null) {
      var t = document.getElementById('avgTime');
      var tu = document.getElementById('avgTimeU');
      if (t) t.value = p.time;
      if (tu && p.timeU) tu.value = p.timeU;
    }
    if (p.speed != null) {
      var s = document.getElementById('avgSpeed');
      var su = document.getElementById('avgSpeedU');
      if (s) s.value = p.speed;
      if (su && p.speedU) su.value = p.speedU;
    }
    avgClearResult();
  }, 20);
}

function initAvgSpeed() {
  if (!document.getElementById('avgMode')) return;
  var filters = document.getElementById('avgModeFilters');
  if (filters && !filters.dataset.bound) {
    filters.dataset.bound = '1';
    filters.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      var b = e.target.closest('[data-avgmode]');
      if (!b) return;
      avgSetMode(b.getAttribute('data-avgmode'));
    });
  }
  avgRenderInputs();
  avgRenderPresets();
}

(function() {
  function hook() {
    if (typeof showView !== 'function' || window.__avgHooked) return;
    window.__avgHooked = true;
    var _o = showView;
    window.showView = function(id) {
      _o(id);
      if (id === 'physAvgSpeed') {
        setTimeout(function(){ try { initAvgSpeed(); } catch(e){} }, 30);
      }
    };
  }
  setTimeout(hook, 0);
  setTimeout(hook, 500);
})();

      { id: 'grad', label: 'grad — گراد', sub: 'گراد' },
      { id: 'arcmin', label: '′ — دقیقه قوسی', sub: 'arcminute' },
      { id: 'arcsec', label: '″ — ثانیه قوسی', sub: 'arcsecond' },
      { id: 'turn', label: 'turn — دور کامل', sub: 'یک دور' }
    ],
    toBase: { rad:1, deg:Math.PI/180, grad:Math.PI/200, arcmin:Math.PI/10800, arcsec:Math.PI/648000, turn:Math.PI*2 }
  }
};

var UC_SHORTCUTS = [
  { cat: 'speed', from: 'm_s', to: 'km_h', val: 1, label: '۱ m/s → km/h' },
  { cat: 'speed', from: 'km_h', to: 'm_s', val: 36, label: '۳۶ km/h → m/s' },
  { cat: 'speed', from: 'km_h', to: 'm_s', val: 72, label: '۷۲ km/h → m/s' },
  { cat: 'speed', from: 'm_s', to: 'km_h', val: 4, label: '۴ m/s → km/h' },
  { cat: 'speed', from: 'mph', to: 'km_h', val: 60, label: '۶۰ mph → km/h' },
  { cat: 'length', from: 'km', to: 'm', val: 1, label: '۱ km → m' },
  { cat: 'length', from: 'in', to: 'cm', val: 1, label: '۱ inch → cm' },
  { cat: 'length', from: 'm', to: 'ft', val: 1, label: '۱ m → ft' },
  { cat: 'mass', from: 'kg', to: 'lb', val: 1, label: '۱ kg → lb' },
  { cat: 'mass', from: 'g', to: 'kg', val: 1000, label: '۱۰۰۰ g → kg' },
  { cat: 'temp', from: 'C', to: 'F', val: 0, label: '۰ °C → °F' },
  { cat: 'temp', from: 'C', to: 'K', val: 25, label: '۲۵ °C → K' },
  { cat: 'temp', from: 'F', to: 'C', val: 98.6, label: '۹۸٫۶ °F → °C' },
  { cat: 'energy', from: 'kcal', to: 'J', val: 1, label: '۱ kcal → J' },
  { cat: 'pressure', from: 'atm', to: 'Pa', val: 1, label: '۱ atm → Pa' },
  { cat: 'force', from: 'kgf', to: 'N', val: 1, label: '۱ kgf → N' },
  { cat: 'time', from: 'h', to: 's', val: 1, label: '۱ h → s' },
  { cat: 'angle', from: 'deg', to: 'rad', val: 180, label: '۱۸۰° → rad' }
];

function ucGetCat() {
  var el = document.getElementById('ucCategory');
  return (el && el.value) || 'speed';
}

function ucUnitOpts(cat) {
  var data = UC_UNITS[cat];
  if (!data) return [];
  return data.units.map(function(u) {
    return { value: u.id, label: u.label, sub: u.sub || '' };
  });
}

function ucDefaultPair(cat) {
  if (cat === 'speed') return ['m_s', 'km_h'];
  if (cat === 'temp') return ['C', 'F'];
  if (cat === 'length') return ['m', 'km'];
  if (cat === 'mass') return ['kg', 'g'];
  if (cat === 'time') return ['s', 'min'];
  if (cat === 'force') return ['N', 'kgf'];
  if (cat === 'energy') return ['J', 'kcal'];
  if (cat === 'power') return ['W', 'kW'];
  if (cat === 'pressure') return ['Pa', 'atm'];
  if (cat === 'area') return ['m2', 'cm2'];
  if (cat === 'volume') return ['L', 'm3'];
  if (cat === 'angle') return ['deg', 'rad'];
  var opts = ucUnitOpts(cat);
  return [opts[0] && opts[0].value, opts[1] && opts[1].value];
}

function ucFillSelects(preferFrom, preferTo) {
  var cat = ucGetCat();
  var opts = ucUnitOpts(cat);
  var pair = ucDefaultPair(cat);
  var fromV = preferFrom || pair[0];
  var toV = preferTo || pair[1];
  if (typeof setupSearchSelect === 'function') {
    setupSearchSelect('ss_ucFrom', opts, fromV);
    setupSearchSelect('ss_ucTo', opts, toV);
  }
  // bind change once
  var a = document.getElementById('ucFrom');
  var b = document.getElementById('ucTo');
  if (a && !a.dataset.ucBound) {
    a.dataset.ucBound = '1';
    a.addEventListener('change', function(){ try { ucClearResult(); } catch(e){} });
  }
  if (b && !b.dataset.ucBound) {
    b.dataset.ucBound = '1';
    b.addEventListener('change', function(){ try { ucClearResult(); } catch(e){} });
  }
}

function ucClearResult() {
  var main = document.getElementById('ucResultMain');
  var hint = document.getElementById('ucResultHint');
  if (main) main.textContent = '—';
  if (hint) hint.innerHTML = '<span style="color:var(--muted)">بعد از انتخاب یکا و مقدار، دکمه «محاسبه» را بزنید.</span>';
}
function ucOnCategoryChange() {
  ucFillSelects();
  ucRenderPresets();
  ucClearResult();
  try { sfxClick(); } catch (e) {}
}

function ucSetCategory(cat) {
  var hid = document.getElementById('ucCategory');
  if (hid) hid.value = cat;
  var box = document.getElementById('ucCatFilters');
  if (box) {
    box.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn').forEach(function(x) {
      x.classList.toggle('active', x.getAttribute('data-ucat') === cat);
    });
  }
  ucOnCategoryChange();
}

function ucFindUnit(cat, id) {
  var data = UC_UNITS[cat];
  if (!data) return null;
  for (var i = 0; i < data.units.length; i++) {
    if (data.units[i].id === id) return data.units[i];
  }
  return null;
}

function ucTempToKelvin(v, id) {
  if (id === 'K') return v;
  if (id === 'C') return v + 273.15;
  if (id === 'F') return (v - 32) * 5/9 + 273.15;
  if (id === 'R') return v * 5/9;
  return NaN;
}
function ucKelvinTo(k, id) {
  if (id === 'K') return k;
  if (id === 'C') return k - 273.15;
  if (id === 'F') return (k - 273.15) * 9/5 + 32;
  if (id === 'R') return k * 9/5;
  return NaN;
}

function ucFmt(n) {
  if (!isFinite(n)) return '—';
  var a = Math.abs(n);
  if (a !== 0 && (a >= 1e7 || a < 1e-4)) {
    return n.toExponential(6).replace(/\.?0+e/, 'e').replace(/e\+?/, 'e');
  }
  var s = n.toPrecision(10);
  if (s.indexOf('e') < 0 && s.indexOf('.') >= 0) {
    s = String(parseFloat(s));
  }
  return s;
}

function ucShortLabel(u) {
  if (!u) return '';
  var p = String(u.label || '').split('—')[0].trim();
  return p || u.id;
}

function ucConvert() {
  var cat = ucGetCat();
  var valEl = document.getElementById('ucValue');
  var fromEl = document.getElementById('ucFrom');
  var toEl = document.getElementById('ucTo');
  var main = document.getElementById('ucResultMain');
  var hint = document.getElementById('ucResultHint');
  if (!valEl || !fromEl || !toEl || !main) return;
  var raw = parseFloat(valEl.value);
  if (!isFinite(raw)) {
    main.textContent = '—';
    if (hint) hint.textContent = 'مقدار عددی معتبر وارد کنید.';
    return;
  }
  var fromU = ucFindUnit(cat, fromEl.value);
  var toU = ucFindUnit(cat, toEl.value);
  if (!fromU || !toU) {
    main.textContent = '—';
    return;
  }
  var result;
  var data = UC_UNITS[cat];
  if (cat === 'temp') {
    var k = ucTempToKelvin(raw, fromU.id);
    result = ucKelvinTo(k, toU.id);
  } else {
    var tb = data.toBase || {};
    var base = raw * (tb[fromU.id] != null ? tb[fromU.id] : 1);
    result = base / (tb[toU.id] != null ? tb[toU.id] : 1);
  }
  main.textContent = ucFmt(raw) + ' ' + ucShortLabel(fromU) + '  =  ' + ucFmt(result) + ' ' + ucShortLabel(toU);
  if (hint) {
    var lines = [];
    lines.push('<b>دسته:</b> ' + (data.label || cat));
    if (cat === 'speed' && fromU.id === 'm_s' && toU.id === 'km_h') {
      lines.push('فرمول: km/h = (m/s) × ۳٫۶  →  ' + ucFmt(raw) + ' × ۳٫۶ = <b>' + ucFmt(result) + '</b>');
    } else if (cat === 'speed' && fromU.id === 'km_h' && toU.id === 'm_s') {
      lines.push('فرمول: m/s = (km/h) ÷ ۳٫۶  →  ' + ucFmt(raw) + ' ÷ ۳٫۶ = <b>' + ucFmt(result) + '</b>');
    } else if (cat === 'temp') {
      lines.push('تبدیل از طریق مقیاس کلوین انجام شد.');
    } else {
      lines.push('ضریب تبدیل نسبت به یکای پایه دسته اعمال شد.');
    }
    lines.push('<span style="font-size:12px">جستجو در لیست یکاها مثل مقایسه شعاع — تایپ کنید تا فیلتر شود.</span>');
    hint.innerHTML = lines.join('<br>');
  }
}

function ucSwap() {
  var fromId = (document.getElementById('ucFrom') || {}).value;
  var toId = (document.getElementById('ucTo') || {}).value;
  if (!fromId || !toId) return;
  if (typeof ssSet === 'function') {
    ssSet('ss_ucFrom', toId);
    ssSet('ss_ucTo', fromId);
  } else {
    var a = document.getElementById('ss_ucFrom');
    var b = document.getElementById('ss_ucTo');
    if (a && a._ssSet) a._ssSet(toId);
    if (b && b._ssSet) b._ssSet(fromId);
  }
  ucClearResult();
  try { sfxClick(); } catch (e) {}
}

function ucApplyShortcut(cat, from, to, val) {
  ucSetCategory(cat);
  ucFillSelects(from, to);
  var valEl = document.getElementById('ucValue');
  if (valEl) valEl.value = val;
  ucRenderPresets();
  ucClearResult();
  try { sfxClick(); } catch (e) {}
}

function ucRenderPresets() {
  var box = document.getElementById('ucPresets');
  if (!box) return;
  box.innerHTML = '';
  var cat = ucGetCat();
  var list = UC_SHORTCUTS.filter(function(sc) { return sc.cat === cat; });
  if (!list.length) list = UC_SHORTCUTS.slice(0, 6);
  list.forEach(function(sc) {
    var btn = document.createElement('button');
    btn.type = 'button';
    btn.className = 'filter-btn';
    btn.textContent = sc.label;
    btn.setAttribute('data-uf', sc.from);
    btn.setAttribute('data-ut', sc.to);
    btn.setAttribute('data-uv', String(sc.val));
    btn.setAttribute('data-uc', sc.cat);
    btn.onclick = function() {
      box.querySelectorAll('.filter-btn').forEach(function(x){ x.classList.remove('active'); });
      btn.classList.add('active');
      ucApplyShortcut(sc.cat, sc.from, sc.to, sc.val);
    };
    box.appendChild(btn);
  });
}

function initUnitConverter() {
  if (!document.getElementById('ucCategory')) return;
  var filters = document.getElementById('ucCatFilters');
  if (filters && !filters.dataset.bound) {
    filters.dataset.bound = '1';
    filters.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      var b = e.target.closest('[data-ucat]');
      if (!b) return;
      ucSetCategory(b.getAttribute('data-ucat'));
    });
  }
  ucFillSelects();
  ucRenderPresets();
  ucClearResult();
}

(function() {
  function hookShowView() {
    if (typeof showView !== 'function' || window.__ucHooked) return;
    window.__ucHooked = true;
    var _origShow = showView;
    window.showView = function(id) {
      _origShow(id);
      if (id === 'physUnits') {
        setTimeout(function() { try { initUnitConverter(); } catch (e) {} }, 30);
      }
    };
  }
  setTimeout(hookShowView, 0);
  setTimeout(hookShowView, 500);
})();

/* ——— فیزیک: چرخ غلتان · نقطه A ——— */
function parseWheelR() {
  var raw = String((document.getElementById('wheelR') || {}).value || '').trim();
  if (!raw) return { ok: false, symbolic: false, value: NaN, label: '' };
  // نماد r یا R
  if (/^r$/i.test(raw)) {
    return { ok: true, symbolic: true, value: 1, label: 'r' };
  }
  // اجازهٔ πr یا 2r ساده؟ فعلاً فقط عدد یا خود r
  var num = parseFloat(raw.replace(/,/g, '.'));
  if (!(num > 0) || isNaN(num)) {
    return { ok: false, symbolic: false, value: NaN, label: raw };
  }
  return { ok: true, symbolic: false, value: num, label: String(num) };
}

function onWheelRInput() {
  wheelClearResult();
}

function wheelFmtSym(coef, symbolic) {
  // coef is the numeric coefficient when r=1; format as expression in r or number
  var c = Number(coef);
  if (!symbolic) return wheelFmtNum(c, 8);
  // symbolic: expression * r
  if (Math.abs(c) < 1e-12) return '0';
  var abs = Math.abs(c);
  var sign = c < 0 ? '-' : '';
  // nice multiples of π
  var piv = c / Math.PI;
  if (Math.abs(piv - Math.round(piv)) < 1e-8) {
    var k = Math.round(piv);
    if (k === 0) return '0';
    if (k === 1) return 'πr';
    if (k === -1) return '−πr';
    return (k < 0 ? '−' : '') + Math.abs(k) + 'πr';
  }
  // half π etc
  var piv2 = c / (Math.PI / 2);
  if (Math.abs(piv2 - Math.round(piv2)) < 1e-8) {
    var k2 = Math.round(piv2);
    if (k2 === 1) return '(π/2)r';
    if (k2 === -1) return '−(π/2)r';
    if (k2 === 2) return 'πr';
    if (k2 === 3) return '(3π/2)r';
    return (k2 < 0 ? '−' : '') + '(' + Math.abs(k2) + 'π/2)r';
  }
  // general: number × r
  var s = wheelFmtNum(abs, 8);
  if (Math.abs(abs - 1) < 1e-10) return sign + 'r';
  return sign + s + 'r';
}

function wheelFmtNum(x, d) {
  if (d == null) d = 6;
  var s = Number(x).toFixed(d);
  s = s.replace(/(\.\d*?)0+$/, '$1').replace(/\.$/, '');
  return s;
}

function getWheelAngleDeg() {
  var v = parseFloat((document.getElementById('wheelAngle') || {}).value);
  if (isNaN(v)) v = 0;
  // normalize 0..360
  v = v % 360;
  if (v < 0) v += 360;
  return v;
}

function wheelClearResult() {
  var res = document.getElementById('wheelResult');
  var wrap = document.getElementById('wheelFigWrap');
  if (res) { res.style.display = 'none'; res.innerHTML = ''; }
  // شکل و جواب فقط بعد از زدن دکمهٔ محاسبه
  if (wrap) wrap.style.display = 'none';
  _wheelLastArgs = null;
  _wheelDrawMeta = null;
}

/** فقط برای انتخاب نقطهٔ A روی شکل — بدون نمایش جواب */
function wheelPreviewOnly() {
  var R = parseWheelR();
  if (!R.ok) R = { ok: true, symbolic: true, value: 1, label: 'r' };
  var n = parseFloat((document.getElementById('wheelN') || {}).value);
  if (isNaN(n) || n < 0.1) n = 1;
  if (n > 100) n = 100;
  var angleDeg = getWheelAngleDeg();
  var P = wheelPointPositions(R.value, n, angleDeg);
  var wrap = document.getElementById('wheelFigWrap');
  if (wrap) wrap.style.display = 'block';
  var res = document.getElementById('wheelResult');
  if (res) { res.style.display = 'none'; }
  try {
    drawWheelFigure(R.value, n, angleDeg, P, R);
  } catch (e) {}
}

function setWheelAngle(deg) {
  var el = document.getElementById('wheelAngle');
  if (el) el.value = String(deg);
  var hint = document.getElementById('wheelAngleHint');
  if (hint) hint.textContent = 'زاویهٔ فعلی A: ' + wheelFmtNum(deg, 1) + '°';
  try { sfxClick(); } catch (e) {}
  wheelClearResult();
}

function onWheelAngleInput() {
  var deg = getWheelAngleDeg();
  var hint = document.getElementById('wheelAngleHint');
  if (hint) hint.textContent = 'زاویهٔ فعلی A: ' + wheelFmtNum(deg, 1) + '°';
  wheelClearResult();
}

function onWheelRInput() {
  wheelClearResult();
}

/** موقعیت شروع و پایان نقطه A
 *  φ از پایین، خلاف عقربه (درجه → رادیان)
 *  غلتش به راست: چرخش ساعت‌گرد به اندازه turn
 */
function wheelPointPositions(r, n, angleDeg) {
  var turn = 2 * Math.PI * n;
  var phi = (angleDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  // شروع: مرکز (0,r)
  var x0 = r * Math.sin(phi);
  var y0 = r * (1 - Math.cos(phi));
  // پایان بعد از چرخش ساعت‌گرد turn
  var x1 = r * turn + r * Math.sin(phi - turn);
  var y1 = r * (1 - Math.cos(phi - turn));
  return {
    turn: turn,
    x0: x0, y0: y0,
    x1: x1, y1: y1,
    dx: x1 - x0,
    dy: y1 - y0,
    dist: Math.sqrt((x1 - x0) * (x1 - x0) + (y1 - y0) * (y1 - y0)),
    centerTravel: n * 2 * Math.PI * r,
    circumference: 2 * Math.PI * r
  };
}

function runWheelRoll(silent) {
  var R = parseWheelR();
  var r = R.value; // برای رسم همیشه عدد (اگر نمادین باشد 1 و برچسب r)
  var n = parseFloat((document.getElementById('wheelN') || {}).value);
  var angleDeg = getWheelAngleDeg();
  var res = document.getElementById('wheelResult');
  var wrap = document.getElementById('wheelFigWrap');
  if (!res) return;

  if (!R.ok) {
    if (!silent) {
      res.style.display = 'block';
      res.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef4444">شعاع را عدد مثبت بگذار یا خود نماد <b dir="ltr">r</b> را بنویس.</span>';
    }
    return;
  }
  if (isNaN(n) || n < 0.1 || n > 100) {
    if (!silent) {
      res.style.display = 'block';
      res.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef4444">تعداد دور n باید بین ۰٫۱ و ۱۰۰ باشد.</span>';
    }
    try { drawWheelFigure(r > 0 ? r : 1, 1, angleDeg, null, R); } catch (e) {}
    return;
  }

  // هم‌سان‌سازی نمایش زاویه در اینپوت
  var angEl = document.getElementById('wheelAngle');
  if (angEl && !silent) {
    // keep user typing; only normalize on blur-like calc
  }
  var hint = document.getElementById('wheelAngleHint');
  if (hint) hint.textContent = 'زاویهٔ فعلی A: ' + wheelFmtNum(angleDeg, 1) + '°';

  var P = wheelPointPositions(r, n, angleDeg);
  var fmt = wheelFmtNum;

  var posName = '';
  var ad = Math.round(angleDeg * 10) / 10;
  if (Math.abs(ad) < 0.15 || Math.abs(ad - 360) < 0.15) posName = ' (پایین‌ترین نقطه)';
  else if (Math.abs(ad - 90) < 0.15) posName = ' (سمت راست)';
  else if (Math.abs(ad - 180) < 0.15) posName = ' (بالاترین نقطه)';
  else if (Math.abs(ad - 270) < 0.15) posName = ' (سمت چپ)';

  var sym = R.symbolic;
  var html = [];
  var rShow = sym ? 'r' : fmt(r);
  var x0s = wheelFmtSym(P.x0, sym), y0s = wheelFmtSym(P.y0, sym);
  var x1s = wheelFmtSym(P.x1, sym), y1s = wheelFmtSym(P.y1, sym);
  var dxs = wheelFmtSym(P.dx, sym), dys = wheelFmtSym(P.dy, sym);
  var distS = sym ? wheelFmtSym(P.dist, true) : fmt(P.dist, 8);
  var circS = wheelFmtSym(P.circumference, sym);
  var cenS = wheelFmtSym(P.centerTravel, sym);
  var dx2 = P.dx * P.dx, dy2 = P.dy * P.dy, sum2 = dx2 + dy2;

  html.push('<div style="font-size:14px;line-height:1.85;font-weight:700">');

  // --- مفروضات ---
  html.push('<div class="set-ans" style="margin-bottom:10px">');
  html.push('<b>مفروضات</b>');
  html.push('<div dir="ltr" style="font-family:\'Courier New\',monospace;font-weight:800;margin-top:6px">');
  html.push('r = ' + rShow + '<br>');
  html.push('n = ' + fmt(n) + '<br>');
  html.push('∠A₀ = ' + fmt(angleDeg, 2) + '°' + (posName || '') + '<br>');
  html.push('غلتش بدون لغزش روی خط افقی');
  html.push('</div></div>');

  // --- گام 1 ---
  html.push('<div class="set-ans" style="margin-bottom:10px">');
  html.push('<b>(۱) محیط چرخ</b>');
  html.push('<div dir="ltr" style="font-family:\'Courier New\',monospace;font-weight:800;margin-top:6px">');
  html.push('C = 2πr = ' + circS);
  html.push('</div></div>');

  // --- گام 2 ---
  html.push('<div class="set-ans" style="margin-bottom:10px">');
  html.push('<b>(۲) جابه‌جایی مرکز چرخ</b>');
  html.push('<div dir="ltr" style="font-family:\'Courier New\',monospace;font-weight:800;margin-top:6px">');
  html.push('چون لغزش نداریم:<br>');
  html.push('s = n · C = n · 2πr = ' + cenS + '<br>');
  html.push('O : (0 , r) → O′ : (' + cenS + ' , ' + rShow + ')');
  html.push('</div></div>');

  // --- گام 3 ---
  html.push('<div class="set-ans" style="margin-bottom:10px">');
  html.push('<b>(۳) مختصات A و A′</b>');
  html.push('<div dir="ltr" style="font-family:\'Courier New\',monospace;font-weight:800;margin-top:6px">');
  html.push('A = (x<sub>A</sub> , y<sub>A</sub>) = ( ' + x0s + ' , ' + y0s + ' )<br>');
  html.push('A′ = (x<sub>A′</sub> , y<sub>A′</sub>) = ( ' + x1s + ' , ' + y1s + ' )');
  html.push('</div></div>');

  // --- گام 4 ---
  html.push('<div class="set-ans" style="margin-bottom:10px">');
  html.push('<b>(۴) مؤلفه‌های بردار جابه‌جایی</b>');
  html.push('<div dir="ltr" style="font-family:\'Courier New\',monospace;font-weight:800;margin-top:6px">');
  html.push('Δx = x<sub>A′</sub> − x<sub>A</sub> = ' + x1s + ' − (' + x0s + ') = ' + dxs + '<br>');
  html.push('Δy = y<sub>A′</sub> − y<sub>A</sub> = ' + y1s + ' − (' + y0s + ') = ' + dys);
  html.push('</div></div>');

  // --- گام 5 فیثاغورس کامل ---
  html.push('<div class="set-ans" style="margin-bottom:10px;border-color:rgba(249,115,22,0.35);background:rgba(249,115,22,0.08)">');
  html.push('<b>(۵) اندازهٔ جابه‌جایی با فیثاغورس</b>');
  html.push('<div dir="ltr" style="font-family:\'Courier New\',monospace;font-weight:800;margin-top:6px">');
  html.push('|AA′|² = (Δx)² + (Δy)²<br><br>');
  if (!sym) {
    html.push('|AA′|² = (' + fmt(P.dx) + ')² + (' + fmt(P.dy) + ')²<br>');
    html.push('|AA′|² = ' + fmt(dx2) + ' + ' + fmt(dy2) + '<br>');
    html.push('|AA′|² = ' + fmt(sum2) + '<br><br>');
    html.push('|AA′| = √(' + fmt(sum2) + ') = <span style="color:#ea580c;font-size:1.15em">' + distS + '</span>');
  } else {
    html.push('|AA′| = √( (Δx)² + (Δy)² ) = <span style="color:#ea580c;font-size:1.15em">' + distS + '</span>');
  }
  html.push('</div></div>');

  // --- حالت‌های خاص ---
  var special = [];
  if (Math.abs(angleDeg) < 0.2 || Math.abs(angleDeg - 360) < 0.2) {
    if (Math.abs(n - Math.round(n)) < 1e-8) {
      special.push('∠A₀ = 0° و n ∈ ℕ ⇒ y<sub>A</sub> = y<sub>A′</sub> = 0<br>|AA′| = |Δx| = n · 2πr = ' + cenS);
    } else if (Math.abs(n * 2 - Math.round(n * 2)) < 1e-8 && (Math.round(n * 2) % 2 === 1)) {
      special.push('∠A₀ = 0° و n = k+½ ⇒ A′ در بالاترین نقطه<br>Δy = 2r<br>|AA′| = √( (Δx)² + (2r)² ) = ' + distS);
    }
  }
  if (Math.abs(angleDeg - 180) < 0.2 && Math.abs(n - Math.round(n)) < 1e-8) {
    special.push('∠A₀ = 180° و n ∈ ℕ ⇒ نقطه از بالا شروع و بعد از دور کامل دوباره بالا<br>|AA′| = n · 2πr = ' + cenS);
  }
  if (special.length) {
    html.push('<div class="set-ans" style="margin-bottom:10px">');
    html.push('<b>(۶) حالت خاص</b>');
    html.push('<div dir="ltr" style="font-family:\'Courier New\',monospace;font-weight:800;margin-top:6px">');
    html.push(special.join('<br><br>'));
    html.push('</div></div>');
  }

  // --- جواب نهایی ---
  html.push('<div class="set-ans" style="border-color:rgba(16,185,129,0.4);background:rgba(16,185,129,0.1)">');
  html.push('<b>جواب نهایی</b>');
  html.push('<div dir="ltr" style="font-family:\'Courier New\',monospace;font-weight:900;font-size:1.2em;margin-top:6px;color:#059669">');
  html.push('|AA′| = ' + distS);
  html.push('</div>');
  html.push('<div dir="ltr" style="font-family:\'Courier New\',monospace;font-weight:800;margin-top:8px;font-size:13px">');
  html.push('A′ = ( ' + x1s + ' , ' + y1s + ' )');
  html.push('</div></div>');

  html.push('</div>');

  res.style.display = 'block';
  res.innerHTML = html.join('');
  if (wrap) wrap.style.display = 'block';
  try { drawWheelFigure(r, n, angleDeg, P, R); } catch (e) { console.warn(e); }
  if (!silent) { try { sfxSuccess(); } catch (e) {} }
}


/* زوم / پن شکل چرخ — زوم حول نشانه‌گر */
var _wheelView = { scale: 1, panX: 0, panY: 0 };
var _wheelDrawMeta = null;
var _wheelLastArgs = null;

function wheelResetView() {
  _wheelView.scale = 1;
  _wheelView.panX = 0;
  _wheelView.panY = 0;
}

function drawWheelFigure(r, n, angleDeg, P, Rinfo) {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('wheelCanvas');
  if (!canvas) return;
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  var W = canvas.width, H = canvas.height;

  if (!P) P = wheelPointPositions(r, n, angleDeg);
  _wheelLastArgs = { r: r, n: n, angleDeg: angleDeg, P: P, Rinfo: Rinfo };

  var dark = document.body.classList.contains('dark');
  ctx.setTransform(1, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0);
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);
  ctx.fillStyle = dark ? '#0f172a' : '#f8fafc';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

  var marginL = 48, marginR = 28, marginT = 28, marginB = 40;
  var groundY = H - marginB;

  var xMin = Math.min(0, P.x0, P.x1, -r * 0.2) - r * 0.15;
  var xMax = Math.max(r * P.turn + r, P.x0, P.x1) + r * 0.4;
  var worldW = Math.max(xMax - xMin, 1);
  var worldH = Math.max(r * 2.5, Math.max(P.y0, P.y1) + r * 0.3, 1);
  var baseScale = Math.min((W - marginL - marginR) / worldW, (groundY - marginT) / worldH);
  if (!(baseScale > 0)) baseScale = 1;

  var scale = baseScale * _wheelView.scale;

  function X(x) { return marginL + (x - xMin) * scale + _wheelView.panX; }
  function Y(y) { return groundY - y * scale + _wheelView.panY; }

  _wheelDrawMeta = {
    r: r, n: n, angleDeg: angleDeg,
    scale: scale, baseScale: baseScale,
    xMin: xMin, groundY: groundY, marginL: marginL,
    c0x: 0, c0y: r, canvas: canvas,
    W: W, H: H
  };

  // clip content
  ctx.save();
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.rect(0, 0, W, H);
  ctx.clip();

  // ground
  ctx.strokeStyle = dark ? '#475569' : '#94a3b8';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(-40, Y(0));
  ctx.lineTo(W + 40, Y(0));
  ctx.stroke();

  // path of A
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.strokeStyle = dark ? '#a78bfa' : '#8b5cf6';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  var steps = Math.max(50, Math.min(600, Math.ceil(Math.max(P.turn, 0.1) * 50)));
  var phi = (angleDeg * Math.PI) / 180;
  for (var i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
    var th = (P.turn * i) / steps;
    var px = r * th + r * Math.sin(phi - th);
    var py = r * (1 - Math.cos(phi - th));
    if (i === 0) ctx.moveTo(X(px), Y(py));
    else ctx.lineTo(X(px), Y(py));
  }
  ctx.stroke();

  var c0x = 0, c0y = r;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.strokeStyle = dark ? '#64748b' : '#94a3b8';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.setLineDash([6, 5]);
  ctx.arc(X(c0x), Y(c0y), Math.max(1, r * scale), 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.setLineDash([]);
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.fillStyle = dark ? 'rgba(249,115,22,0.06)' : 'rgba(249,115,22,0.07)';
  ctx.arc(X(c0x), Y(c0y), Math.max(1, r * scale), 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(X(c0x), Y(c0y));
  ctx.lineTo(X(P.x0), Y(P.y0));
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#f97316';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.stroke();

  var c1x = r * P.turn, c1y = r;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.strokeStyle = dark ? '#94a3b8' : '#475569';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2.5;
  ctx.arc(X(c1x), Y(c1y), Math.max(1, r * scale), 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.moveTo(X(c1x), Y(c1y));
  ctx.lineTo(X(P.x1), Y(P.y1));
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#2563eb';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#64748b';
  ctx.arc(X(c1x), Y(c1y), 3.5, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();

  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#f97316';
  ctx.arc(X(P.x0), Y(P.y0), 7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = dark ? '#fdba74' : '#c2410c';
  ctx.font = 'bold 13px Tahoma, sans-serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText('A  ' + wheelFmtNum(angleDeg, 1) + '°', X(P.x0) + 10, Y(P.y0) - 8);

  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.fillStyle = '#2563eb';
  ctx.arc(X(P.x1), Y(P.y1), 7, 0, Math.PI * 2);
  ctx.fill();
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#fff';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.fillStyle = dark ? '#93c5fd' : '#1d4ed8';
  ctx.fillText("A'", X(P.x1) + 10, Y(P.y1) - 8);

  ctx.beginPath();
  ctx.setLineDash([4, 4]);
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#ef4444';
  ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
  ctx.moveTo(X(P.x0), Y(P.y0));
  ctx.lineTo(X(P.x1), Y(P.y1));
  ctx.stroke();
  ctx.setLineDash([]);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#ef4444';
  ctx.font = 'bold 12px Tahoma, sans-serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.fillText('|AA′| = ' + (Rinfo && Rinfo.symbolic ? wheelFmtSym(P.dist, true) : wheelFmtNum(P.dist, 4)), X((P.x0 + P.x1) / 2), Y((P.y0 + P.y1) / 2) - 10);

  ctx.restore();

  var rLab = (Rinfo && Rinfo.symbolic) ? 'r' : String(r);
  ctx.fillStyle = dark ? '#94a3b8' : '#64748b';
  ctx.font = '11px Tahoma, sans-serif';
  ctx.textAlign = 'right';
  ctx.fillText('n=' + n + ' · r=' + rLab + ' · zoom ' + wheelFmtNum(_wheelView.scale, 2) + '×', W - marginR, 16);
}

function wheelRedraw() {
  if (!_wheelLastArgs) return;
  var a = _wheelLastArgs;
  drawWheelFigure(a.r, a.n, a.angleDeg, a.P, a.Rinfo);
}

function wheelZoomAt(screenX, screenY, factor) {
  var m = _wheelDrawMeta;
  if (!m) return;
  var oldS = _wheelView.scale;
  var newS = Math.max(0.25, Math.min(16, oldS * factor));
  if (Math.abs(newS - oldS) < 1e-9) return;
  var ratio = newS / oldS;
  var ml = m.marginL;
  var gy = m.groundY;
  var oldPanX = _wheelView.panX;
  var oldPanY = _wheelView.panY;
  // screenX = ml + (wx - xMin)*base*oldS + oldPanX
  // screenX = ml + (wx - xMin)*base*newS + newPanX
  // => newPanX = screenX - ml - ratio * (screenX - ml - oldPanX)
  _wheelView.panX = screenX - ml - ratio * (screenX - ml - oldPanX);
  // screenY = gy - wy*base*oldS + oldPanY
  // screenY = gy - wy*base*newS + newPanY
  // wy*base*oldS = gy + oldPanY - screenY
  // newPanY = screenY - gy + ratio * (gy + oldPanY - screenY)
  _wheelView.panY = screenY - gy + ratio * (gy + oldPanY - screenY);
  _wheelView.scale = newS;
  wheelRedraw();
}

function initWheelCanvasClick() {
  var canvas = document.getElementById('wheelCanvas');
  if (!canvas || canvas.dataset.wheelBound === '1') return;
  canvas.dataset.wheelBound = '1';

  function clientToCanvas(ev, touchIdx) {
    var rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    var sx = canvas.width / Math.max(rect.width, 1);
    var sy = canvas.height / Math.max(rect.height, 1);
    var clientX, clientY;
    if (ev.touches && ev.touches.length) {
      var t = ev.touches[typeof touchIdx === 'number' ? touchIdx : 0];
      clientX = t.clientX; clientY = t.clientY;
    } else if (ev.changedTouches && ev.changedTouches.length) {
      var t2 = ev.changedTouches[typeof touchIdx === 'number' ? touchIdx : 0];
      clientX = t2.clientX; clientY = t2.clientY;
    } else {
      clientX = ev.clientX; clientY = ev.clientY;
    }
    return { x: (clientX - rect.left) * sx, y: (clientY - rect.top) * sy };
  }

  canvas.addEventListener('wheel', function(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    var pt = clientToCanvas(ev);
    var factor = ev.deltaY < 0 ? 1.12 : (1 / 1.12);
    wheelZoomAt(pt.x, pt.y, factor);
  }, { passive: false });

  canvas.addEventListener('dblclick', function(ev) {
    ev.preventDefault();
    wheelResetView();
    wheelRedraw();
  });

  var dragging = false;
  var lastX = 0, lastY = 0;
  var moved = false;

  canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', function(ev) {
    if (ev.button !== 0 && ev.button !== 1 && ev.button !== 2) return;
    dragging = true;
    moved = false;
    var pt = clientToCanvas(ev);
    lastX = pt.x; lastY = pt.y;
  });
  window.addEventListener('mousemove', function(ev) {
    if (!dragging) return;
    var pt = clientToCanvas(ev);
    var dx = pt.x - lastX, dy = pt.y - lastY;
    if (Math.abs(dx) + Math.abs(dy) > 2) moved = true;
    _wheelView.panX += dx;
    _wheelView.panY += dy;
    lastX = pt.x; lastY = pt.y;
    wheelRedraw();
  });
  window.addEventListener('mouseup', function(ev) {
    if (!dragging) return;
    dragging = false;
    if (!moved && ev.button === 0) setAngleFromEvent(ev);
  });
  canvas.addEventListener('contextmenu', function(ev) { ev.preventDefault(); });

  function setAngleFromEvent(ev) {
    if (!_wheelDrawMeta) {
      try { wheelPreviewOnly(); } catch (e) {}
    }
    var m = _wheelDrawMeta;
    if (!m) return;
    var pt = clientToCanvas(ev);
    var wx = m.xMin + (pt.x - m.marginL - _wheelView.panX) / m.scale;
    var wy = (m.groundY + _wheelView.panY - pt.y) / m.scale;
    var dx = wx - m.c0x;
    var dy = wy - m.c0y;
    if (Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) < m.r * 0.1) return;
    var deg = Math.atan2(dx, -dy) * 180 / Math.PI;
    if (deg < 0) deg += 360;
    deg = Math.round(deg * 10) / 10;
    var el = document.getElementById('wheelAngle');
    if (el) el.value = String(deg);
    var hint = document.getElementById('wheelAngleHint');
    if (hint) hint.textContent = 'زاویهٔ فعلی A: ' + wheelFmtNum(deg, 1) + '°';
    try { sfxClick(); } catch (e) {}
    // زاویه تنظیم شد؛ شکل پیش‌نمایش بدون جواب
    try { wheelPreviewOnly(); } catch (e) {}
    var res = document.getElementById('wheelResult');
    if (res) { res.style.display = 'none'; res.innerHTML = ''; }
  }

  var pinchStartDist = 0;
  var pinchStartScale = 1;
  var pinchStartPanX = 0, pinchStartPanY = 0;
  var pinchOrigin = { x: 0, y: 0 };
  var touchPanLast = null;
  var movedTouch = false;

  function touchDist(ev) {
    var a = ev.touches[0], b = ev.touches[1];
    var dx = a.clientX - b.clientX, dy = a.clientY - b.clientY;
    return Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);
  }
  function touchCenterCanvas(ev) {
    var rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    var sx = canvas.width / Math.max(rect.width, 1);
    var sy = canvas.height / Math.max(rect.height, 1);
    var a = ev.touches[0], b = ev.touches[1];
    return {
      x: (((a.clientX + b.clientX) / 2) - rect.left) * sx,
      y: (((a.clientY + b.clientY) / 2) - rect.top) * sy
    };
  }

  canvas.addEventListener('touchstart', function(ev) {
    if (ev.touches.length === 2) {
      pinchStartDist = touchDist(ev) || 1;
      pinchStartScale = _wheelView.scale;
      pinchStartPanX = _wheelView.panX;
      pinchStartPanY = _wheelView.panY;
      pinchOrigin = touchCenterCanvas(ev);
      touchPanLast = { x: pinchOrigin.x, y: pinchOrigin.y };
      movedTouch = true;
      if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
    } else if (ev.touches.length === 1) {
      touchPanLast = clientToCanvas(ev);
      movedTouch = false;
    }
  }, { passive: false });

  canvas.addEventListener('touchmove', function(ev) {
    if (ev.touches.length === 2) {
      var d = touchDist(ev) || 1;
      var target = Math.max(0.25, Math.min(16, pinchStartScale * (d / pinchStartDist)));
      var cen = touchCenterCanvas(ev);
      // زوم حول نقطهٔ شروع پینچ، بعد پن با جابه‌جایی مرکز
      var ratio = target / pinchStartScale;
      var ml = (_wheelDrawMeta && _wheelDrawMeta.marginL) || 48;
      var gy = (_wheelDrawMeta && _wheelDrawMeta.groundY) || (canvas.height - 40);
      var ox = pinchOrigin.x, oy = pinchOrigin.y;
      _wheelView.scale = target;
      _wheelView.panX = ox - ml - ratio * (ox - ml - pinchStartPanX) + (cen.x - ox);
      _wheelView.panY = oy - gy + ratio * (gy + pinchStartPanY - oy) + (cen.y - oy);
      touchPanLast = cen;
      wheelRedraw();
      if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
    } else if (ev.touches.length === 1 && touchPanLast) {
      var pt = clientToCanvas(ev);
      var dx = pt.x - touchPanLast.x, dy = pt.y - touchPanLast.y;
      if (Math.abs(dx) + Math.abs(dy) > 4) movedTouch = true;
      _wheelView.panX += dx;
      _wheelView.panY += dy;
      touchPanLast = pt;
      wheelRedraw();
      if (ev.cancelable) ev.preventDefault();
    }
  }, { passive: false });

  canvas.addEventListener('touchend', function(ev) {
    if (ev.touches.length === 0) {
      if (!movedTouch && ev.changedTouches && ev.changedTouches.length === 1) {
        setAngleFromEvent({
          clientX: ev.changedTouches[0].clientX,
          clientY: ev.changedTouches[0].clientY
        });
      }
      pinchStartDist = 0;
      touchPanLast = null;
    } else if (ev.touches.length === 1) {
      pinchStartDist = 0;
      touchPanLast = clientToCanvas(ev);
    }
  }, { passive: false });
}

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
  try { initWheelCanvasClick(); } catch (e) {}
});
setTimeout(function() {
  try { initWheelCanvasClick(); } catch (e) {}
}, 400);


/* ——— جمع و ضرب مجموعه‌ها + معادله مجموعه‌ها ——— */
function spApplyPreset(a, b) {
  var elA = document.getElementById('spSetA');
  var elB = document.getElementById('spSetB');
  if (elA) elA.value = a || '';
  if (elB) elB.value = b || '';
  var res = document.getElementById('spResult');
  if (res) { res.style.display = 'none'; res.innerHTML = ''; }
  try { sfxClick(); } catch (e) {}
}
function setCartesian(a, b) {
  var pairs = [], arrA = setToSortedArr(a), arrB = setToSortedArr(b);
  for (var i = 0; i < arrA.length; i++)
    for (var j = 0; j < arrB.length; j++)
      pairs.push('(' + arrA[i] + ', ' + arrB[j] + ')');
  return pairs;
}
function formatCartesian(pairs) {
  if (!pairs.length) return '∅';
  return '{ ' + pairs.join(', ') + ' }';
}
function _spGetAB() {
  return {
    A: parseSetInput(document.getElementById('spSetA') && document.getElementById('spSetA').value),
    B: parseSetInput(document.getElementById('spSetB') && document.getElementById('spSetB').value)
  };
}
function _spShow(html) {
  var res = document.getElementById('spResult');
  if (!res) return;
  res.style.display = 'block';
  res.innerHTML = html;
  try { sfxSuccess(); } catch (e) {}
}
function calcSetSum() {
  var ab = _spGetAB();
  var uni = setUnion(ab.A, ab.B);
  var inter = setInter(ab.A, ab.B);
  var html = '<b style="color:#8b5cf6">＋ جمع = اتحاد (A ∪ B)</b><br><br>';
  html += '<b>A =</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(ab.A) + '</span> · |A|=' + ab.A.size + '<br>';
  html += '<b>B =</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(ab.B) + '</span> · |B|=' + ab.B.size + '<br><br>';
  html += '<div class="set-ans"><b dir="ltr">A ∪ B = ' + formatSet(uni) + '</b></div>';
  html += '|A ∪ B| = ' + uni.size + '<br>';
  html += '<span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">|A ∪ B| = |A|+|B|−|A ∩ B| = ' + ab.A.size + '+' + ab.B.size + '−' + inter.size + ' = ' + uni.size + '</span>';
  _spShow(html);
}
function calcSetProd() {
  var ab = _spGetAB();
  var pairs = setCartesian(ab.A, ab.B);
  var html = '<b style="color:#d946ef">× ضرب = ضرب دکارتی (A × B)</b><br><br>';
  html += '<b>A =</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(ab.A) + '</span> · |A|=' + ab.A.size + '<br>';
  html += '<b>B =</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(ab.B) + '</span> · |B|=' + ab.B.size + '<br><br>';
  html += '<div class="set-ans"><b dir="ltr">A × B = ' + formatCartesian(pairs) + '</b></div>';
  html += '|A × B| = ' + ab.A.size + ' × ' + ab.B.size + ' = <b>' + pairs.length + '</b>';
  _spShow(html);
}
function calcSetBoth() {
  var ab = _spGetAB();
  var uni = setUnion(ab.A, ab.B);
  var pairs = setCartesian(ab.A, ab.B);
  var html = '<b>A =</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(ab.A) + '</span> · |A|=' + ab.A.size + '<br>';
  html += '<b>B =</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(ab.B) + '</span> · |B|=' + ab.B.size + '<br><br>';
  html += '<b style="color:#8b5cf6">＋ اتحاد</b><div class="set-ans"><b dir="ltr">A ∪ B = ' + formatSet(uni) + '</b></div>';
  html += '<b style="color:#d946ef">× دکارتی</b><div class="set-ans"><b dir="ltr">A × B = ' + formatCartesian(pairs) + '</b></div>';
  html += '|A × B| = ' + pairs.length;
  _spShow(html);
}
function seqClear() {
  ['seqEq1','seqEq2','seqU','seqA','seqB','seqC','seqD'].forEach(function(id) {
    var el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.value = '';
  });
  var res = document.getElementById('seqResult');
  if (res) { res.style.display = 'none'; res.innerHTML = ''; }
}
function seqLoad(eq1, eq2) {
  var e1 = document.getElementById('seqEq1');
  var e2 = document.getElementById('seqEq2');
  if (e1) e1.value = eq1 || '';
  if (e2) e2.value = eq2 || '';
  try { sfxClick(); } catch (e) {}
}
(function bindSeqChips() {
  document.addEventListener('click', function(ev) {
    var btn = ev.target && ev.target.closest && ev.target.closest('[data-seq-ins]');
    if (!btn) return;
    var ins = btn.getAttribute('data-seq-ins');
    var active = document.activeElement;
    var target = (active && (active.id === 'seqEq1' || active.id === 'seqEq2')) ? active : document.getElementById('seqEq1');
    if (!target) return;
    var start = target.selectionStart || target.value.length;
    var end = target.selectionEnd || start;
    var v = target.value;
    target.value = v.slice(0, start) + ins + v.slice(end);
    target.focus();
    var pos = start + ins.length;
    try { target.setSelectionRange(pos, pos); } catch (e) {}
  });
})();
function seqGetKnown() {
  var U = parseSetInput(document.getElementById('seqU') && document.getElementById('seqU').value);
  var known = {
    U: U,
    A: parseSetInput(document.getElementById('seqA') && document.getElementById('seqA').value),
    B: parseSetInput(document.getElementById('seqB') && document.getElementById('seqB').value),
    C: parseSetInput(document.getElementById('seqC') && document.getElementById('seqC').value),
    D: parseSetInput(document.getElementById('seqD') && document.getElementById('seqD').value)
  };
  ['A','B','C','D'].forEach(function(k) { known[k].forEach(function(x) { U.add(x); }); });
  known.U = U;
  return known;
}
function seqNormalizeExpr(s) {
  return String(s || '').replace(/′/g, "'").replace(/\\/g, '−').replace(/–|—/g, '−').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim();
}
function seqEvalSide(expr, env) {
  expr = seqNormalizeExpr(expr);

  var tokens = [], i = 0;
  while (i < expr.length) {
    var ch = expr[i];
    if (ch === ' ') { i++; continue; }
    if ('ABCDUXY'.indexOf(ch) >= 0) {
      var name = ch; i++;
      var comp = false;
      if (i < expr.length && (expr[i] === "'" || expr[i] === '′')) { comp = true; i++; }
      tokens.push({ t: 'set', v: name, comp: comp });
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === "'" || ch === '′') {
      if (tokens.length && tokens[tokens.length - 1].t === 'set') tokens[tokens.length - 1].comp = true;
      i++; continue;
    }
    if (ch === '∪' || ch === '∩' || ch === '−' || ch === '-') {
      tokens.push({ t: 'op', v: (ch === '-' ? '−' : ch) }); i++; continue;
    }
    if (ch === '(' || ch === ')') { tokens.push({ t: 'paren', v: ch }); i++; continue; }
    i++;
  }
  var prec = { '∪': 1, '∩': 2, '−': 2 };
  var out = [], ops = [];
  tokens.forEach(function(tok) {
    if (tok.t === 'set') out.push(tok);
    else if (tok.t === 'op') {
      while (ops.length && ops[ops.length - 1].t === 'op' && prec[ops[ops.length - 1].v] >= prec[tok.v]) out.push(ops.pop());
      ops.push(tok);
    } else if (tok.v === '(') ops.push(tok);
    else if (tok.v === ')') {
      while (ops.length && ops[ops.length - 1].v !== '(') out.push(ops.pop());
      if (ops.length && ops[ops.length - 1].v === '(') ops.pop();
    }
  });
  while (ops.length) out.push(ops.pop());
  var stack = [];
  for (var k = 0; k < out.length; k++) {
    var t = out[k];
    if (t.t === 'set') {
      var base = env[t.v] || new Set();
      if (t.comp) base = setCompl(base, env.U || new Set());
      stack.push(new Set(base));
    } else if (t.t === 'op') {
      var b = stack.pop() || new Set();
      var a = stack.pop() || new Set();

      else if (t.v === '∩') stack.push(setInter(a, b));

}
function seqSetsEqual(a, b) {
  if (a.size !== b.size) return false;
  var ok = true;
  a.forEach(function(x) { if (!b.has(x)) ok = false; });
  return ok;
}
function seqSolvePattern(eq, known) {
  var m = seqNormalizeExpr(eq).match(/^(.+?)\s*=\s*(.+)$/);
  if (!m) return null;
  var L = m[1].trim(), R = m[2].trim();
  var A = known.A, B = known.B, C = known.C, U = known.U;
  function tryOne(leftRe, rightName, solver) {
    var re = new RegExp('^' + leftRe + '$');
    if (re.test(L) && R === rightName) return solver();
    if (re.test(R) && L === rightName) return solver();
    return null;
  }
  var r;
  r = tryOne('A\\s*∪\\s*X|X\\s*∪\\s*A', 'B', function() {
    var ok = true; A.forEach(function(x) { if (!B.has(x)) ok = false; });
    if (!ok) return { ok: false, note: 'شرط لازم A ⊆ B برقرار نیست → جواب ندارد.' };
    var must = setDiff(B, A);
    return { ok: true, kind: 'range', low: must, high: new Set(B), particular: must,
      note: 'A ⊆ B برقرار است. جواب کلی: (B − A) ⊆ X ⊆ B\\nجواب خاص: X = B − A' };
  });
  if (r) return r;
  r = tryOne('A\\s*∩\\s*X|X\\s*∩\\s*A', 'C', function() {
    var ok = true; C.forEach(function(x) { if (!A.has(x)) ok = false; });
    if (!ok) return { ok: false, note: 'شرط لازم C ⊆ A برقرار نیست → جواب ندارد.' };
    return { ok: true, kind: 'constraints', forcedIn: new Set(C), forcedOut: setDiff(A, C), particular: new Set(C),
      note: 'C ⊆ A.\\n• اعضای C در X هستند\\n• اعضای A−C خارج از X هستند\\n• بقیه آزادند\\nجواب خاص: X = C' };
  });
  if (r) return r;
  r = tryOne('X\\s*−\\s*A', 'B', function() {
    var ok = true; B.forEach(function(x) { if (A.has(x)) ok = false; });
    if (!ok) return { ok: false, note: 'شرط B ∩ A = ∅ برقرار نیست → جواب ندارد.' };
    return { ok: true, kind: 'range', low: new Set(B), high: setUnion(B, A), particular: new Set(B),
      note: 'B ∩ A = ∅.\\nجواب کلی: B ⊆ X ⊆ (A ∪ B)\\nجواب خاص: X = B' };
  });
  if (r) return r;
  r = tryOne('A\\s*−\\s*X', 'B', function() {
    var ok = true; B.forEach(function(x) { if (!A.has(x)) ok = false; });
    if (!ok) return { ok: false, note: 'شرط B ⊆ A برقرار نیست → جواب ندارد.' };
    var must = setDiff(A, B);
    return { ok: true, kind: 'constraints', forcedIn: must, forcedOut: new Set(B), particular: must,
      note: 'B ⊆ A.\\n• A−B ⊆ X و X ∩ B = ∅\\nجواب خاص: X = A − B' };
  });
  if (r) return r;
  r = tryOne("X'", 'A', function() {
    if (!U.size) return { ok: false, note: 'برای متمم باید U تعریف شود.' };
    return { ok: true, kind: 'unique', particular: setCompl(A, U), note: 'X′ = A ⇒ X = U − A' };
  });
  if (r) return r;
  if (((L === 'A ∪ X' || L === 'X ∪ A') && (R === 'A ∩ X' || R === 'X ∩ A')) ||
      ((R === 'A ∪ X' || R === 'X ∪ A') && (L === 'A ∩ X' || L === 'X ∩ A'))) {
    return { ok: true, kind: 'unique', particular: new Set(A), note: 'A ∪ X = A ∩ X ⇒ X = A' };

}
function seqBruteForce(eqs, known, unknowns) {
  var U = known.U;
  if (!U.size) ['A','B','C','D'].forEach(function(k) { known[k].forEach(function(x) { U.add(x); }); });
  if (!U.size) return { ok: false, note: 'مجموعهٔ مرجع U خالی است.' };
  var elems = setToSortedArr(U);
  var hasY = unknowns.indexOf('Y') >= 0;
  var n = elems.length;
  if (n > 12) return { ok: false, note: 'تعداد اعضای U بیش از ۱۲ است؛ U را کوچک‌تر کنید.' };
  function bitsToSet(mask) {
    var s = new Set();
    for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) if (mask & (1 << i)) s.add(elems[i]);
    return s;
  }
  var solutions = [], total = 1 << n, yTotal = hasY ? total : 1;
  for (var mx = 0; mx < total; mx++) {
    for (var my = 0; my < yTotal; my++) {
      var X = bitsToSet(mx), Y = hasY ? bitsToSet(my) : new Set();
      var env = { U: U, A: known.A, B: known.B, C: known.C, D: known.D, X: X, Y: Y };
      var good = true;
      for (var qi = 0; qi < eqs.length; qi++) {
        var parts = seqNormalizeExpr(eqs[qi]).split('=');
        if (parts.length !== 2) { good = false; break; }
        if (!seqSetsEqual(seqEvalSide(parts[0], env), seqEvalSide(parts[1], env))) { good = false; break; }
      }
      if (good) solutions.push({ X: X, Y: Y });
      if (solutions.length > 200) break;
    }
    if (solutions.length > 200) break;
  }
  if (!solutions.length) return { ok: false, note: 'هیچ جوابی یافت نشد.' };
  var allXin = new Set(solutions[0].X), anyXin = new Set(solutions[0].X);
  var allYin = hasY ? new Set(solutions[0].Y) : new Set();
  var anyYin = hasY ? new Set(solutions[0].Y) : new Set();
  solutions.forEach(function(sol) {
    allXin.forEach(function(x) { if (!sol.X.has(x)) allXin.delete(x); });
    sol.X.forEach(function(x) { anyXin.add(x); });
    if (hasY) {
      allYin.forEach(function(x) { if (!sol.Y.has(x)) allYin.delete(x); });
      sol.Y.forEach(function(x) { anyYin.add(x); });
    }
  });
  return {
    ok: true, kind: 'enum', count: solutions.length, solutions: solutions.slice(0, 30),
    forcedXin: allXin, freeX: setDiff(anyXin, allXin), forcedXOut: setDiff(U, anyXin),
    forcedYin: allYin, freeY: setDiff(anyYin, allYin), forcedYOut: hasY ? setDiff(U, anyYin) : new Set(),
    hasY: hasY, particular: solutions[0]
  };
}
function solveSetEquations() {
  var eq1 = seqNormalizeExpr(document.getElementById('seqEq1') && document.getElementById('seqEq1').value);
  var eq2 = seqNormalizeExpr(document.getElementById('seqEq2') && document.getElementById('seqEq2').value);
  var res = document.getElementById('seqResult');
  if (!res) return;
  if (!eq1) {
    res.style.display = 'block';
    res.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef4444">حداقل معادلهٔ ۱ را وارد کنید.</span>';
    try { sfxError(); } catch (e) {}
    return;
  }
  var eqs = [eq1]; if (eq2) eqs.push(eq2);
  var known = seqGetKnown();
  var text = eqs.join(' ');
  var hasX = /X/.test(text), hasY = /Y/.test(text);
  if (!hasX && !hasY) {
    res.style.display = 'block';
    res.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef4444">مجهولی (X یا Y) در معادله نیست.</span>';
    return;
  }
  var html = '<b style="color:#f43f5e">نتیجهٔ حل معادله مجموعه‌ها</b><br>';
  html += '<span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">معادله‌ها: <span dir="ltr">' + eqs.join(' | ') + '</span></span><br><br>';
  html += '<b>A =</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(known.A) + '</span><br>';
  html += '<b>B =</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(known.B) + '</span><br>';
  if (known.C.size) html += '<b>C =</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(known.C) + '</span><br>';
  if (known.D.size) html += '<b>D =</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(known.D) + '</span><br>';
  html += '<b>U =</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(known.U) + '</span><br><br>';
  if (eqs.length === 1 && hasX && !hasY) {
    var pat = seqSolvePattern(eq1, known);
    if (pat) {
      if (!pat.ok) {
        html += '<span style="color:#ef4444">' + pat.note.replace(/\\n/g, '<br>') + '</span>';
        res.style.display = 'block'; res.innerHTML = html; try { sfxError(); } catch (e) {} return;
      }
      html += '<div class="set-ans">' + pat.note.replace(/\\n/g, '<br>') + '</div>';
      if (pat.particular) html += '<br><b>جواب خاص:</b> <span dir="ltr">X = ' + formatSet(pat.particular) + '</span>';
      if (pat.kind === 'range') {
        html += '<br><b>کران پایین:</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(pat.low) + '</span>';
        html += '<br><b>کران بالا:</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(pat.high) + '</span>';
      }
      if (pat.kind === 'constraints') {
        html += '<br><b>اجباری داخل X:</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(pat.forcedIn) + '</span>';
        html += '<br><b>اجباری خارج X:</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(pat.forcedOut) + '</span>';
      }
      res.style.display = 'block'; res.innerHTML = html; try { sfxSuccess(); } catch (e) {} return;
    }
  }
  var unknowns = []; if (hasX) unknowns.push('X'); if (hasY) unknowns.push('Y');
  var brute = seqBruteForce(eqs, known, unknowns);
  if (!brute.ok) {
    html += '<span style="color:#ef4444">' + brute.note + '</span>';
    res.style.display = 'block'; res.innerHTML = html; try { sfxError(); } catch (e) {} return;
  }
  html += '<b>تعداد جواب‌ها:</b> ' + brute.count + '<br><br>';
  html += '<b>X — اجباری داخل:</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(brute.forcedXin) + '</span><br>';
  html += '<b>X — آزاد:</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(brute.freeX) + '</span><br>';
  html += '<b>X — اجباری خارج:</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(brute.forcedXOut) + '</span><br>';
  if (brute.hasY) {
    html += '<br><b>Y — اجباری داخل:</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(brute.forcedYin) + '</span><br>';
    html += '<b>Y — آزاد:</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(brute.freeY) + '</span><br>';
    html += '<b>Y — اجباری خارج:</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(brute.forcedYOut) + '</span><br>';
  }
  html += '<br><b>یک جواب خاص:</b><div class="set-ans" dir="ltr">X = ' + formatSet(brute.particular.X);
  if (brute.hasY) html += '<br>Y = ' + formatSet(brute.particular.Y);
  html += '</div>';
  if (brute.solutions.length > 1 && brute.solutions.length <= 12) {
    html += '<br><b>همهٔ جواب‌ها:</b><ul style="margin:8px 0 0;padding-right:18px">';
    brute.solutions.forEach(function(sol, idx) {
      html += '<li dir="ltr">#' + (idx+1) + ' X=' + formatSet(sol.X) + (brute.hasY ? ' ; Y=' + formatSet(sol.Y) : '') + '</li>';
    });
    html += '</ul>';
  }
  res.style.display = 'block'; res.innerHTML = html; try { sfxSuccess(); } catch (e) {}
}


function parseSetInput(str) {
  if (!str || !String(str).trim()) return new Set();
  return new Set(String(str).split(/[,،\s]+/).map(function(s) { return s.trim(); }).filter(Boolean));
}

function setToSortedArr(s) {
  return Array.from(s).sort(function(a, b) {
    var na = Number(a), nb = Number(b);
    if (!isNaN(na) && !isNaN(nb) && String(na) === a && String(nb) === b) return na - nb;
    return String(a).localeCompare(String(b), 'fa');
  });
}

function formatSet(s) {
  var arr = setToSortedArr(s);
  if (!arr.length) return '∅';
  return '{ ' + arr.join(', ') + ' }';
}

function getUniverseAndSets() {
  var U = parseSetInput(document.getElementById('setU') && document.getElementById('setU').value);
  var sets = {
    U: U,
    A: parseSetInput(document.getElementById('setA') && document.getElementById('setA').value),
    B: parseSetInput(document.getElementById('setB') && document.getElementById('setB').value),
    C: parseSetInput(document.getElementById('setC') && document.getElementById('setC').value),
    D: parseSetInput(document.getElementById('setD') && document.getElementById('setD').value),
    E: parseSetInput(document.getElementById('setE') && document.getElementById('setE').value),
    F: parseSetInput(document.getElementById('setF') && document.getElementById('setF').value)
  };
  // auto-expand U to include all named set members if U empty or incomplete
  ['A','B','C','D','E','F'].forEach(function(k) {
    sets[k].forEach(function(x) { U.add(x); });
  });
  sets.U = U;
  return sets;
}

function setUnion(a, b) {
  var r = new Set(a);
  b.forEach(function(x) { r.add(x); });
  return r;
}
function setInter(a, b) {
  var r = new Set();
  a.forEach(function(x) { if (b.has(x)) r.add(x); });
  return r;
}
function setDiff(a, b) {
  var r = new Set();
  a.forEach(function(x) { if (!b.has(x)) r.add(x); });
  return r;
}
function setCompl(a, U) {
  var r = new Set();
  U.forEach(function(x) { if (!a.has(x)) r.add(x); });
  return r;
}

function tokenizeSetExpr(expr) {
  var s = String(expr).trim();
  s = s.replace(/⋃/g, '∪').replace(/⋂/g, '∩').replace(/\\/g, '−').replace(/–|—/g, '−').replace(/′/g, "'");
  s = s.replace(/\bc\b/gi, "'");
  var tokens = [];
  var i = 0;
  while (i < s.length) {
    var ch = s[i];
    if (/\s/.test(ch)) { i++; continue; }
    if (/[A-Fa-fU]/.test(ch)) {
      tokens.push({ type: 'set', v: ch.toUpperCase() });
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === "'") { tokens.push({ type: 'comp' }); i++; continue; }
    if (ch === '∪' || ch === '∩' || ch === '−' || ch === '-') {
      tokens.push({ type: 'op', v: ch === '-' ? '−' : ch });
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (ch === '(') { tokens.push({ type: 'lparen' }); i++; continue; }
    if (ch === ')') { tokens.push({ type: 'rparen' }); i++; continue; }
    throw new Error('نماد ناشناخته: ' + ch);
  }
  return tokens;
}

function evalSetExpr(expr, sets) {
  var tokens = tokenizeSetExpr(expr);
  var pos = 0;

  function peek() { return tokens[pos]; }
  function next() { return tokens[pos++]; }

  // Grammar with precedence: complement > intersection > union/diff
  // parseExpr := parseUnionDiff
  // parseUnionDiff := parseInter ( (∪|−) parseInter )*
  // parseInter := parseUnary ( ∩ parseUnary )*
  // parseUnary := parsePrimary ( ' )*
  // parsePrimary := SET | ( expr )

  function parseExpr() {
    return parseUnionDiff();
  }
  function parseUnionDiff() {
    var left = parseInter();
    while (peek() && peek().type === 'op' && (peek().v === '∪' || peek().v === '−')) {
      var op = next().v;
      var right = parseInter();
      left = (op === '∪') ? setUnion(left, right) : setDiff(left, right);
    }
    return left;
  }
  function parseInter() {
    var left = parseUnary();
    while (peek() && peek().type === 'op' && peek().v === '∩') {
      next();
      var right = parseUnary();
      left = setInter(left, right);
    }
    return left;
  }
  function parseUnary() {
    var node = parsePrimary();
    while (peek() && peek().type === 'comp') {
      next();
      node = setCompl(node, sets.U);
    }
    return node;
  }
  function parsePrimary() {
    var t = peek();
    if (!t) throw new Error('عبارت ناقص است');
    if (t.type === 'set') {
      next();
      if (!sets[t.v] && t.v !== 'U') throw new Error('مجموعه ' + t.v + ' تعریف نشده');
      return new Set(sets[t.v] || []);
    }
    if (t.type === 'lparen') {
      next();
      var inner = parseExpr();
      if (!peek() || peek().type !== 'rparen') throw new Error('پرانتز بسته نشده');
      next();
      return inner;
    }
    throw new Error('انتظار مجموعه یا پرانتز');
  }

  var result = parseExpr();
  if (pos < tokens.length) throw new Error('عبارت اضافه در پایان');
  return result;
}

function setsUsedInExpr(expr) {
  var s = String(expr || '');
  var used = [];
  ['A','B','C','D','E','F'].forEach(function(k) {
    if (new RegExp('(?:^|[^A-Za-z])' + k + '(?:[^A-Za-z]|$)').test(s.replace(/'/g, ''))) used.push(k);
  });
  return used;
}

function regionInResult(expr, membership) {
  // membership: {A:bool,B:bool,...} — آیا نقطهٔ آزمایشی داخل آن مجموعه است؟
  var marker = '__x__';
  var synth = { U: new Set([marker]) };
  ['A','B','C','D','E','F'].forEach(function(k) {
    synth[k] = membership[k] ? new Set([marker]) : new Set();
  });
  try {
    var r = evalSetExpr(expr, synth);
    return r.has(marker);
  } catch (e) {
    return false;
  }
}

function drawHatch(ctx, pathFn, color) {
  // clip to path and draw diagonal hatch
  ctx.save();
  ctx.beginPath();
  pathFn(ctx);
  ctx.clip();
  var b = ctx.canvas;
  ctx.strokeStyle = color || 'rgba(37,99,235,0.65)';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  var step = 8;
  for (var x = -b.height; x < b.width + b.height; x += step) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x, 0);
    ctx.lineTo(x + b.height, b.height);
    ctx.stroke();
  }
  // light fill under hatch
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(37,99,235,0.12)';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, b.width, b.height);
  ctx.restore();
}

function drawVennDiagram(expr) {
  var box = document.getElementById('vennBox');
  var canvas = document.getElementById('vennCanvas');
  if (!box || !canvas) return;
  var used = setsUsedInExpr(expr).filter(function(k) { return k === 'A' || k === 'B' || k === 'C'; });
  // اگر D/E/F تنها بودند، همان را به A/B نگاشت نکن — فقط A,B,C پشتیبانی ون
  var allUsed = setsUsedInExpr(expr);
  var extra = allUsed.filter(function(k) { return k !== 'A' && k !== 'B' && k !== 'C'; });
  if (!used.length && !allUsed.length) {
    box.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }
  if (extra.length && !used.length) {
    box.style.display = 'block';
    var ctx0 = canvas.getContext('2d');
    canvas.width = 520; canvas.height = 200;
    ctx0.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
    ctx0.fillStyle = '#64748b';
    ctx0.font = '14px Tahoma';
    ctx0.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx0.fillText('نمودار ون برای مجموعه‌های A، B و C پشتیبانی می‌شود.', canvas.width/2, 100);
    return;
  }
  // حداکثر ۳ مجموعه از A,B,C
  if (used.indexOf('A') < 0 && (used.indexOf('B') >= 0 || used.indexOf('C') >= 0)) {
    // هنوز دایره‌ها را با نام واقعی بکش
  }
  var n = used.length;
  if (n === 0) n = 1;
  if (n > 3) n = 3;

  box.style.display = 'block';
  var W = 520, H = n >= 3 ? 400 : 340;
  canvas.width = W;
  canvas.height = H;
  var ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
  ctx.clearRect(0, 0, W, H);

  // universe rectangle
  var ux = 36, uy = 28, uw = W - 72, uh = H - 70;
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(148,163,184,0.08)';
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#64748b';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.fillRect(ux, uy, uw, uh);
  ctx.strokeRect(ux, uy, uw, uh);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#64748b';
  ctx.font = 'bold 14px Tahoma';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText('U', ux + 8, uy + 18);

  // circle geometry
  var circles = [];
  if (n === 1) {
    var name1 = used[0] || 'A';
    circles.push({ name: name1, cx: W/2, cy: H/2 + 6, r: 110 });
  } else if (n === 2) {
    circles.push({ name: used[0] || 'A', cx: W/2 - 70, cy: H/2 + 6, r: 105 });
    circles.push({ name: used[1] || 'B', cx: W/2 + 70, cy: H/2 + 6, r: 105 });
  } else {
    circles.push({ name: 'A', cx: W/2 - 70, cy: H/2 - 18, r: 100 });
    circles.push({ name: 'B', cx: W/2 + 70, cy: H/2 - 18, r: 100 });
    circles.push({ name: 'C', cx: W/2, cy: H/2 + 70, r: 100 });
    // ensure used names map - always draw A,B,C if 3
    if (used.indexOf('A') < 0 && used[0]) circles[0].name = used[0];
  }

  function inside(c, x, y) {
    var dx = x - c.cx, dy = y - c.cy;
    return dx*dx + dy*dy <= c.r * c.r;
  }

  // pixel-wise membership shading (haчshur) for answer region inside U
  var img = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, W, H);
  var data = img.data;
  var step = 1;
  for (var y = uy; y < uy + uh; y += step) {
    for (var x = ux; x < ux + uw; x += step) {
      var mem = { A: false, B: false, C: false, D: false, E: false, F: false };
      for (var i = 0; i < circles.length; i++) {
        var c = circles[i];
        if (inside(c, x, y)) mem[c.name] = true;
      }
      // فقط نقاط داخل دست‌کم یک دایره یا کل U برای متمم
      var inAny = mem.A || mem.B || mem.C || mem.D || mem.E || mem.F;
      var need = regionInResult(expr, mem);
      if (need) {
        // هاشور: خطوط مورب — فقط بعضی پیکسل‌ها
        var hatch = ((x + y) % 8) < 3;
        var i4 = (y * W + x) * 4;
        if (hatch) {
          data[i4] = 37; data[i4+1] = 99; data[i4+2] = 235; data[i4+3] = 170;
        } else {
          data[i4] = 37; data[i4+1] = 99; data[i4+2] = 235; data[i4+3] = 40;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);

  // redraw U border and circles on top
  ctx.strokeStyle = '#64748b';
  ctx.lineWidth = 2;
  ctx.strokeRect(ux, uy, uw, uh);
  ctx.fillStyle = '#64748b';
  ctx.font = 'bold 14px Tahoma';
  ctx.textAlign = 'left';
  ctx.fillText('U', ux + 8, uy + 18);

  var colors = { A: '#2563eb', B: '#dc2626', C: '#16a34a', D: '#9333ea', E: '#ea580c', F: '#0891b2' };
  circles.forEach(function(c) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.arc(c.cx, c.cy, c.r, 0, Math.PI * 2);
    ctx.strokeStyle = colors[c.name] || '#334155';
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.fillStyle = colors[c.name] || '#334155';
    ctx.font = 'bold 22px Tahoma';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';
    // label position toward outside center
    var lx = c.cx + (c.cx < W/2 ? -c.r * 0.55 : (c.cx > W/2 ? c.r * 0.55 : 0));
    var ly = c.cy + (c.cy > H/2 + 20 ? c.r * 0.55 : -c.r * 0.55);
    if (circles.length === 1) { lx = c.cx; ly = c.cy - c.r + 24; }
    if (circles.length === 2) { lx = c.cx + (c.cx < W/2 ? -50 : 50); ly = c.cy - c.r + 20; }
    if (circles.length === 3) {
      if (c.name === 'A') { lx = c.cx - 55; ly = c.cy - 55; }
      if (c.name === 'B') { lx = c.cx + 55; ly = c.cy - 55; }
      if (c.name === 'C') { lx = c.cx; ly = c.cy + 60; }
    }
    ctx.fillText(c.name, lx, ly);
  });

  // caption
  ctx.fillStyle = '#64748b';
  ctx.font = '12px Tahoma';
  ctx.textAlign = 'center';
  ctx.textBaseline = 'bottom';
  ctx.fillText('ناحیهٔ جواب با هاشور آبی مشخص شده است', W/2, H - 10);
}

function calcSetExpr() {
  var exprInput = document.getElementById('setExpr');
  var out = document.getElementById('setResult');
  var vbox = document.getElementById('vennBox');
  if (!exprInput || !out) return;
  var expr = exprInput.value.trim();
  if (!expr) {
    out.style.display = 'block';
    out.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef4444">عبارت را وارد کنید.</span>';
    if (vbox) vbox.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }
  try {
    var sets = getUniverseAndSets();
    var result = evalSetExpr(expr, sets);
    var lines = [];
    lines.push('<b>عبارت:</b> <span dir="ltr" style="font-family:monospace">' + expr.replace(/</g,'&lt;') + '</span>');
    lines.push('<b>U =</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(sets.U) + '</span>');
    ['A','B','C','D','E','F'].forEach(function(k) {
      if (sets[k].size) lines.push('<b>' + k + ' =</b> <span dir="ltr">' + formatSet(sets[k]) + '</span>');
    });
    lines.push('<div class="set-ans">نتیجه = ' + formatSet(result) + '</div>');
    lines.push('<span style="color:var(--muted);font-size:12px">تعداد اعضا: ' + result.size + '</span>');
    out.style.display = 'block';
    out.innerHTML = lines.join('<br>');
    try { drawVennDiagram(expr); } catch (eV) { if (vbox) vbox.style.display = 'none'; }
  } catch (err) {
    out.style.display = 'block';
    out.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef4444">خطا: ' + (err.message || err) + '</span>';
    if (vbox) vbox.style.display = 'none';
  }
}

function runSetShortcut(expr) {
  var el = document.getElementById('setExpr');
  if (el) el.value = expr;
  calcSetExpr();
}

function initMathSets() {
  var quick = document.getElementById('setQuick');
  if (quick && !quick.dataset.bound) {
    quick.dataset.bound = '1';
    quick.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      var b = e.target.closest('.set-chip');
      if (!b) return;
      var ins = b.dataset.ins;
      var el = document.getElementById('setExpr');
      if (!el) return;
      var start = el.selectionStart || el.value.length;
      var end = el.selectionEnd || el.value.length;
      var v = el.value;
      el.value = v.slice(0, start) + ins + v.slice(end);
      el.focus();
      var pos = start + ins.length;
      try { el.setSelectionRange(pos, pos); } catch (e) {}
    });
  }
  var sc = document.getElementById('setShortcuts');
  if (sc && !sc.dataset.bound) {
    sc.dataset.bound = '1';
    sc.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
      var b = e.target.closest('[data-expr]');
      if (!b) return;
      runSetShortcut(b.dataset.expr);
    });
  }
  var expr = document.getElementById('setExpr');
  if (expr && !expr.dataset.bound) {
    expr.dataset.bound = '1';
    expr.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
      if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); calcSetExpr(); }
    });
  }
}



/* ——— Set builder: A={(x^2)+2|x∈N, x≤34} ——— */

function renderWithCaret(el, value, pos) {
  if (!el) return;
  value = String(value || '');
  if (pos == null || isNaN(pos)) pos = value.length;
  pos = Math.max(0, Math.min(value.length, pos));
  el.dataset.value = value;
  el.dataset.pos = String(pos);
  // نمایش با نشانگر
  var left = value.slice(0, pos);
  var right = value.slice(pos);
  el.innerHTML = '';
  if (!value) {
    // placeholder via CSS :empty won't work with caret span — manual
    var ph = el.getAttribute('data-placeholder');
    if (ph && !value) {
      var span = document.createElement('span');
      span.style.color = 'var(--muted)';
      span.style.opacity = '0.75';
      // show caret at start with empty
    }
  }
  el.appendChild(document.createTextNode(left));
  var caret = document.createElement('span');
  caret.className = 'caret';
  el.appendChild(caret);
  el.appendChild(document.createTextNode(right));
}
function movePos(el, dir) {
  if (!el) return;
  var v = el.dataset.value != null ? el.dataset.value : '';
  var pos = el.dataset.pos != null ? parseInt(el.dataset.pos, 10) : v.length;
  if (isNaN(pos)) pos = v.length;
  if (dir === 'left') pos = Math.max(0, pos - 1);
  else if (dir === 'right') pos = Math.min(v.length, pos + 1);
  else if (dir === 'home') pos = 0;
  else if (dir === 'end') pos = v.length;
  renderWithCaret(el, v, pos);
}

function builderGetValue() {
  var el = document.getElementById('builderExpr');
  if (!el) return '';
  return el.dataset.value != null ? el.dataset.value : '';
}
function builderSetValue(v) {
  var el = document.getElementById('builderExpr');
  if (!el) return;
  v = String(v || '');
  renderWithCaret(el, v, v.length);
}
function builderInsert(str) {
  var el = document.getElementById('builderExpr');
  if (!el) return;
  var v = builderGetValue();
  var pos = el.dataset.pos != null ? parseInt(el.dataset.pos, 10) : v.length;
  if (isNaN(pos) || pos < 0) pos = v.length;
  if (pos > v.length) pos = v.length;
  var nv = v.slice(0, pos) + str + v.slice(pos);
  renderWithCaret(el, nv, pos + str.length);
}
function builderBackspace() {
  // همیشه یک نویسه قبل از مکان‌نما پاک می‌شود (از راستِ مکان‌نما به چپ)
  var el = document.getElementById('builderExpr');
  if (!el) return;
  var v = builderGetValue();
  if (!v) return;
  var pos = el.dataset.pos != null ? parseInt(el.dataset.pos, 10) : v.length;
  if (isNaN(pos)) pos = v.length;
  if (pos <= 0) return; // چیزی سمت چپ مکان‌نما نیست
  var nv = v.slice(0, pos - 1) + v.slice(pos);
  renderWithCaret(el, nv, pos - 1);
}

function calcSetBuilder() {
  var input = document.getElementById('builderExpr');
  var out = document.getElementById('builderResult');
  if (!input || !out) return;
  var raw = builderGetValue().trim();
  if (!raw) {
    out.style.display = 'block';
    out.innerHTML = '<span style="color:#ef4444">عبارت را وارد کنید.</span>';
    return;
  }
  try {
    var parsed = parseSetBuilder(raw);
    var members = expandSetBuilder(parsed);
    var setStr = formatSet(new Set(members.map(String)));
    var lines = [];
    lines.push('<b>عبارت:</b> <span dir="ltr" style="font-family:monospace">' + raw.replace(/</g,'&lt;') + '</span>');
    if (parsed.name) lines.push('<b>نام مجموعه:</b> ' + parsed.name);
